Abstract: Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) have emerged as a promising platform for firefighting operations due to their flexibility, low operational cost, and ability to acquire high-resolution imagery in locations that may be difficult or dangerous to access using conventional methods. Recent advances in deep learning have significantly improved the capabilities of UAV-based wildfire monitoring systems. The present work investigates RGB-infrared fusion for binary wildfire segmentation on the FLAME3 dataset. In this Study, RGB and Infrared baselines are compared with three representative fusion strategies across three segmentation architectures, including U-Net, DeepLabV3+, and SegFormer. The key motivation of this work is to analyze the contribution of each modality, evaluate the impact of fusion timing, and examine how different network architectures exploit multimodal information for UAV wildfire delineation. The findings indicate that thermal information plays a dominant role in UAV segmentation and that feature-level multimodal fusion combined with transformer-based architectures offers the most promising direction for future research.

</details>


### [99] [Scale-based Approach for Active Wildfire Segmentation on Satellite Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01392)
*Matheus F. Kovaleski,Cristiano Premebida,João Ruivo Paulo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用Landsat-8多光谱影像进行活火分割的问题，针对火灾像素稀疏且类别极度不平衡的挑战，提出了一种基于连通分量分析和四分位距准则的数据驱动协议来刻画火区尺寸分布。在多种尺度火情下评估了U-Net、DeepLabV3+和SegFormer三种分割架构及不同SWIR光谱配置，结果表明U-Net鲁棒性最强，SWIR2波段对活火分割最为关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从卫星影像中绘制活火分布具有挑战性，因为火灾像素稀疏且高度不平衡，尤其在早期或低密度火灾观测中。现有研究对多尺度火灾条件下模型鲁棒性的系统评估不足，需要数据驱动的方法来刻画火区尺寸分布，以评估模型在不同局部火区密度下的性能。

Method: 提出一个数据驱动的协议，通过连通分量分析和四分位距准则来刻画火区尺寸分布，据此评估模型在不同局部火区密度下的鲁棒性。在Landsat-8多光谱影像上测试了三种分割架构（U-Net、DeepLabV3+、SegFormer），并结合不同的基于SWIR的光谱配置进行系统比较。

Result: U-Net在所有评估条件下展现出最强鲁棒性，SegFormer表现有竞争力，而DeepLabV3+倾向于产生保守的预测且召回率较低。跨架构比较中，SWIR2波段持续取得最强或接近最优的结果，凸显其在Landsat-8影像活火分割中的重要性。

Conclusion: 光谱波段选择和架构设计对于基于低活火像素密度影像训练的卫星活火测绘模型的鲁棒性至关重要。研究建议在构建此类系统时应优先考虑SWIR2波段以及具有强大鲁棒性的分割架构。

Abstract: Active wildfire mapping from satellite imagery is challenging due to the sparse and highly imbalanced nature of fire pixels, especially in early-stage or low-density fire observations. This work investigates the use of multispectral Landsat-8 imagery for active-fire segmentation under multi-scale wildfire size conditions. We propose a data-driven protocol to characterize fire-region size distributions through connected-component analysis and an interquartile range criterion, enabling the evaluation of model robustness across different local fire-region densities. Three segmentation architectures, U-Net, DeepLabV3+, and SegFormer, are evaluated under different SWIR-based spectral configurations. Results show that U-Net achieves the strongest robustness across the evaluated conditions, SegFormer provides competitive performance, and DeepLabV3+ tends to produce conservative predictions with reduced recall. Across architectures, SWIR2 consistently achieves the strongest or near-best results, highlighting its importance for active-fire segmentation in Landsat-8 imagery. These findings suggest that both spectral band selection and architectural design are critical for robust satellite-based active wildfire mapping trained on low active fire-pixel density images.

</details>


### [100] [MegaStyle++: Scaling Image Style Space through Hierarchical Style Definition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01423)
*Junyao Gao,Sibo Liu,Jiaxing Li,Yanan Sun,Weidong Zhang,Cairong Zhao,Jun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出层级式图像风格定义，从整体风格身份到细粒度视觉属性对风格进行结构化描述，并据此构建包含150K整体风格身份、1M细粒度风格提示和8M风格化图像的大规模数据集MegaStyle++-8M。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像风格是人类高度抽象的概念，受多种视觉因素影响且与内容本质耦合，但缺乏统一、显式的风格定义。作者希望回答"什么是风格"这一根本问题，并提供一个更具结构化、可迁移和可解释的风格表征。

Method: 首先讨论风格的定义问题，提出层级式风格定义框架，从整体风格身份到细粒度视觉属性对风格进行多粒度描述；然后基于此定义改进MegaStyle的标注流程，构建大规模风格数据集MegaStyle++-8M，包含150K个整体风格身份、1M个细粒度风格提示和8M张风格化图像。

Result: 广泛分析表明，所提出的层级式定义在多样性和语义广度上大幅扩展了风格空间，同时能精确捕捉参考图像的内在视觉风格。数据集和代码已公开。

Conclusion: MegaStyle++为研究和建模多样化图像风格提供了可扩展的基础，填补了风格定义缺乏结构化框架和大规模资源之间的空白。

Abstract: Image style is a highly abstract, human-constructed concept shaped by a range of visual factors and intrinsically entangled with content, yet a unified and explicit definition of image style remains lacking. In this work, we first discuss the fundamental question of what is style and then propose a hierarchical style definition that describes image style from an overall style identity to fine-grained visual attributes, providing a more structured, transferable, and interpretable style representation. Based on this definition, we refine the style annotation pipeline of MegaStyle and construct MegaStyle++-8M, a large-scale style dataset containing 150K overall style identities, 1M fine-grained style prompts, and 8M stylized images. Extensive analyses demonstrate that our hierarchical definition substantially expands the style space in both diversity and semantic breadth, while precisely capturing intrinsic visual style of reference images. The dataset and code will be updated at https://github.com/Tencent/MegaStyle, we hope MegaStyle++ provides a scalable foundation for studying and modeling diverse image styles.

</details>


### [101] [Semantic-Guided Multimodal Preprocessing for Vision Transformer-Based Clear Cell Renal Cell Carcinoma Grading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01426)
*Fatemeh Javadian,Zhu Chen,Zahra Aminparast,Johannes Stegmaier*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种语义引导的多模态预处理方法，将现有预训练模型的细胞核分类图谱与RGB组织病理图像整合到ViT架构中进行透明细胞肾细胞癌分级，在平衡准确率上达到0.916，显著优于RGB-only基线（0.707）和先前研究中的最大投票聚合方法（0.427），并证明即使在模拟的核分类器误差下仍保持21个百分点的性能优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 透明细胞肾细胞癌（CCRCC）分级对治疗规划至关重要，但现有方法要么直接分析patch级图像，要么仅关注细胞核级分类，未将核级分析结果与最终肿瘤分级关联起来。缺乏将细粒度核级分析与粗粒度patch级分类桥接的有效手段。

Method: 提出语义引导的多模态预处理方法：(1) 将现有预训练模型的核分类图谱与RGB组织病理图像整合；(2) 采用分类图通道拼接和乘法调制两种融合策略，并优化叠加方式；(3) 在ViT架构上进行CCRCC分级；(4) 通过敏感度分析模拟核分类模型误差，验证方法的鲁棒性。

Result: 语义引导增强方法达到0.916平衡准确率，优于RGB-only基线（0.707）和最大投票聚合（0.427）。敏感度分析显示即使在模拟当前最先进核分类模型的误差率扰动下，仍保持21个百分点的性能优势。各类别召回率一致（0.93, 0.91, 0.91），说明增益未集中于多数类。

Conclusion: 基于预处理的语义引导多模态融合能有效利用现有不完美的核分类器的诊断潜力，弥合了细粒度核级分析与粗粒度ViT patch分类之间的鸿沟。但需要指出，目前敏感度分析的扰动基于ground-truth图谱而非实际核模型预测，真实上游模型的部署评估留待未来工作。

Abstract: Clear cell renal cell carcinoma (CCRCC) grading is essential for treatment planning, yet existing approaches either analyze patch-level images directly or focus solely on nuclei-level classification, without linking to final tumor grading. We propose a semantic-guided multimodal preprocessing method that integrates nuclei classification maps from existing pre-trained models with RGB histopathology images for Vision Transformer (ViT)-based CCRCC grading. Our approach employs classification map channel concatenation and multiplicative modulation, with optimized overlays to leverage nuclei grading information, while preserving RGB textural features. Evaluation of multiple preprocessing strategies demonstrates that semantic-guided enhancement achieves 0.916 balanced accuracy, outperforming RGB-only baseline (0.707) and max-voting aggregation from prior studies (0.427). Sensitivity analysis reveals that this 21 percentage point improvement over baseline persists even under simulated perturbation at rates matching current state-of-the-art nuclei classification model error thresholds, suggesting both effective semantic utilization and practical robustness. These findings show that preprocessing-based multimodal fusion can leverage the diagnostic potential of existing imperfect nuclei classifiers, effectively bridging previously isolated fine-grained nuclear-level analysis with coarse-grained ViT-based patch classification. Per-class recall was consistent across grades (0.93, 0.91, 0.91), indicating that gains are not concentrated in the majority class. Because the sensitivity analysis perturbs ground-truth maps rather than predictions from an actual nuclei model, this result characterizes robustness under simulated error rather than deployment with a real upstream model, which remains for future work.

</details>


### [102] [Pix2Rep-v2: Data-Efficient Representation Learning for Dense Medical Imaging Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01427)
*S. Sifaoui,E. Angelini,S. Toupin,T. Pezel,L. Le Folgoc*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Pix2Rep-v2是一个面向密集医学影像任务的像素级和体素级自监督学习框架，通过像素级冗余缩减目标和等变性原则实现高效扩展至3D或宽视野应用，在少样本下游任务中显著超越全监督基线，并在一次分割任务中取得+9.3 Dice的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集自监督学习虽是无注释条件下学习医学成像局部描述符的强大范式，但仍面临扩展性、效率和下游适配等主要挑战，特别是在3D或大视野场景中。作者旨在构建一个无需注释即可学习像素/体素级表示且高效可扩展的SSL框架，并减少对下游标注数据的需求。

Method: Pix2Rep-v2采用两个核心策略：(1) 在像素级别实现冗余缩减（redundancy reduction）目标，降低表示中特征间的冗余；(2) 引入密集表示的等变性（equivariance）原则，确保表征随输入变换保持一致变换。框架支持2D和3D、多种后端网络和多模态数据。此外提出了一种基于密集原型（dense prototype）的in-context变体，在下游任务中无需额外训练即可进行推理，作为线性探测或全量微调的替代方案。

Result: 在四个数据集上、多种任务/模态/解剖结构/2D/3D后端及多种数据体系下进行系统评估。结果显示Pix2Rep-v2在少样本场景中的数据效率显著高于全监督基线，并与现有最优方法相当；例如在M&Ms-2数据集的一次分割中取得+9.3 Dice点的提升。

Conclusion: Pix2Rep-v2通过冗余缩减与等变性的结合，有效解决了密集SSL的扩展性问题，在少样本医学成像下游任务中展现出高数据效率和与SOTA竞争的性能，且代码和预训练模型已公开供社区使用。

Abstract: Dense self-supervised learning (SSL) is a powerful paradigm for learning without annotations the local descriptors required to solve dense medical imaging tasks. We present Pix2Rep-v2, a framework for SSL of pixel- and voxel-level representations suitable for few-shot downstream applications. Pix2Rep-v2 addresses the main challenges of dense SSL by leveraging a redundancy reduction objective at the pixel-level with a principle of equivariance of dense representations, that scales efficiently to 3D or wide field-of-view applications. We evaluate our method on four datasets, across multiple tasks, multiple modalities and anatomical structures using multiple backbones in 2D and 3D, and under various data regimes. As an alternative to linear probing or full fine-tuning on the downstream task, we also propose an in-context variant, without downstream training, based on a dense prototype approach. Pix2Rep-v2 shows substantially higher data-efficiency in few-shot scenarios compared to fully supervised baselines, and is competitive with the state-of-the-art e.g., +9.3 Dice points in one-shot segmentation on the M&Ms-2 dataset. Our code and pre-trained models are publicly available at https://github.com/BioMedTP/pix2rep-v2.

</details>


### [103] [Gaussian Core LoRA: Distribution-Aware Dynamic Adaptation for Broad Concept Erasure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01433)
*Qinghui Gong,Xunlei Chen,Yu-Xuan Zhang,Hua Meng,Zhengchun Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Gaussian Core LoRA，一种分布感知的低秩适配框架，用于文本到图像扩散模型的概念消除。通过在提示特征空间中拟合高斯混合模型以估计目标概念内的潜在语义原型，并在推理时计算每个输入提示的高斯后验责任来条件化核心生成器，从而产生提示特定的残差重构，实现原型自适应的消除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于适配器的方法（如LoRA）为每个目标概念分配静态的语义消除方向，但复杂概念包含多个潜在语义原型（涉及不同物体、场景或关系），需要不同的局部消除方向。单一的LoRA更新会平均这些异构的消除需求，导致难原型欠消除和附近良性语义的过度编辑。

Method: Gaussian Core LoRA框架：1) 在提示特征空间中拟合高斯混合模型(GMM)以估计目标概念内的潜在语义原型；2) 推理时，将每个输入提示投影到特征空间并计算高斯后验责任；3) 后验责任条件化核心生成器，产生提示特定且范数有界的共享LoRA秩空间的残差重构；4) 使用单一轻量级适配器实现原型自适应消除。

Result: 与各指标上最强基线相比：平均攻击成功率(ASR)降低7.95%，COCO FID降低14.72%，CLIP Score提高4.98%。此外，对对抗性提示具有鲁棒性，可扩展到多身份和多风格消除，并与SDXL和FLUX兼容。

Conclusion: Gaussian Core LoRA通过分布感知的方式解决了概念消除中静态方向假设过于粗糙的问题，以单一轻量级适配器实现了原型自适应的概念消除，在消除效果、图像质量和语义保持方面均优于现有方法，且具有良好的可扩展性和模型兼容性。

Abstract: Concept erasure aims to suppress unsafe, privacy-sensitive, or undesirable generations in text-to-image diffusion models while preserving benign semantics, visual quality, and deployment efficiency. Existing adapter-based methods, such as Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), typically freeze the diffusion backbone and learn lightweight parameter updates to steer generation away from target semantics. However, these methods usually assign a static semantic erasure direction to each target concept. This assumption is overly coarse for broad and complex target concepts, since a concept often contains multiple latent semantic prototypes involving different objects, scenes, or relations, and requires different local erasure directions. A single LoRA update averages these heterogeneous erasure demands, leading to under-erasure on difficult prototypes and over-editing of nearby benign semantics. To address this limitation, we propose Gaussian Core LoRA, a distribution-aware low-rank adaptation framework. It fits a Gaussian mixture model in the prompt feature space to estimate latent semantic prototypes within the target concept. During inference, each input prompt is projected into this feature space to compute its Gaussian posterior responsibilities, which condition the core generator to produce a prompt-specific, norm-bounded residual reconfiguration of the shared LoRA rank space. This enables prototype-adaptive erasure with a single lightweight adapter. Compared with the strongest baseline on each metric, Gaussian Core LoRA reduces average Attack Success Rate (ASR) by 7.95%, lowers COCO Fr'echet Inception Distance (FID) by 14.72%, and improves CLIP Score by 4.98%. Further experiments show robustness to adversarial prompts, scalability to multi-identity and multi-style erasure, and compatibility with SDXL and FLUX.

</details>


### [104] [RadMatch: Auditable Radiology Report Evaluation via Finding-Level Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01470)
*Charles Corbière,Léo Machado,Aubin Charley,Baptiste Callard,Pierre Manceron,Corentin Dancette*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RadMatch是一个基于LLM的多阶段放射学报告评估指标，将报告比较分解为结构化的发现级匹配，进行显著性感知评分，并在七个临床属性维度上进行错误表征。主评分为可解释、可审计的可操作错误数，同时提供分诊、可操作安全召回率/精确率等补充视角。在两个专家基准上，RadMatch是最符合临床的指标，在ReXVal上匹配放射科医生间一致性，在更难的RadEvalExpert上比最佳先前指标提升超过一倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的放射学报告评估指标虽然与放射科医生判断相关性最佳，但输出的是单一不透明的分数，临床医生和模型构建者都难以解释或审计，亟需一种结构化、可解释且可审计的评估方法。

Method: RadMatch采用多阶段LLM提示方法：将报告比较分解为发现级匹配，进行显著性感知评分，错误在七个临床属性维度和可操作错误类型（正确/部分/不正确/遗漏/幻觉）上被表征，并辅以分诊和可操作安全召回率/精确率等互补视角，仅依赖少样本提示。

Result: 在两个专家基准上，RadMatch是最符合临床对齐的指标；在ReXVal上匹配了放射科医生间的判断一致性；在更难的RadEvalExpert基准上，性能是先前的两倍多。

Conclusion: RadMatch为放射学报告评估提供了可解释、可审计且临床对齐的替代方案，可自然扩展到其他模态和解剖部位，并作为开源代码与交互式仪表板发布。

Abstract: As AI systems are increasingly used to draft radiology reports, reliably evaluating their clinical quality remains a critical challenge. Large language model (LLM)-based metrics are now the best-correlated with radiologist judgment, yet they output a single opaque score that neither a clinician nor a model builder can easily interpret or audit. We introduce RadMatch, a multi-stage, LLM-based metric that decomposes report comparison into a structured finding-level matching with significance-aware scoring and error characterization across seven clinical attribute dimensions (status, location, severity, morphology, certainty, longitudinal comparison, and measurement). The main score is the actionable-error count, both interpretable and auditable. Candidate findings are graded correct, partial, or incorrect, and unmatched findings are counted as missed or hallucinated. Triage and actionable safety recall/precision and per-subset views add complementary, deployment-oriented lenses. Across two expert benchmarks, RadMatch is the most clinically aligned metric, matching inter-radiologist agreement on ReXVal and more than doubling the best prior metric on the harder RadEvalExpert. Relying only on few-shot prompting, it is designed to extend to other modalities and anatomies. We will release RadMatch as open-source code with an interactive dashboard for inspecting results.

</details>


### [105] [CameraEditor: Camera-Controlled Image Editing via Video-Prior Sequential Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01479)
*Xin Shen,Chengyou Jia,Keshuo Xing,Zifeng Zhu,Changliang Xia,Bowen Ping,Zhuohang Dang,Hangwei Qian,Minnan Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CameraEditor是一个将相机参数控制的图像编辑任务重构为时序序列预测问题的框架。它利用视频扩散模型的时间一致性，集成几何感知模块和动态参考路由机制，通过动态全景裁剪构建几何严格的视觉参考对，并策略性插入中间过渡帧来分解大幅视角变化，从而在保持源内容身份的同时实现精确的相机控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像编辑模型在语义和风格操作上表现出色，但在显式相机参数控制方面存在不足。当面临大幅视角变换时，指令驱动的模型要么出现结构撕裂，要么产生忽略几何指令的保守输出。基于文本指令的方式具有歧义性，无法精确传达所需的相机几何变化。

Method: CameraEditor将相机控制的图像编辑从空间问题转化为时间序列预测任务。具体方法包括：(1) 利用视频扩散模型的时间一致性；(2) 集成显式几何感知模块与动态参考路由机制；(3) 通过动态全景裁剪构建几何上严谨的视觉参考对，避免基于文本指令的歧义；(4) 策略性插入中间过渡帧以分解大的视角变化，提供保留内容身份和空间一致性的鲁棒时间缓冲。

Result: 构建了包含5,760个实例的训练数据集，并提出CamEditor-Bench评估基准（包含462个测试用例）。大量实验证明CameraEditor在相机控制精度和源身份保持方面达到最先进水平，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 将相机控制编辑重新定义为时间序列预测，利用视频扩散模型的时间一致性和中间帧分解策略，CameraEditor成功解决了大幅视角变化下结构撕裂与几何指令执行不充分的问题，实现了精确的相机控制编辑。

Abstract: Beyond semantic content, camera parameters play a pivotal role in dictating the geometric perspective and appearance of any given image. While recent image editing models excel at semantic and stylistic manipulation, they struggle with explicit camera parameter control. When handling large perspective shifts, instruction-driven models face a dilemma: they either suffer from structural tearing or generate conservative outputs that ignore geometric instructions. To address this, we introduce CameraEditor, a framework that reformulates camera-controlled editing from a spatial problem into a temporal sequence prediction task. By leveraging the temporal coherence of video diffusion models, our approach integrates an explicit geometric perception module with a dynamic reference routing mechanism. This allows us to construct geometrically rigorous visual reference pairs via dynamic panorama cropping, overcoming the ambiguity of text-based instructions. Furthermore, CameraEditor strategically inserts intermediate transition frames to decompose large perspective shifts, providing a robust temporal buffer that preserves content identity and spatial coherence. We construct a training dataset of 5,760 instances. As an independent contribution, we introduce CamEditor-Bench, a model-agnostic evaluation suite of 462 test cases. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CameraEditor achieves state-of-the-art camera control precision and source identity preservation, outperforming existing methods.

</details>


### [106] [Benchmarking Spatial, Spectral, and Self-Supervised Cues for Face Forgery Detection under Realistic Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01511)
*Lucas Cunha,Lucas Sotomaior,Lucas Gasperin,Beatriz Caldas,Eduardo Pianovski,Rayson Laroca*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于MFFI数据集的标准人脸伪造检测基准，在干净和退化（压缩、缩放、模糊）测试分区上评估了六种模型家族（CNN、Transformer、冻结DINOv3自监督骨干），分别使用空间、频谱和混合输入表征。结果显示干净集性能不能可靠预测鲁棒性：Xception（RGB）在干净集上最佳（0.884 mean ROC-AUC）但退化时大幅下降；冻结DINOv3在退化集上最强（0.726 mean ROC-AUC），且仅需训练线性分类头。频谱线索需与RGB结合才有效，纯频谱输入该于空间表征。该工作强调了退化评估协议的必要性，并指出自监督视觉表征是鲁棒伪造检测的有前景方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度伪造检测器在受控基准上表现优异，但在真实图像退化（如压缩、缩放、模糊）条件下可靠性有限。目前缺乏一个标准的、覆盖多种退化场景的评测基准来系统比较不同模型家族和输入表征的鲁棒性，导致领域内对什么是真正鲁棒的方法缺乏清晰认识。

Method: 使用Multi-Dimensional Face Forgery Image (MFFI)数据集构建标准化基准，包含干净和退化两种测试分区（模拟压缩、缩放和模糊）。系统比较六种模型家族（卷积网络、Transformer模型、冻结的自监督DINOv3骨干），每种模型在空间（RGB）、频谱（傅里叶域）及混合输入表征下进行评估，以mean ROC-AUC为主要指标，并辅以归因图进行定性分析。

Result: 干净集与退化集性能相关性弱。Xception+RGB获得最佳干净集性能（0.884 mean ROC-AUC），但在困难退化解集上显著退化。冻结DINOv3（仅训练线性头）在退化集上达到最强的0.726 mean ROC-AUC。表征分析表明傅里叶域线索须与RGB结合才有效，纯频谱输入显著劣于空间表征。归因图显示CNN关注局部伪影，而DINOv3依赖更广泛的整体面部结构。

Conclusion: 研究结论凸显了采用退化评估协议的必要性；同时证明冻结的自监督视觉表征（DINOv3）是构建鲁棒人脸伪造检测器的有前景方向，即使仅训练简单分类头也能取得最稳健的表现。源代码已公开。

Abstract: Face forgery detectors often achieve strong results on controlled benchmarks, but their reliability under realistic image degradations remains limited. This paper presents a standardized benchmark for face forgery detection using the Multi-Dimensional Face Forgery Image (MFFI) dataset and evaluates performance on both clean and degraded test partitions. We compare six model families, including convolutional networks, transformer-based models, and a frozen self-supervised DINOv3 backbone, across spatial, spectral, and hybrid input representations. The results show that clean-set performance is not a reliable indicator of robustness under compression, resizing, and blurring. Xception with RGB obtains the best clean performance, reaching 0.884 mean ROC-AUC, but degrades substantially on the harder partition. In contrast, frozen DINOv3 achieves the strongest degraded-set result, with 0.726 mean ROC-AUC, while training only a linear classification head. The representation analysis indicates that Fourier-domain cues are most useful when combined with RGB information, whereas purely spectral inputs consistently underperform spatial representations. Qualitative attribution maps further suggest that convolutional detectors focus on localized artifacts, while DINOv3 relies on broader facial structure. These findings reinforce the need for degraded evaluation protocols and highlight self-supervised visual representations as a promising direction for robust face forgery detection. Our source code is publicly available at https://github.com/lucasdocunha/FaceForgery-Benchmark/.

</details>


### [107] [TempCloze: Can Video-LLMs Identify the Missing Middle?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01515)
*Wenqi Pei,Henry Hengyuan Zhao,Yilai Liu,Jiahao Meng,Han Chen,Ziyu Wang,Hongyang Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TempCloze基准，一个视频完形填空基准，通过要求模型从四个候选中识别视频缺失的中间片段，评估Video-LLMs的视觉时间推理能力，减少语言捷径干扰。评估了31个Video-LLMs，发现时间对齐是最主要的瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间推理基准常被语言中介干扰，模型可利用选项措辞、答案相关性或语言先验取巧，无法真正检验视觉时间推理能力。

Method: 构建TempCloze基准：给定视频开头和结尾片段，模型须从四个候选中选出真实缺失的中间片段。包含1,521个精心筛选的视频（来自七个来源，主要为长镜头和第一人称视角视频）。干扰项沿三个维度构造：Semantic（事件语义内容）、Alignment（事件时间对齐）和Progression（事件展开过程），共享场景和物体以减少外观线索。评估10个专有和21个开源Video-LLMs，并进行误差模式和行为敏感性分析。

Result: 评估发现Alignment（时间对齐）是Video-LLMs的主要瓶颈：模型常能识别合理的语义内容和局部事件推进，但在时间对齐方面表现困难。对TempCloze-Mixed和TempCloze-Hard的进一步分析考察了候选顺序、上下文方向、可见跨度、帧密度和测试时扩展等变量对模型选择的影响。

Conclusion: TempCloze揭示了现有Video-LLMs时间推理能力的真实短板，特别是时间对齐维度上的不足，为未来改进视频时间理解提供了诊断性基准和洞察。

Abstract: Temporal reasoning benchmarks for Video-LLMs are often mediated by language, leaving room for linguistic shortcuts from option wording, answer correlations, or language priors. To reduce such shortcuts, we introduce TempCloze, a video cloze benchmark for evaluating visual temporal reasoning in Video-LLMs. Given the beginning and ending clips of a video, models must identify the true missing middle from four candidates. TempCloze contains 1,521 carefully filtered videos from seven sources, mainly long-take and egocentric videos. We construct same-source distractors along three dimensions: Semantic asks what event should happen, Alignment probes when it should occur, and Progression tests how it should unfold, while shared scenes and objects reduce appearance cues. Our evaluation of 10 proprietary and 21 open-source Video-LLMs reveals Alignment as the primary bottleneck: models often recognize plausible semantic content and local event progression but struggle with temporal alignment. We further conduct error pattern and behavioral sensitivity analyses on TempCloze-Mixed and TempCloze-Hard with four representative models to examine where errors arise and how candidate order, context direction, visible span, frame density, and test-time scaling influence model choices.

</details>


### [108] [DualDiff3D: Dual Structure-Appearance Diffusion Priors for Reliability-Enhanced 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01516)
*Qian Wang,Yu Wang,Weiqi Li,Xinhua Cheng,Xiandong Meng,Ronggang Wang,Jian Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DualDiff框架，利用双扩散先验和结构-外观注意力（SAA）模块，分别从低质量新视角和参考视角中提取结构信息与保证外观一致性，以改进由不完善3D表示渲染出的低质量新视角；并在此基础上提出DualDiff3D重建框架，通过可靠性增强的Render-Refine-Optimize（RRO）循环逐步优化3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）表示。实验表明该方法在仅推理设置下即优于现有最先进方法，训练后性能进一步提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS在有限输入视角场景下重建质量差、新视角渲染存在伪影。现有利用扩散先验的方法通常将渲染视图与参考视图沿额外维度拼接送入单一网络，忽视了不同视图之间"外观相似但结构因视角偏移而异"的本质特性，导致属性冲突产生模糊。

Method: 提出DualDiff管线：保留一个扩散分支专注从低质量新视角提取结构信息，引入另一分支保证与参考视图的外观一致性，并通过结构-外观注意力（SAA）模块实现双分支交互。同时构建DualDiff3D框架，集成可靠性增强的Render-Refine-Optimize（RRO）循环，逐步且稳健地将精炼后的新视角纳入3DGS优化。

Result: 大量实验表明，所提方法在仅推理设置下即超越最先进方法，通过训练可进一步提升性能。代码和预训练权重已公开。

Conclusion: 通过解耦结构提取与外观一致性两个任务而非在单一网络中拼接处理，能有效避免视图间属性冲突导致的模糊，显著提升有限视角下3DGS的重建质量与新视角合成效果。

Abstract: While 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has revolutionized 3D reconstruction and novel-view synthesis, scenarios with limited input views often lead to poor reconstruction quality and artifacts in rendered novel views. Recent efforts attempt to utilize powerful diffusion priors, yet they typically process rendered and reference views concatenated along an additional dimension in a single network. These methods overlook an inherent nature that different views should maintain appearance similarity but differ in structure due to view shifts, leading to blur caused by conflicts between the two properties. In this paper, we propose DualDiff, a novel pipeline that leverages dual diffusion priors with a Structure-Appearance Attention (SAA) module to introduce reference guidance for refining low-quality novel views rendered from flawed 3D representations. Specifically, we retain one diffusion branch to focus on extracting structural information from the low-quality novel views, while introducing another branch to ensure appearance consistency with reference views. Furthermore, we present a 3D reconstruction framework named DualDiff3D, which integrates a reliability-enhanced Render-Refine-Optimize (RRO) loop to progressively and robustly incorporate the refined novel views, yielding more accurate 3DGS. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach outperforms state-of-the-art methods even in the inference-only setting, with further performance gains achievable through training. Our code and pre-trained weights are available at https://github.com/Akaneqwq/DualDiff3D.

</details>


### [109] [Revisiting Cross-View Completion: Self-Supervised Pre-Training via Reconstruction Error Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01530)
*Thibaut Loiseau,Guillaume Bourmaud,Vincent Lepetit*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Gekko是一种新型自监督3D视觉预训练网络，在跨视角补全和掩码自编码的基础上，额外预测"相对重建误差改进量"作为共可见性的自监督代理信号，从而为所有被掩码区域提供双目线索。在零样本对应估计、相对位姿估计和点图回归等任务上一致优于CroCo，最高在严格相对位姿阈值下准确率提升6倍，ETH3D端点误差降低22%。额外学习到的通道本身即是强共可见性检测器，且支持直接从原始视频训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于跨视角补全的自监督预训练（如CroCo）仅利用图像对的共可见区域学习3D特征，但在非共可见区域中，参考视角无法提供有效信息，隐式退化为单目训练信号，浪费了双目信息的潜力。

Method: Gekko的核心洞察是：跨视角补全误差相对于掩码自编码误差的相对改善量可作为共可见性的自监督代理——改善大表示共可见区域，改善可忽略则表示非共可见区域。Gekko网络同时执行三项任务：(1) 跨视角补全；(2) 掩码自编码；(3) 逐像素预测这一相对改善量，为所有掩码区域提供额外的双目信号。整个网络从零开始训练，无需任何3D标注，且可通过简单的步幅课程直接从原始视频训练。

Result: 在相同架构和训练数据下，Gekko在零样本对应估计、相对位姿估计和点图回归上一致优于CroCo；在最严格的相对位姿阈值上准确率最高提升6倍，ETH3D上的端点误差降低22%。其学习的额外通道在未见场景上即构成强共可见性检测器，冻结特征优于同规模或更大规模的已发布跨视角骨干网络。

Conclusion: Gekko成功地将跨视角补全的"局限性"（非共可见区域缺乏参考信息）转化为可用的监督信号，证明了"相对改善量"作为共可见性代理的有效性，并提供了一种无需3D预处理、可直接从原始视频训练的自监督3D视觉预训练新范式。

Abstract: Self-supervised pre-training via cross-view completion learns strong features for 3D vision from co-visible regions of image pairs. However, the reference view provides little information for reconstructing non-co-visible patches, implicitly yielding a monocular training signal in these regions. We introduce Gekko, which turns this limitation into a useful signal. The relative improvement of the cross-view reconstruction error over a masked-autoencoder error is a self-supervised proxy for co-visibility: large improvements indicate co-visible regions, negligible ones non-co-visible areas. Gekko is a network, trained from scratch, that jointly performs cross-view completion, masked autoencoding, and per-pixel prediction of this relative improvement, providing an additional binocular signal for all masked regions without any ground-truth 3D annotation. Under identical architectures and training data, Gekko consistently outperforms CroCo on zero-shot correspondence estimation, relative pose estimation, and pointmap regression, with up to 6 times higher accuracy at the strictest relative-pose threshold and a 22% drop in end-point error on ETH3D. The extra channel it learns is itself a strong co-visibility detector on unseen scenes, and Gekko's frozen features outperform released cross-view backbones of comparable or larger size. It can also be trained directly from raw videos with a simple stride-based curriculum, removing the cumbersome 3D preprocessing prior methods require while matching models trained on curated data. Code and pre-trained models are publicly available.

</details>


### [110] [What, Where, and How: Probing Spatiotemporal Representations in Video Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01551)
*Sharon S. Musa,Fereshteh Forghani,Harrish Thasarathan,Sonia Joseph,Matthew Kowal,Konstantinos G. Derpanis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对V-JEPA 2和VideoMAE-v2两个自监督视频基础模型进行了系统的逐层分析，探究其编码的视觉概念、概念在Transformer层中的位置以及空间的几何组织方式。研究发现两种模型均能编码相机运动信息（最优性能出现在网络60-70%深度），异常检测表现中等，但直观物理推理能力接近随机水平。此外，时间特征形成平滑的低维轨迹，表明相机运动不仅可线性解码还具有几何组织性。基于此，作者提出基于几何感知样条的潜在表征引导方法，其插值视频比线性插值具有更平滑的轨迹和更连贯的时间演进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管自监督视频基础模型能学习丰富的时空表征，但其内部到底编码了哪些视觉概念、这些概念出现在Transformer的哪些层以及它们在表示空间中如何几何组织，这些问题仍不清楚。理解这些内部机制对于解释模型行为、指导模型改进和实际应用至关重要。

Method: 对V-JEPA 2和VideoMAE-v2进行逐层分析，使用轻量级探针发现三类时间接地属性：（i）相机运动理解、（ii）直观物理、（iii）异常检测。在分类任务之外，还分析单视频时间特征在表示空间中形成的低维轨迹结构，并基于几何分析提出基于样条的潜在空间引导插值方法以操纵相机运动。

Result: 两个模型均在网络60-70%深度处展现出最优的相机运动理解性能（ROC AUC > 90），异常检测达到中等水平（ROC AUC > 60），但在直观物理任务上接近随机水平。时间特征形成平滑的低维轨迹，样条引导的插值相比线性插值产生更平滑的轨迹和更连贯的时间进展。

Conclusion: 自监督视频基础模型的核心时空理解能力（如相机运动）在其深层表征中已得到良好编码且呈几何有组织的方式，但直观物理等更深层的推理仍未被有效编码。研究还展示了对表征几何结构理解的价值——利用样条引导可在潜在空间中实现更自然的相机运动插值，为表征编辑和视频生成提供了新途径。

Abstract: Self-supervised video foundation models learn rich spatiotemporal representations, yet it remains unclear what visual concepts these representations encode, where they emerge across transformer layers, and how they are geometrically organized. In this work, we tackle these three questions through a systematic layer-wise analysis of V-JEPA 2 and VideoMAE-v2. We leverage lightweight probes trained to discover three temporally grounded properties: (i) camera motion understanding, (ii) intuitive physics, and (iii) anomaly detection. Both models encode camera motion, with best results ($>90$ ROC AUC) emerging at 60-70% of network depth, and achieve moderate anomaly detection performance ($>60$ ROC AUC), but remain near chance on intuitive-physics tasks, suggesting a limited encoding of deeper physical reasoning. Beyond classification, we find that temporal features from individual videos form smooth low-dimensional trajectories in representation space, suggesting that camera motion is not only linearly decodable but also geometrically organized. Based on these results, we apply geometry-aware spline-based steering in the model's latent representations to interpolate camera motion, yielding steered videos with smoother trajectories and more coherent temporal progression than linear interpolation.

</details>


### [111] [BS: Take the Hint - Interactive Multitracer PET/CT Lesion Segmentation with a Scribble-Conditioned ResEnc U-Net](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01554)
*Marven Sherif,Amgad Elmasry,Youssef Ghazal,Ayman Elghotni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于涂抹（scribble）条件化的残差编码器U-Net用于全身PET/CT交互式病灶分割。网络在autoPET-III获胜权重基础上从两输入通道扩展为四输入通道（CT、PET、前景涂抹图和背景涂抹图），涂抹通道零初始化以完全保留预训练表示。PET强度以CT分割得到的主动脉血池参考进行归一化，消除示踪剂与中心差异。五折模型通过软max平均集成，无涂抹时平均Dice为0.554和病变级F1为0.528，经过五轮修正后提升至0.751和0.733，约85%的增益来自首次涂抹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全身PET/CT的自动化病灶分割因生理性示踪剂摄取模式的多样性和不同示踪剂间病灶外观差异而十分困难。autoPET/CT V挑战通过引入用户涂抹交互来解决此问题，但如何在充分利用预训练模型的基础上高效整合交互信息仍是一个待解决的关键问题。

Method: 提出涂抹条件化的残差编码器U-Net，输入为四个通道：CT、PET、前景和背景的稀疏涂抹图。网络从autoPET-III获胜权重初始化，通道数从二扩展至四，涂抹通道零初始化以精确保持预训练表示。每折模型从对应autoPET-III折检查点微调，确保预训练中未见验证病例。PET强度使用由CT分割得出的主动脉血池参考进行逐扫描归一化。推理时五折模型在滑动窗口块级对softmax输出取平均，再进行高斯加权拼接融合。

Result: 在挑战的五折划分上，每折在其自身验证集上评估，无涂抹时平均Dice为0.554、病变级F1为0.528；经过五轮修正后提升至0.751和0.733。约85%的性能提升来自首次涂抹，且折叠模型间的差异在修正中收窄五倍。

Conclusion: 交互式涂抹能够大幅显著提升病灶分割性能，且大部分增益在首次交互后即可获得。交互过程不仅提升了整体准确率，还缩小了不同模型间（不同折）的性能差距，说明用户交互在很大程度上弥补了模型在没有辅助时的分割能力差异。预训练权重的合理扩展和主动脉血池归一化策略被证实有效。

Abstract: Automated lesion segmentation in whole-body PET/CT is complicated by the variety of physiological tracer uptake patterns and by the differing appearance of lesions across tracers. The autoPET/CT V challenge addresses this by making segmentation interactive: user scribbles marking foreground and background are supplied alongside the image, and the algorithm is expected to exploit them. We present our submission, a scribble-conditioned residual encoder U-Net operating on four input channels: CT, PET, and a sparse scribble map for each of foreground and background. The network is initialised from the autoPET-III winning weights and extended from two to four input channels, with the two scribble channels zero-initialised so that the pretrained representation is preserved exactly at initialisation. Every model is fine-tuned per fold from the corresponding autoPET-III fold checkpoint, so that no validation case is seen during pretraining. PET intensities are normalised against a per-scan aorta blood-pool reference derived from a CT segmentation, which removes tracer- and centre-specific scaling without requiring lesion labels. At inference the five fold models are ensembled by averaging their softmax outputs per sliding-window patch, before Gaussian-weighted stitching. On the challenge's five-fold split, with each fold evaluated on its own validation cases, mean Dice is 0.554 and mean lesion-level F1 is 0.528 without scribbles, rising to 0.751 and 0.733 after five correction rounds. About 85% of that gain follows the first scribble, and the spread between fold models narrows five-fold over the same rounds, so interaction largely compensates for how well or badly a given model segments unaided.

</details>


### [112] [H3-World: Turning Language Understanding into World Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01560)
*Danze Chen,Zeqing Wang,Ziyue Lin,Xingyi Yang,Yeying Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: H3-World 是一个高效框架，将33B参数的MiniMax-H3视频生成器转变为交互式世界模型。它利用大型视频生成器中涌现的语言控制能力，通过结构化指令表示和时间注意力路由实现精确的、时间对齐的角色与镜头控制，仅需8,000个游戏样本和0.199%的可训练参数即可达到有效控制并保持生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型视频生成器能力的增强，语言正自然涌现为控制接口（如MiniMax-H3已支持零样本的自然语言控制），但这类控制是粗略的、缺乏时间层面的精确对齐。作者旨在将这种粗粒度的语言接口转化为精确的、时间可感知的世界控制，而不引入专门的动作模块或破坏预训练学到的语义表示。

Method: (1) 将每个动作表示为角色指令和镜头指令的结构化组合，并与对应的时序视频潜变量对齐；(2) 提出时间注意力路由（temporal attention routing）机制，将每条指令限制在其预设的时间区间内，减少动作间控制泄漏；(3) 直接复用视频预训练中学到的语义表示，仅需轻量适配（LoRA），不引入额外的动作模块。

Result: 仅用8,000个游戏样本、10,000步LoRA优化和0.199%可训练参数，H3-World实现了有效的角色控制和镜头控制，同时保持了良好的生成质量，并能泛化到未见过的场景。

Conclusion: 大型视频生成器中涌现的控制能力可以被高效地转化为交互式的世界控制，验证了"利用语言作为世界模型控制接口"这一范式的可行性，无需训练专门的动作模块或大规模微调。

Abstract: We present H3-World, an efficient framework that turns the 33B MiniMax-H3 video generator into an interactive world model. Our key finding is that, as large video generators become more capable, language is emerging as a natural interface for control. MiniMax-H3, for example, already supports zero-shot control of character behavior and camera motion through natural-language instructions. Building on this, H3-World turns this coarse language interface into precise, temporally grounded world control, without introducing dedicated action modules. Specifically, we represent each action as a structured combination of character and camera instructions, and align them with the corresponding temporal video latents. To make the control temporally precise, we further introduce temporal attention routing, which restricts each instruction to its intended time interval and reduces control leakage across actions. Importantly, H3-World directly reuses the semantic representations learned during large-scale video pretraining and requires only lightweight adaptation. With only 8,000 gameplay samples, 10,000 LoRA optimization steps, and 0.199% trainable parameters, H3-World achieves effective character and camera control while preserving strong generation quality. It also generalizes to unseen scenarios. These results show that the control capabilities emerging in large video generators can be efficiently transformed into interactive world control.

</details>


### [113] [SpatialGuard: Harness-Guided Verifiable Spatial Reasoning for Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01582)
*Ziyun Qian,Zizhi Chen,Yizhou Liu,Mingyang Sun,Dingkang Yang,Lihua Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpatialGuard是一个面向复杂3D空间文生图的框架，通过结构化布局引导将自然语言提示解析为3D布局，再转化为视觉条件与候选图像，并引入视觉对齐评判器与布局约束器实现"规划-实现-验证-修复"的可验证流程，在空间保真度上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示驱动或布局条件方法虽提升了可控性，但缺乏视觉采样前可优化、可验证的空间中间表示，导致多轮生成中物体关系、遮挡、可见性与相机约束逐渐退化。

Method: 提出三层架构：(1) Spatial Layout Architect将提示解析为面向图像合成的3D布局；(2) Visual Realizer将布局实现为视觉条件与候选图像；(3) Visual Alignment Critic验证提示-布局-图像间的一致性。此外引入Layout Harness，围绕可编辑布局状态组织规则约束、工具调用、共享知识与反馈循环。

Result: 综合实验表明SpatialGuard在复杂3D空间布局生成上达到SOTA性能，并在空间保真度上优于现有文生图与布局控制基线。

Conclusion: 该工作将复杂空间生成从隐式提示跟随转变为规划、实现、验证与修复的可验证过程，为空间可控文生图提供了结构化方法论支撑。

Abstract: Complex 3D spatial text to image generation requires models to convert natural language into stable visual geometry, not merely semantic appearance. Existing prompt-driven or layout-conditioned methods improve controllability, but often lack an optimizable and verifiable spatial intermediary before visual sampling. As a result, object relations, occlusion, visibility, and camera constraints can decay during multi-round generation. This paper presents SpatialGuard, a structured layout-guided framework for complex 3D spatial text-to-image generation. SpatialGuard parses prompts into image synthesis-oriented 3D layouts through a Spatial Layout Architect, realizes them as visual conditions and candidate images through a Visual Realizer, and uses a Visual Alignment Critic to validate consistency among prompt, layout, and image. To keep constraints stable across iterations, SpatialGuard introduces a Layout Harness that organizes rule constraints, tool invocation, shared knowledge, and feedback loops around the editable layout state. This design turns complex spatial generation from implicit prompt following into a verifiable process of planning, realization, validation, and repair. Comprehensive experiments show that SpatialGuard achieves state-of-the-art performance in complex 3D spatial layout generation and improves spatial faithfulness over existing text-to-image and layout control baselines.

</details>


### [114] [A Benchmark for Vehicle Attribute Classification in Cross-Domain Surveillance Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01584)
*Sergio M. Silva,Otavio T. Remer,Gabriel E. Lima,Lucas Wojcik,Rayson Laroca,David Menotti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了UVIB基准，一个用于评估智能交通系统中车辆属性分析跨域泛化能力的benchmark，包含84,835张来自七个巴西公开数据集的车辆图像，统一标注了朝向、VMMR遮挡适切性和颜色清晰度三类二值属性，并通过混合域、跨域和跨数据集三种协议评估四种代表性架构，发现域偏移对模型性能的影响大于架构选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有车辆属性分析模型在受控条件下表现良好，但在真实监控场景中因视角变化、遮挡、光照和传感器特性差异导致性能显著下降。缺少能够显式衡量跨域鲁棒性的benchmark和评估协议，限制了该领域在ITS实际部署中的可靠性。

Method: 构建UVIB基准：从七个巴西公开数据集汇总84,835张图像，划分为监控域和一般采集域，统一标注三类二值属性（前/后朝向、VMMR遮挡适切性、颜色清晰度）。在混合域、跨域、跨数据集三种协议下评估EfficientNetV2-S、ResNet-50、ViT/B-16和YOLO11s-cls四种代表性架构。

Result: 实验表明域偏移比架构选择对性能影响更大，跨域设置下性能显著下降，尤其对VMMR适切性和颜色清晰度任务影响明显。朝向识别泛化较可靠；VMMR适切性受类别不平衡和模糊遮挡影响；颜色清晰度对光照和传感器模态高度敏感。

Conclusion: 研究发现强调了建立能显式衡量超越标准域内准确率的操作鲁棒性benchmark和评估协议的必要性。UVIB基准已公开，为该领域提供了统一的跨域评估基础设施。

Abstract: Vehicle attribute analysis is a key component of Intelligent Transportation Systems (ITS), supporting applications such as vehicle identification, traffic monitoring, and forensic investigation. However, models trained under controlled conditions often degrade in real surveillance scenarios due to changes in viewpoint, occlusion, illumination, and sensor characteristics. This paper introduces Unconstrained Vehicle Identification Benchmark (UVIB), a benchmark for evaluating three operational vehicle-analysis tasks: front/rear orientation, occlusion-related suitability for Vehicle Make and Model Recognition (VMMR), and color clarity. The benchmark contains 84,835 vehicle images from seven public Brazilian datasets, grouped into surveillance and general acquisition domains, with unified binary annotations that were not jointly available in the original sources. Four representative architectures, EfficientNetV2-S, ResNet-50, ViT/B-16, and YOLO11s-cls, are evaluated under mixed-domain, cross-domain, and cross-dataset protocols. The results show that domain shift has a stronger impact than architecture choice, with substantial degradation in cross-domain settings, especially for VMMR suitability and color clarity. While orientation generalizes more reliably, VMMR suitability remains affected by class imbalance and ambiguous occlusions, and color clarity is highly sensitive to illumination and sensor modality. These findings highlight the need for benchmarks and evaluation protocols that explicitly measure operational robustness beyond standard in-domain accuracy. The proposed benchmark is publicly available at https://github.com/UFPR-IPASP-PR/uvib-vehicle-attributes/.

</details>


### [115] [UI-VISA: U-Net Initialized Vascular Image Segmentation Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01598)
*Asees Kaur,Suzanne S. Sindi,Erica M. Rutter*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出UI-VISA混合分割架构，利用U-Net预测作为种子点，结合CNN引导的区域生长算法，在DSA血管图像上显著提升连通性指标clDice。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DSA血管分割中，像素级深度学习方法（如U-Net）在细血管区域常产生断裂或不连续预测，未显式强化结构连通性；而区域生长算法虽保持拓扑连续性，但对种子点初始化敏感且计算开销大。二者互补优势尚未被充分利用。

Method: 提出UI-VISA混合流水线：先用U-Net生成前景预测作为信息化的种子点，再交由CNN引导的区域生长算法迭代细化分割，通过强制局部连通性并恢复U-Net遗漏或过度预测的细小血管细节。

Result: 在26张DSA图像上使用5折交叉验证，UI-VISA在平均Dice和clDice上均取得最高分；配对Wilcoxon符号秩检验显示clDice改善具有统计学显著性（p=0.023），而Dice提升未达显著水平（p=0.104）。

Conclusion: UI-VISA有效结合了深度学习和传统区域生长的互补优势，其设计目标——保持血管连通性——在统计检验中得到验证，证明该方法在细长血管分割任务中优于单独使用U-Net及先前基于区域生长的方法VISA。

Abstract: Accurate segmentation of vascular structures in digital subtraction angiography (DSA) images remains challenging due to the thin, elongated, and branching nature of blood vessels. Pixel-wise deep learning approaches such as U-Net achieve strong general-purpose segmentation performance but often produce fragmented or discontinuous predictions in fine vascular regions, since they do not explicitly enforce structural connectivity. Region growing algorithms preserve spatial context and topological continuity, but are highly sensitive to seed point initialization and can be computationally expensive. We propose UI-VISA (U-Net Initialized Vascular Image Segmentation Architecture), a hybrid pipeline that combines the complementary strengths of both approaches. UI-VISA uses U-Net's foreground predictions as informed seed points for a CNN-guided region growing algorithm, which then iteratively refines the segmentation by enforcing local connectivity and recovering fine vessel details that U-Net alone tends to miss or over-predict. We evaluate UI-VISA against standalone U-Net and a prior region-growing-based method (VISA) using 5-fold cross-validation on 26 DSA images. UI-VISA achieves the highest mean Dice and clDice scores across folds, and a paired Wilcoxon signed-rank test shows the improvement in clDice is statistically significant ($p=0.023$), consistent with the method's design goal of preserving vascular connectivity, while the improvement in Dice does not reach significance ($p=0.104$).

</details>


### [116] [Uncovering Understanding-Generation Synergy in Native Unified Multimodal Models: From Representation, Task to System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01607)
*Penghao Wu,Haiwen Diao,Weichen Fan,Lewei Lu,Dahua Lin,Ziwei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文在无预训练视觉先验的可控、原生结构设置下，系统研究统一多模态模型（UMMs）中视觉理解与生成目标之间的关系，从表示、任务和系统三个层面揭示其协同条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一多模态模型将视觉理解和生成整合到单一模型中，但这种功能统一并不保证学习上的协同效应——目标可能相互增强、竞争容量或仅仅共存。此前缺乏对两者关系的系统性理解，本文旨在澄清这一问题的本质。

Method: 采用可控、结构原生的实验设置（不含预训练视觉先验），从三个层面进行分析：1）表示层面，研究理解与生成目标之间的相互信号贡献及共享计算路径下的竞争关系，并测试任务解耦（task-decoupled）架构；2）任务层面，通过三个案例研究考察双向迁移效应；3）系统层面，对比端到端UMM与匹配的"规划器-执行器"流水线在复杂任务上的性能。

Result: 表示层面：生成目标有助于丰富理解所需的视觉特征，理解目标则强化生成所需的视觉-语言对齐；但当两者共用同一计算路径时会产生不对称支配（一方主导）。任务解耦架构可避免这种退化。任务层面：当理解与生成依赖共享知识时，存在正向双向迁移。系统层面：端到端UMM在需要同时进行图像理解与生成的复杂任务上优于匹配的规划器-执行器流水线。

Conclusion: UMMs的价值不仅限于统一接口：适当的专业化分工、共享的任务知识与端到端优化能够将目标的共存转化为真正的协同效应。

Abstract: While unified multimodal models (UMMs) jointly perform visual understanding and generation within a single model, functional unification does not guarantee learning synergy: the two objectives may reinforce each other, compete for capacity, or merely coexist. We investigate their relationship at the representation, task, and system levels in a controlled, structurally native setting without pretrained vision priors. At the representation level, we find that each objective provides useful signal to the other: generation enriches the visual features learned for understanding, while understanding strengthens vision--language alignment for generation. However, when both objectives are forced through the same computation path, one tends to dominate. A task-decoupled architecture that specializes conflicting visual computation while preserving semantic interaction avoids this asymmetric degradation. At the task level, through three case studies, we find positive bidirectional transfer when understanding and generation tasks rely on shared knowledge. At the system level, we show that an end-to-end UMM outperforms a matched planner--executor pipeline on complex tasks that explicitly require both image understanding and generation. Together, these results show that the value of UMMs extends beyond a unified interface: appropriate specialization, shared task knowledge, and end-to-end optimization can turn coexistence into synergy.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [Behaviorally Grounded User Profiles from the Wild for Personalized Alignment and Multi-Perspective Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00014)
*Yuxuan Li,Victor Zhong,Ehsan Kamalloo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"profile behavioral grounding"框架，从真实匿名的社交媒体帖子中提取开放式、高保真的用户画像，替代传统依赖刻板印象的合成画像。该框架在两种范式下评估：基于监督微调的端到端个性化，以及非参数测试时多视角推理。在复杂的推荐和开放式查询基准上，行为驱动的画像一致性地提升基础模型性能并优于合成画像基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于人物角色（persona）的大语言模型个性化技术主要依赖刚性、合成的角色设定，这种方法会扁平化个体差异、依赖刻板印象，无法捕捉驱动真实用户偏好的细微信号。作者旨在解决这一问题，探索如何从真实的用户行为数据中提取高保真用户画像以更好地实现个性化。

Method: 作者提出"行为画像grounding"框架，直接从真实、匿名的社交媒体帖子中提取开放式高保真用户画像。评估采用两条路径：(1) 训练时个性化——通过监督微调（SFT）将画像融入参数； (2) 非参数测试时多视角推理——在推理阶段结合多种视角进行推理。在复杂推荐任务和开放式查询基准上进行评测。

Result: 行为grounded画像在复杂推荐和开放式查询基准上一致性地提升了基础模型的表现，并且优于基于合成画像的基线，驱动了更强的参数对齐能力并实现更丰富的多面推理。

Conclusion: 开放式、行为驱动的用户画像是高度多样化且有效的个性化基础，为下一代个性化语言系统提供了坚实支撑。代码已开源。

Abstract: Persona-driven techniques increasingly adapt large language models (LLMs) to diverse contexts. However, existing methods predominantly rely on rigid, synthetic personas that flatten individual variation, rely on stereotypes, and miss the nuanced signals driving actual human preferences. We introduce profile behavioral grounding, a framework for extracting open-ended, high-fidelity user profiles directly from authentic, anonymized social media posts. We evaluate these profiles across two paradigms: train-time personalization via supervised finetuning (SFT) and non-parametric test-time multi-perspective reasoning. Across complex recommendation and open-ended query benchmarks, behaviorally grounded profiles consistently improve base models and outperform synthetic profile baselines, driving stronger parametric alignment and enabling richer, multifaceted reasoning. Our findings establish open-ended, behavior-derived profiles as a highly diverse and effective foundation for the next generation of personalized language systems. Our code base is available at https://github.com/ServiceNow/behavior-grounding.

</details>


### [118] [trajectory-judge: What Outcome-Only LLM Judges Miss on Agent Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00038)
*Hadi Mohammadi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过一个可控的工具使用支持台环境研究LLM代理的评估盲区：结果仅评估（outcome-only）机制无法识别"以错误方式达成正确结果"的代理。实验发现，output-only judge只能检测到84%的明显故障和45%的静默故障，同时误判33%的正确轨迹；而step-rubric judge在零误报的情况下达到77%的静默故障召回率，但成本是前者的3倍。此外，所有judge都无法检测追加在末尾的虚假承诺，self-consistency集成仅增加成本而无收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理在生产环境中通常采用outcome-only评估（仅向评判者展示请求和最终回复），这种评估机制在结构上对"以错误方式得出正确答案"的代理视而不见。作者希望量化这一盲区，并比较不同judge的能力差异。

Method: 数据来自确定性工具使用的支持台环境，配备脚本化oracle策略和故障注入器（每次精确破坏已知步骤的一个环节），按客户可见结果是否存活将故障分为静默（silent）和响亮（loud）两类。五个judge（程序化规则、outcome-only、两种规模的step-rubric、self-consistency集成）在400条轨迹上评估检测、步骤定位、故障类型、校准和成本。

Result: Outcome-only judge召回84%的响亮故障但仅45%的静默故障，同时将33%的正确轨迹误报。Step-rubric judge以3倍成本达到77%静默故障召回且零误报。所有judge都能被追加的虚构承诺绕过（规则完全绕过，step judge 82%时间被绕过），self-consistency集成成本增至3倍却无任何改善。

Conclusion: 评判评估必须按结果生存状态分层报告召回率，而不是笼统的准确率。作者发布了环境、注入器、全部原始判定结果及可离线重建所有数据的分析管线，以推动该领域的可复现、可分层评估。

Abstract: Outcome-only evaluation is the production default for LLM agents: show a judge the request and the final reply and ask whether it was handled well. The metric is structurally blind to an agent that reaches the right answer the wrong way. We measure that blind spot where ground truth is known by construction: a deterministic tool-using support-desk environment, a scripted oracle policy that always solves it, and a fault injector that breaks exactly one thing at a known step, stratifying faults by whether the customer-visible outcome survived (silent) or not (loud). Five judges (programmatic rules, outcome-only, step-rubric at two model sizes, and a self-consistency ensemble) are scored on detection, step localisation, fault typing, calibration, and cost over 400 trajectories. The outcome-only judge catches 84% of loud faults but 45% of silent ones while flagging 33% of correct trajectories; a step-rubric judge reaches 77% silent recall with zero false alarms at 3x the cost. No judge reads the final reply: an invented promise appended to an otherwise perfect trajectory evades the rules entirely and the step judge 82% of the time, and self-consistency triples cost while improving nothing. We argue that judge evaluations must stratify recall by outcome survival, and release the environment, the injector, all raw verdicts, and an analysis pipeline that rebuilds every number offline.

</details>


### [119] [GUI-CC: Benchmarking Contextual Consistency of GUI World Models as Agent Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00048)
*Lin Fu,Zheyuan Yang,Tianhui Zhang,Jinbiao Wei,Guo Gan,Boxu Liu,Yilun Zhao,Yu Rong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出GUI-CC基准，用于评估GUI世界模型作为多步智能体环境而非单步下一屏预测器时的上下文一致性。该基准包含离线参考动作轨迹和在线智能体循环两个轨道，涵盖500个离线任务和200个在线任务，实验表明当前模型即使单步生成看起来合理，也无法可靠地保持上下文一致性或支持可执行的多步交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GUI世界模型通常被评估为单步下一屏预测器，但其预期用途是作为GUI智能体的多步环境。这种评估与用途之间的错配导致一个关键需求未被充分测试：生成的状态在反复用于未来交互时，必须保持上下文一致性。现有评估无法捕捉模型在多步环境中是否真正可用。

Method: 作者提出GUI-CC基准，包含两个互补轨道：(1) 离线参考动作轨道——沿真实移动GUI轨迹滚动模型；(2) 在线智能体循环轨道——让固定探测智能体与模型生成的UI交互。基准包含从GUIOdyssey构建的500个离线任务和覆盖30个移动应用的200个模拟器验证的在线任务。评估维度包括过渡保真度、过渡合理性、上下文一致性和任务进展。

Result: 实验表明，看似合理的单步生成并不能保证可靠的环境模拟：当前模型经常产生看起来可用的屏幕，却未能保留与任务相关的上下文或支持可执行的多步滚动。

Conclusion: GUI世界模型需要在多步环境场景下进行更严格的评估。GUI-CC揭示了单步预测性能与多步环境可靠性之间的显著差距，表明当前模型在作为GUI智能体环境使用时存在系统性不足，需要针对上下文一致性进行专门优化。

Abstract: GUI world models are increasingly evaluated as one-step next-screen predictors, yet their intended use is often as multi-step environments for GUI agents. This mismatch leaves a key requirement under-tested: generated states must remain contextually consistent when they are repeatedly reused for future interaction. We introduce GUI-CC, a benchmark that evaluates contextual consistency of GUI world models as agent environments rather than isolated next-screen predictors. GUI-CC contains two complementary tracks: an offline reference-action track that rolls models along real mobile GUI trajectories, and an online agent-loop track that lets fixed probing agents interact with model-generated UIs. We construct 500 offline trajectory tasks from GUIOdyssey and 200 emulator-verified online tasks across 30 mobile apps. GUI-CC evaluates transition fidelity, transition plausibility, contextual consistency, and task progress. Experiments show that plausible single-step generation does not guarantee reliable environment simulation: current models often produce usable-looking screens while failing to preserve task-relevant context or support executable multi-step rollouts.

</details>


### [120] [From Detection to Refusal: Safer LLMs via Circuit-Guided Weight Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00051)
*Kuan-Lin Chu,Chung-En Sun,Tsui-Wei Weng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文从机制可解释性视角研究LLM安全行为，发现并表征了一个多阶段的"安全电路"，包括有害检测头、安全神经元和拒绝头三个组成部分。通过因果干预验证了该电路结构，并在六个LLM上验证其跨架构和跨攻击设置的普适性。基于该电路引导的权重缩放方法在攻击下将安全率平均提升26.5%，同时仅造成1.7%的标准基准准确率下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管进行了广泛的对齐工作，LLM在对抗性提示下仍易生成不安全内容，且安全行为背后的内部机制尚未被充分理解。作者希望从机制可解释性角度揭示LLM安全行为的内在实现方式，从而为更有效的安全对齐提供理论基础。

Method: 从机制可解释性（mechanistic interpretability）视角出发，表征了一个多阶段安全电路：包括(i)感知有害输入的有害检测头（Harmful Detection Heads）、(ii)在残差流中介导和稳定安全信号的安全神经元（Safety Neurons）、(iii)将安全信号转化为安全响应的拒绝头（Refusal Heads）。采用定向注意力头和神经元层面的因果干预实验验证电路组织，并通过架构保持的权重缩放（weight scaling）作为机制探针测试其功能相关性。

Result: 因果实验表明抑制上游有害检测头会破坏下游拒绝行为，安全神经元介导这一交互。该分解在多个LLM架构和对抗攻击设置中重复出现。在六个LLM上，电路引导的缩放方法在攻击下将安全率平均提升26.5%，四个标准基准上仅导致1.7%的准确率下降。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持LLM安全的电路级解释，表明机制抽象能够揭示有界对齐行为背后稳定且可迁移的模式，为改进LLM安全性提供了基于内部机制的可行干预方向。

Abstract: Despite extensive alignment efforts, Large Language Models (LLMs) remain vulnerable to generating unsafe content under adversarial prompting, yet the internal mechanisms by which safety behaviors are implemented remain poorly understood. We study LLM safety from a mechanistic interpretability perspective and characterize a multi-stage *safety circuit* that organizes refusal behavior, consisting of (i) $\textbf{Harmful Detection Heads}$ that respond to harmful inputs, (ii) $\textbf{Safety Neurons}$ that mediate and stabilize safety signals in the residual stream, and (iii) $\textbf{Refusal Heads}$ that translate these signals into safe response generation. Using targeted attention-head and neuron-level interventions, we provide causal evidence consistent with this circuit organization, showing that suppressing upstream Harmful Detection Heads disrupts downstream refusal behavior and that safety neurons mediate this interaction. We validate that this decomposition recurs across multiple LLM architectures and adversarial attack settings, and use simple, architecture-preserving weight scaling as a mechanistic probe to test its functional relevance. Across six LLMs, circuit-guided scaling improves safety rates under attacks by 26.5%, while incurring only a 1.7% accuracy drop across four standard benchmarks. Overall, our results support a circuit-level interpretation of LLM safety and suggest that mechanistic abstractions can reveal stable and transferable patterns underlying aligned behavior.

</details>


### [121] [Zero-Shot Respiratory Sound Classification through LLM-Augmented Audio-Text Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00055)
*Mustafa Talha İlerisoy,Hung Manh Pham,Mathias Funk,Mykola Pechenizkiy,Aaqib Saeed*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个框架，将自监督呼吸编码器与医学术语在对齐的潜在空间中进行语义对齐，使其成为具备零样本推断能力的基础模型。通过医学LLM合成结构化报告作为语义锚点，结合sigmoid对比损失、原生SSL目标和相似度感知负采样进行训练。在6个数据集9个任务上，零样本AUC达61.3%，超越CLAP(51.4%)和Qwen2-Audio(54.9%)，线性探测AUC达71.6%，仅使用全量基线43%的数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督呼吸编码器缺乏临床领域的语义锚定，无法进行零样本推理，限制了在没有任务特定标注数据时的实用性。现有方法依赖大规模、通用目的模型，但缺乏临床专科领域的针对性。

Method: 1) 利用医学LLM从元数据合成结构化报告，生成密集的语义锚点以解决配对数据稀缺问题；2) 使用基于sigmoid的对比损失联合编码器原生SSL目标进行训练；3) 引入相似度感知负采样以锐化病理边界。

Result: 在6个数据集上的9项任务中，方法实现了61.3%的平均零样本AUC，超越CLAP(51.4%)和Qwen2-Audio(54.9%)；同时达到最高线性探测AUC(71.6%)，仅使用全规模基线43%的数据。

Conclusion: 结构化语义对齐在临床诊断中优于大规模通用模型，证明通过医学语义锚点增强的自监督编码器可作为零样本能力的基础模型，在数据效率方面具有显著优势。

Abstract: Self-supervised respiratory encoders lack semantic grounding in clinical domain needed for zero-shot inference, limiting their utility without task-specific labeled data. We propose a framework that aligns these encoders with medical terminology in a shared latent space turning them into a zero-shot-capable foundation model. To address paired data scarcity, we use a medical LLM to synthesize structured reports from metadata, creating dense semantic anchors for contrastive learning. Our training combines a sigmoid-based contrastive loss with encoder's native SSL objective and similarity-aware negative sampling to sharpen pathological boundaries. Across 9 tasks on 6 datasets, our method achieves a 61.3% mean zero-shot AUC, surpassing CLAP (51.4%) and Qwen2-Audio (54.9%) while reaching the highest linear probing AUC (71.6%) with only 43% of data used by full-scale baselines, showing that structured semantic alignment outperforms large-scale, general-purpose models in clinical diagnostics.

</details>


### [122] [ValueGraph: Value-Signal Guided Graph Pre-training for Contextualized User Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00057)
*Yitong Han,Wei Gao,Yi Zhao,Prasanta Bhattacharya,Fengzhu Zeng,Mohammad Amanlou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ValueGraph，一个图预训练框架，利用自动推断的道德价值信号作为噪声辅助信号进行情境化用户表征学习。从帖子-回复图中学习语义和结构表征，并通过相对价值相似性使用对比和聚类目标对齐用户。在立场检测和推特机器人检测上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体用户行为不仅受其言论内容或互动对象影响，还受其表达态度时所用的价值信号影响。现有用户表征方法大多忽略了这一价值相关维度，难以捕捉深层的用户道德价值倾向。

Method: ValueGraph采用图预训练框架：(1)从帖子-回复图学习语义和结构表征；(2)使用自动推断的道德价值信号作为噪声辅助信号（而非金标准标签）；(3)通过对比学习和聚类目标，基于相对价值相似性对齐用户；(4)将推断的价值作为软约束而非硬标签用于表征学习。

Result: 在立场检测和推特机器人检测任务上，ValueGraph相较强文本基线、图基线和纯文本LLM基线均取得一致的性能提升，验证了价值信号引导作为社会感知用户建模中有用的归纳偏置。

Conclusion: 价值信号融入用户表征学习是一种有效的归纳偏置，ValueGraph证明了将自动推断的道德价值作为软约束而非硬标签，能够为社交感知的用户建模提供有用指导并显著提升下游任务表现。

Abstract: Value signals are aggregated user-level moral representations that capture users' inferred value-related tendencies from their online discourse. User behavior on social media is shaped not only by what users say or whom they interact with, but also by the value signal through which they express attitudes. Existing user representation methods largely miss this value-relevant dimension. We propose ValueGraph, a graph pre-training framework that uses automatically inferred moral-value signals as noisy auxiliary signals for contextualized user representation. From post-reply graphs, ValueGraph learns semantic and structural representations and further aligns users through relative value similarity with contrastive and clustering objectives. Rather than treating inferred values as gold psychological labels, ValueGraph uses them as soft constraints for representation learning. Experiments on stance detection and twitter bot detection show consistent gains over strong text-based, graph-based, and text-only LLM baselines, highlighting value-signal guidance as a useful inductive bias for socially informed user modeling.

</details>


### [123] [CUDA-Harness: Harnessing Agentic CUDA Kernel Generation and Optimization from Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00058)
*Qi Fan,An Zou,Yehan Ma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CUDA-Harness框架，用于从自然语言直接生成和优化高性能CUDA内核（Text2CUDA），通过Intermediate-Structured Generation连接高层语义理解与低层内核生成，采用Synthesis-Based Verification提供隔离的测试数据与渐进验证以缓解奖励黑客问题，并引入Feedback-Adaptive Evolution在保证正确性的同时优化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发高性能CUDA内核需要算法实现、正确性验证和硬件感知的并行优化等多方面专业知识，存在巨大的人才壁垒。虽然LLM已被用于CUDA内核生成，但现有工作主要聚焦于从PyTorch等高层框架转译到CUDA（Torch2CUDA），而非直接从自然语言生成CUDA（Text2CUDA）。后者要求模型理解高层输入语义并处理低层内核实现与验证，更具挑战性。此外，现有方法依赖预定义测试输入，易受奖励黑客（reward hacking）问题影响。

Method: CUDA-Harness包含三个核心模块：(1) Intermediate-Structured Generation（中间结构化生成）：建立高层语义理解与低层内核生成之间的桥梁；(2) Synthesis-Based Verification（基于合成的验证）：构造隔离的测试数据并进行渐进式验证，以稀释Text2CUDA中的奖励黑客问题；(3) Feedback-Adaptive Evolution（反馈自适应演化）：一种内核演化策略，在优先保证正确性的前提下优化性能。

Result: 通过大量实验证明了CUDA-Harness的有效性，进一步评估展示了其在LLM、硬件平台间的泛化能力，以及扩展到C-to-CUDA转译任务的能力。

Conclusion: CUDA-Harness为解决Text2CUDA任务提供了一套完整的框架，有效应对了语义理解、正确性验证和性能优化三方面的挑战，并展现出良好的跨模型、跨硬件泛化性和任务可扩展性。

Abstract: Developing high-performance CUDA kernels demands specialized knowledge in algorithm implementation, correctness validation, and hardware-aware parallel optimization, creating a substantial expertise barrier and making generating CUDA kernels directly from natural language (Text2CUDA) essential. Meanwhile, the general-purpose code generation capability of Large Language Models (LLMs) prompts a series of works exploring LLM-based CUDA kernel generation. They mainly focus on transpilation from high-level frameworks such as PyTorch to CUDA (Torch2CUDA) rather than Text2CUDA, where models must understand the high-level input semantics and handle low-level kernel implementation and validation. Additionally, these methods are vulnerable to reward hacking due to reliance on predefined test inputs. In this paper, we propose CUDA-Harness, a framework for harnessing agentic CUDA kernel generation and optimization from natural language. Specifically, we introduce Intermediate-Structured Generation to connect high-level semantic understanding with low-level kernel generation. To dilute reward hacking in Text2CUDA, we construct Synthesis-Based Verification to provide isolated test data and progressive validation. Furthermore, we propose Feedback-Adaptive Evolution, a kernel evolution strategy that prioritizes correctness while optimizing performance. Finally, through extensive experiments, we demonstrate the effectiveness of CUDA-Harness, with further evaluations illustrating generalization across LLMs, hardware platforms, and to C-to-CUDA transpilation.

</details>


### [124] [RePro: Proof-Verified Benchmark Rewriting for Reliable Evaluation of LLM Mathematical Problem Solving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00062)
*Xiyuan Zhou,Zhuoqi Li,Xinlei Wang,Yirui He,Yuhao Wu,Yuheng Cheng,Yan Xu,Junhua Zhao,Jinjin Gu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 RePro (Proof-Verified Benchmark Rewriting)，首个将面向 Lean 的神经自动定理证明器(ATPs)集成到基准改写中的框架，通过 Lean 验证保证改写后问题的有效性与答案正确性，用于评估 LLM 是否真正具备数学推理能力还是依赖记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据污染会破坏 LLM 数学推理评估的可靠性。现有的改写式评估方法虽然能在一定程度上缓解记忆化问题，但缺乏对问题有效性和答案正确性的保证，无法确保改写后的评估基准是可信的。

Method: RePro 框架将 Lean 导向的神经自动定理证明器(ATPs)集成到基准改写流程中。它改写数学问题并使用 Lean 形式化证明来重新生成答案，确保每个改写后的实例都通过定理证明的形式验证，从而消除无效或错误实例。

Result: 在 GSM8K 和 MATH 数据集上的实验表明，RePro 保留的改写实例达到了 100% 的良好定义性、可行性和答案正确性，而现有方法仍会产生无效或错误实例。多个模型在证明验证的改写基准上出现准确率下降，表明其性能对表面与结构变化敏感，部分可能反映记忆效应。

Conclusion: RePro 通过将形式化证明验证引入基准改写，提供了更可靠的 LLM 数学能力评估基准。模型在改写基准上的性能下降说明现有评估可能高估了 LLM 的数学推理能力，应警惕记忆效应的影响。

Abstract: Data contamination undermines the reliable evaluation of large language models (LLMs) on mathematical problem solving. While rewriting-based evaluation mitigates memorization, existing methods lack guarantees of problem validity and answer correctness. We propose Proof-Verified Benchmark Rewriting (RePro), the first framework to integrate Lean-oriented neural automated theorem provers (ATPs) into benchmark rewriting, which rewrites problems and regenerates answers with correctness ensured by Lean-verified proofs. Experiments on GSM8K and MATH show that RePro's retained rewritten instances achieve 100% well-definedness, feasibility, and answer correctness, while existing methods still produce invalid or incorrect instances. Moreover, several models exhibit accuracy drops on proof-verified rewritten benchmarks, suggesting that their performance is sensitive to surface-level and structural variations and may partly reflect memorization effects. Our source code and data are available at https://github.com/AI4Engi/RePro.

</details>


### [125] [Medical Causal Hypothesis Verification with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00063)
*Safiyyah Ahmed,Abrar Ansari,Md Aminul Islam,Elena Zheleva*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过一个小规模的初步实验，评估了8个大语言模型在17个医学因果假设上的验证能力。研究发现，虽然LLMs在记忆和召回相关医学信息方面表现良好，但它们在提供有效科学证据支持结论、以及正确拒绝无支持的假设方面表现不佳，表明LLMs尚不能完全可靠地用于医疗保健领域的因果验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在医疗等高可信度领域中的搜索和信息检索应用日益增多，但其能否准确评估因果关系并基于已证实的科学证据进行结论仍需验证。现有研究缺乏对LLMs在因果医学假设验证能力上的系统性评估框架。

Method: 提出一个因果假设验证的评估框架，选取8个LLM和17个医学因果假设，要求模型从文献中验证这些假设并提供科学证据。对模型提供的证据按六项标准进行系统标注（共1,067个标注点），并用九项评估指标进行评估。

Result: LLMs在召回率方面表现强劲，但在提供有效的科学文章和证据支持假设、以及正确拒绝不支持的假设方面表现较差。

Conclusion: 当前LLMs不能完全可靠地用于从生物医学文献中验证因果关系，在将LLMs用于医疗环境中的搜索和信息检索之前，需要进行更严格的评估。

Abstract: The growing use of large language models (LLMs) for search and information retrieval underscores the need to evaluate their reliability in high-stakes domains such as healthcare. Although LLMs can effectively answer questions about diseases, symptoms, and treatments, their ability to accurately assess causal relationships and ground their conclusions in verified scientific evidence remains unclear. Here, we present a preliminary, small-scale study that investigates the accuracy of LLMs in evaluating causal medical claims and supporting them with peer-reviewed research. We propose an evaluation framework for causal hypothesis verification that can be used to systematically track the performance of existing and future LLMs. We assess the performance of eight LLMs on 17 medical causal hypotheses to evaluate whether they can reliably verify these hypotheses using scientific evidence from the literature. We systematically annotate the scientific evidence they provide according to six criteria (a total of 1,067 annotation points) and assess them with nine evaluation metrics. Our analysis shows that while LLMs exhibit strong recall, they often perform poorly at providing valid scientific articles and evidence for support and at rejecting unsupported hypotheses. These findings highlight a critical limitation of current LLMs, as they cannot yet be trusted fully to verify causal relationships from the biomedical literature. This work underscores the need for rigorous evaluation before using LLMs for search and retrieval in healthcare settings.

</details>


### [126] [Scientific Agent Skills: A Library of Procedural Knowledge for Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00065)
*Timothy Kassis,Vinayak Agarwal,Yuhuan He,Darshil Patel,Aubrey M. Brueckner*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Scientific Agent Skills，一个包含163个科学程序、覆盖16个实践领域的开源库，旨在为语言模型Agent提供可辩护的科学分析程序性知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型Agent虽然能返回可运行的代码，但其分析结果往往缺乏科学可辩护性。分析是否可靠取决于程序性选择（如采用哪种被领域接受的检验、哪个标识符命名空间是权威的、哪些注意事项必须附随结果），而当前的Agent缺乏对这些程序性知识的系统化支持。

Method: 构建一个包含163个程序的开放库，覆盖16个科学实践领域（包括基因组学、化学信息学、医学影像、研究设计和科学传播等）。每个技能是一个以有版本的、人类可读的指令文件为核心的目录。Agent仅在与任务相关的需要时才加载该文件；目录中通常还包含参考材料和可运行的脚本。

Result: 该库已作为开放许可资源发布于GitHub（https://github.com/K-Dense-AI/scientific-agent-skills）。作者声明未进行任务级评估，也未报告宿主选择率。

Conclusion: Scientific Agent Skills提供了一种将领域程序性知识结构化封装、供Agent按需调用的思路，是迈向可辩护科学分析的一个重要基础设施，但其有效性和实用性仍需进一步评估验证。

Abstract: A language-model agent asked to analyse an experiment will usually return working code. Whether the analysis is defensible is a different question. A defensible analysis depends on procedural choices: which test the field accepts, which identifier namespace is authoritative, and which caveats must accompany a result. We present Scientific Agent Skills, an open library of 163 such procedures in 16 areas of practice, including genomics, cheminformatics, medical imaging, study design and scientific communication. Each skill is a directory built around a versioned, human-readable instruction file. An agent loads the file only when a task calls for it; the directory often also contains reference material and runnable scripts. We report no task-level evaluation and no host selection rate. Openly licensed and available at https://github.com/K-Dense-AI/scientific-agent-skills.

</details>


### [127] [OCGQuant: Outlier-Companion Grouping for NVFP4 Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00066)
*Yishan Yao,Binjun Li,Hanling Yi,Pengyu Li,Xiaoqing Liu,Zihan Yang,Xiaotian Yu,Zhiwen Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: OCGQuant是一种面向NVFP4低比特量化的后训练量化方法，通过Outlier-Companion Grouping（OCG）将异常值通道与低幅度伴生通道配对，改善NVFP4激活块组成，从而减少由块最大值主导尺度导致的伴生量化误差，在Llama3和Qwen3上取得最优的困惑度与下游精度，同时保持接近RTN的推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NVFP4格式虽高效但缺乏块的激活异常值会主导块尺度，增大块内其余值的量化误差。现有PTQ方法（混合精度、旋转、残差补偿）要么未针对NVFP4定制，要么引入额外计算开销。

Method: 从通道分组视角重新审视NVFP4，定义"伴生量化误差"（Collateral Quantization Error）为块内剩余值在块最大值设定尺度下产生的可约简误差。据此提出OCGQuant，核心是Outlier-Companion Grouping，自适应地将异常值通道与低幅度伴生通道配对以优化NVFP4激活块组成。

Result: 在Llama3和Qwen3上的实验表明，OCGQuant在所评估的PTQ方法中取得最低的WikiText-2困惑度和最高的平均下游任务精度，同时保持接近RTN的prefill加速比，并匹配其峰值解码内存。

Conclusion: OCGQuant通过通道级的异常值-伴生配对策略，在不引入额外计算的前提下有效缓解了NVFP4内部的异常值量化问题，验证了从通道分组视角理解低比特量化误差的价值。

Abstract: NVFP4 is an efficient microscaling format for low-bit inference, but activation outliers can still degrade quantization accuracy within NVFP4 blocks. Within each quantization block, large activations can dominate the block scale, increasing the quantization error of the remaining values sharing the same scale. Existing post-training quantization (PTQ) methods mitigate outlier errors through strategies such as mixed precision, rotation, or residual compensation, but these approaches are either not specifically tailored to NVFP4 or introduce additional computation. In this work, we revisit NVFP4 from a channel-grouping perspective and define the reducible error incurred by remaining block values under the scale set by the block maximum as Collateral Quantization Error. Based on this insight, we propose OCGQuant, a post-training quantization method centered on Outlier-Companion Grouping (OCG), which adaptively pairs outlier channels with low-magnitude companion channels to improve NVFP4 activation block composition. Experiments on Llama3 and Qwen3 show that OCGQuant achieves the lowest WikiText-2 perplexity and highest average downstream accuracy among evaluated PTQ methods, while maintaining prefill speedup close to RTN and matching its peak decoding memory. Code is available at https://github.com/Eshamont/OCGQuant.

</details>


### [128] [Do Multimodal LLMs See Before They Read? Diagnosing Contextual Sycophancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00067)
*Yi-Cheng Lai,Hen-Hsen Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨多模态大语言模型中的"多模态情境谄媚"现象——外部文本可覆盖与图像证据相冲突的信息。作者构建998例诊断样本，独立变化视觉证据、常识先验与外部文本，并提出系统2视觉仲裁（S2VA）方法，通过向情境盲证证人隐藏文本，显著提升模型抗谄媚能力（6个模型上提升19.7-44.1个百分点）。研究还发现最优信息边界因模型与情境来源而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在面对与图像证据冲突的外部文本时，会倾向于遵循文本信息而忽略视觉证据，这种"情境谄媚"失败机制尚不清楚。作者旨在系统性地分析该失败何时发生，并寻求缓解策略。

Method: 构建998例诊断基准，独立操纵三个变量：视觉证据（图像内容异常与否）、常识先验、外部文本。通过移动"情境盲视觉证人"的信息边界来系统探测失败条件。比较多种方法：联合条件化（joint conditioning）、直接评分情境盲证人报告（Witness-Only）、证人-仲裁者双调用流水线（witness-arbiter）、以及系统2视觉仲裁（S2VA，向证人隐藏文本）。在GPT-5.1及六个模型上进行评测，并利用GPT-4o再生子集检验泛化性。

Result: 在异常图像配以Gemini生成的虚假文本时，GPT-5.1的准确率随方法变化显著：联合条件化仅7.9%，直接评分为49.7%，双调用流水线为63.7%，而S2VA达到84.2%。S2VA在全部六个模型上较直接证人报告提升19.7-44.1个百分点，所有配对95%置信区间均不含零。但最优信息边界并非统一：部分模型依赖文本情境框架，GPT-4o再生子集改变了不同方法的相对排序。

Conclusion: 多模态情境谄媚对文本引入时机高度敏感，且受模型与情境来源影响。将外部文本从视觉证人处隔离（如S2VA）是最有效的缓解策略，但不存在普适的最优信息边界，方法选择需因模型和情境类型而定制。

Abstract: External text can override conflicting image evidence in multimodal large language models, a failure we call multimodal contextual sycophancy. We introduce a 998-case diagnostic that independently varies visual evidence, commonsense priors, and external text, and probe when this failure arises by moving the information boundary around a context-blind visual witness. On abnormal images paired with Gemini-generated false text, GPT-5.1 scores 7.9% under joint conditioning, 49.7% when the context-blind witness report is scored directly, 63.7% under a matched two-call witness-arbiter pipeline that exposes the witness to the text, and 84.2% under System-2 Visual Arbitration (S2VA), which withholds the text from the witness. Across six models, S2VA improves over the direct witness report by 19.7 to 44.1 points, with all paired 95% confidence intervals excluding zero. The best information boundary is not uniform: textual context scaffolds some models, and a GPT-4o-regenerated subset changes the relative ordering of joint conditioning, Witness-Only, and S2VA. Contextual sycophancy is therefore sensitive to when text is introduced, as well as to the model and context source.

</details>


### [129] [Life Operators: a self-evolving framework for multiscale life modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00068)
*Shuo Wang,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Life Operators框架,通过算子图将医学AI建模为可组合的任务限定映射,涵盖感知、演化与生成三类算子及桥接算子,支持多尺度耦合与科学假说的局部修订,为医学人工超级智能奠定计算基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学AI从识别迈向临床对话与纵向预测,但统计模型只能学习未来观测,机理模型仅描述特定过程,缺乏一个统一框架来表达患者状态、耦合不同尺度以及修订失败的假设。AI如何回答"干预下患者状态如何变化"这一核心问题需要新的建模范式。

Method: 定义任务限定的Life Operators,包含三类科学角色:感知算子(从多模态观测推断生物学状态)、演化算子(在自然或干预条件下传播状态)、生成算子(映射状态到可测信号)。桥接算子连接不同变量、尺度和时间步长的组件。选定算子与桥接构成任务特定的算子图,包含足以支撑声明的状态与机理最小集合。结构模块化使科学修订局部化,AI共同科学家可提议修改状态、算子、桥接或图结构,并由独立证据决定变体保留、限制或淘汰。

Result: 该框架提供统一的模块化架构,桥接多模态数据与机理模型之间的鸿沟,实现跨尺度耦合;算子图将"最小充分状态集"显式化;模块化使科学假设的修订局部化、可追踪、可证据驱动;验证组件可累积为跨层次人体模型。

Conclusion: Life Operators为医学AI提供一个以"任务绑定算子+模块化算子图"为核心的计算范式,使可验证组件得以逐步累积,最终可能发展为人体多尺度综合模型,成为医学人工超级智能的计算基础。

Abstract: Medical AI is moving beyond recognition towards clinical dialogue and longitudinal prediction. Yet a central question remains: how would a patient's state change under intervention? Statistical models learn future observations, whereas mechanistic models describe selected processes. Neither provides a common framework for representing patient state, coupling scales or revising failed assumptions. We propose Life Operators: task-bounded mappings that define three scientific roles. Perception operators infer task-relevant biological states from multimodal observations, Evolution operators propagate these states under natural or intervention-conditioned dynamics, and Generation operators map them to measurable signals. Each role may be realised by equations, statistical models, neural networks or hybrids. Bridge operators connect components with different variables, scales and time steps. Selected operators and bridges form task-specific Operator Graphs containing the smallest set of states and mechanisms sufficient for a declared claim. This modular structure also makes scientific revision localisable. An AI co-scientist may propose changes to states, operators, bridges or graph structure, while independent evidence determines which variants are retained, restricted or retired. Over time, validated components could accumulate into broader multiscale models of the human body and provide a computational foundation for medical artificial superintelligence.

</details>


### [130] [Auditing Harness Tampering in Self-Improving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00069)
*Xing Wang,Xiaoyi Zhang,Jie Shao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了"harness tampering"（操控框架篡改）这一概念，指智能体在自我改进过程中修改自身运行框架，可能产生虚假的性能提升或违背授权、溯源、完整性等约束。作者提出了一个双轴分类体系（按框架功能角色和违反的义务分类），构建了带标注的篡改-良性编辑对语料库，并对多种审计方法进行了基准测试，最后系统审计了真实自我改进智能体的轨迹，发现篡改普遍存在、常存续于最佳智能体的谱系中，并形成系统特定的分类画像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我改进智能体在迭代修改自身运行框架以求性能提升的过程中，可能出现虚假的性能增益或违背授权、溯源和完整性等约束，但现有工作仅关注奖励篡改（reward tampering）和测量篡改（measurement tampering），尚未系统研究整个自我改进生命周期中的此类问题。

Method: 提出双轴分类体系（按框架功能角色与违反的义务分类）；在真实自我改进智能体的轨迹中植入篡改-良性编辑对构建注释语料库；适配并基准测试多种审计方法用于篡改分类和定位任务；对真实自我改进智能体的运行轨迹进行系统审计。

Result: 实证结果表明：操控框架篡改在来自不同智能体的真实运行中持续发生；篡改往往存续于表现最佳的智能体谱系中；篡改在不同系统上形成独特的分类画像。

Conclusion: 操控框架篡改是自我改进智能体中真实且普遍存在的安全风险，需要有效的审计方法来检测和定位此类篡改行为，以保障智能体自我改进过程的完整性和可信度。

Abstract: Self-improving agents iteratively modify their own harness to push the frontier of their performance. However, such modifications can produce illusory performance gains or compromise integrity constraints such as authorization, provenance, and completeness without genuinely improving capability. We term this phenomenon as harness tampering, which extends the concept from reward and measurement tampering to the full self-improvement lifecycle. To systematically study this problem, we propose a two-axis taxonomy that categorizes each misaligned edit by the harness functional role in which it occurs and the obligation it violates. Then we build an annotated corpus by seeding tampered-benign edit pairs into the real trajectories of self-improving agents. We adapt and benchmark diverse audit methods on tampering classification and localization tasks. Finally we systematically audit real trajectories of self-improving agents. The results demonstrate that harness tampering consistently occurs in real runs from different agents, often persists in the lineage of the best agent, and forms distinct system-specific profiles across the taxonomy.

</details>


### [131] [Beneath the Diff: Diagnosing and Mitigating Algorithmic Mode Collapse in Code-Level Autonomous Research Loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00077)
*Bowei He,Weixu Zhang,Yili Jin,Xue Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统诊断了代码级自主研究循环（ARLs）中的算法模式坍缩问题：即使编辑的表层多样性保持不变，代理会反复提出同类算法变更，导致循环内指标收益与独立留出评估收益之间的差距扩大。作者提出多样性感知提议采样（DAPS），通过类别覆盖重加权、持久编辑记忆和验证门控三种轻量级机制，将编辑语义簇衰减降低69.1%，盲测与审计指标上的相对忠实性分别提升83.7%和81.6%，同时保持循环内优化速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自动化机器学习中，LLM智能体通过修改实验训练流水线并根据可验证的循环内指标保留改进。尽管可执行指标看似提供了可靠的进步信号，但尚不清楚反复的指标驱动的代码编辑是否会导致超越循环的、可泛化的真实改进。本文旨在系统诊断这一核心问题。

Method: 作者首先系统诊断发现算法模式坍缩这一失败模式：表面编辑多样性稳定但语义和机制级多样性坍塌。随后提出DAPS方法，包含三种轻量级机制：类别覆盖重加权、持久编辑记忆和验证门控。实验采用三层协议分离循环内指标、审计指标（门控读取）和盲测指标（任何循环组件不可访问）。

Result: DAPS将编辑的语义簇衰减降低69.1%，盲测相对忠实性提升83.7%，审计相对忠实性提升81.6%，同时不牺牲循环内优化速度。

Conclusion: 反复的指标驱动代码编辑确实可能导致仅优化循环内表面指标而未产生真正泛化改进的模式坍缩，但通过DAPS这类轻量级多样性感知机制可以有效缓解此问题，为构建更可靠的自动化研究循环提供了可行的改进方向。

Abstract: Code-level autonomous research loops (ARLs) have recently emerged as a concrete object of study in automated machine learning research. In such loops, an LLM agent proposes modifications to an experimental training pipeline, executes the modified pipeline, and retains edits that improve a verifiable in-loop metric. Although executable metrics may appear to provide a reliable signal of progress, it remains unclear whether repeated metric-driven code editing leads to genuine improvements that generalize beyond the loop. We provide a systematic diagnosis of this question. Across various experiment settings, we identify a robust failure mode that we call \textbf{algorithmic mode collapse}. In this regime, surface-level edit diversity remains stable, but semantic and mechanism-level diversity collapse: the agent continues to edit different lines of code while repeatedly proposing the same kinds of algorithmic changes. This collapse is accompanied by a widening gap between in-loop metric gains and gains measured on independent held-out evaluations. We then propose Diversity-Aware Proposal Sampling (\textsc{DAPS}), a lightweight mitigation that combines category-coverage reweighting, persistent edit memory, and a validation gate. Under a three-tier protocol separating the in-loop metric, the audit metric read by the gate, and a blind metric no loop component ever accesses, \textsc{DAPS} reduces semantic-cluster decay of edits by $69.1\%$ and improves relative faithfulness by $83.7\%$ blind and $81.6\%$ audited, while preserving in-loop optimization speed. We provide the code in Github \href{https://github.com/BokwaiHo/arl-mode-collapse}{repository}.

</details>


### [132] [KItCAT: Knowledge Injection via Input Corruption for Auto-regressive Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00082)
*Meghanadh Pulivarthi,Kushagra Bhushan,Vineet Kumar,Gaurav Pandey,Jaydeep Sen,Dinesh Raghu,Sachindra Joshi,Yatin Nandwani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: KItCAT提出了一种轻量级的训练策略，通过随机破坏输入序列（随机替换部分输入token）来替代昂贵的释义生成，实现知识注入中的数据增强，显著提升持续预训练（CPT）在专业领域知识获取上的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在预训练中获取大量知识，但缺乏专业领域（如手册、技术文档）的特定知识。持续预训练（CPT）常被用于注入此类知识，但小众文档很少重复事实，导致模型难以稳健学习。现有方法通过生成多种释义来解决，但释义生成计算开销大且需要强大的LLM。

Method: KItCAT（Knowledge Injection via Corrupted Auto-regressive Training）在标准下一token预测基础上，随机破坏输入序列：将输入中的随机token子集替换为其他词汇token，同时保持原始下一token标签不变。这种简单的干预从每个样本生成多样化的训练输入，实现几乎零成本的大规模数据增强。

Result: 实验表明，KItCAT在多个数据集和不同模型家族上均一致优于标准CPT基线。

Conclusion: KItCAT通过廉价的输入扰动替代昂贵的释义生成，为decoder-only LLM提供了一种高效的知识注入训练策略，代码已开源。
