Analysis of real coding-agent traces show online recovery opportunities: 118 sessions retain observable work before a queued later message is delivered; across 167 overlapping assistant responses, enqueue-to-completion overlap reaches 56.55~s at p95. Across 300 challenging revision/commit executions, \textsc{Revise} matches a latest-version oracle with no stale outputs or effects. On unmodified LangGraph and LLMCompiler applications using Qwen3-14B, it reduces model calls by 40.6--56.0\% relative to full restart and by 31.3--43.6\% relative to suffix recomputation. Under serving pressure, it further reduces revision-to-correct-completion tokens by 13.26\% and improves SLO goodput by 3.07--5.43\%.

</details>


### [300] [DramaChain Bench: An End-to-End Benchmark for Short-Drama Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00646)
*Haoyuan Shi,Mingtao Chen,Shuo Jiang,Ziyan Chen,Xuyi Sheng,Yiming Liu,Ying Zhang,Miao Wang,Jianxiang Lu,Fanyang Lu,Songyuanyi Lu,Xiele Wu,Zhichao Hu,Yuhong Liu,Richeng Xuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DramaChain Bench 是首个覆盖短剧全生产链路（剧本、分镜、关键帧、镜头级视频、成片）的评测基准，基于内部三大系统构建，含63个叶子维度，由专业标注员生成17,488个有效评分，并引入 DramaChain Agentic Judge 实现无标注成本下的可靠自动评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有短剧评测基准仅评估视频生成单一阶段，且使用预先编写的输入而非真实上游pipeline输出，无法回答两个关键问题：各阶段是否符合原始剧本意图（而非仅符合其直接输入提示），以及不同镜头在多集发布后是否保持连贯性。

Method: 提出 DramaChain Bench，基于三个内部系统：DramaChain Dimensions（五条评测轴实例化于每个阶段，解析为63个叶子维度）、DramaChain Agent（依据商业短剧平台在流程和质量上校准，支持跨模型逐阶段公平比较）、DramaChain Labeling System（5,785项由三位专业标注员独立评分，缺陷在时空上定位并从预定义清单选择，产出17,488个有效评分和255,925条可追溯归因记录）。同时引入 DramaChain Agentic Judge，在每个叶子维度上自动打分，经多轮智能体证据收集后依据逐项检查清单判定。

Result: 人工标注证实上游缺陷会向下游级联传播，最终集数质量并非仅由视频生成阶段决定。DramaChain Agentic Judge 在叶子维度上的平均PLCC达0.918，足以在不产生额外标注成本的情况下准入新模型。

Conclusion: 全链路逐阶段评测是理解短剧质量的关键，上游缺陷级联效应说明仅优化视频生成环节无法保证最终成片质量；自动评测器在较高相关性下可取代人工标注，支撑大规模模型评测与准入。

Abstract: Commercial short-drama production follows a multi-stage chain: script, storyboard, keyframe imagery, shot-level video, and the finished short drama. Most existing benchmarks evaluate solely the video-generation stage using pre-authored inputs instead of real upstream pipeline outputs. This leaves two critical questions unanswerable: whether each stage adheres to the original script intent (rather than only its immediate input prompt), and whether disparate shots remain coherent after assembly into multi-episode releases. We present DramaChain Bench, the first short-drama benchmark that evaluates every stage of the complete production chain. It is built upon three in-house systems sharing one dimension system, DramaChain Dimensions: five evaluation axes instantiated at every stage, resolving into 63 leaf dimensions. DramaChain Agent is calibrated against commercial short-drama platforms in both workflow and finished short-drama quality, enabling stage-wise fair comparison across models. DramaChain Labeling System has each of the 5,785 items scored independently by three professional annotators, with all defects spatio-temporally localised and selected from a predefined defect list. This process produces 17,488 valid scores and 255,925 traceable attribution records. The human annotations confirm that upstream defects cascade across the pipeline, demonstrating that final episode quality is not governed by video generation alone. DramaChain Agentic Judge then scores every leaf dimension automatically, gathering evidence over multiple agentic rounds before judging against a per-item checklist; it reproduces the model ranking at a mean PLCC of 0.918, enough to admit new models at no annotation cost.

</details>


### [301] [Self-Reports Are Not Verification: Environment-Grounded Auditing of LLM Operators in Evolutionary Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00652)
*Enrong Pan,Ryan Zhou,Ting Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种环境锚定的审计方法，用于检验语言模型智能体自我报告的可靠性。在无人工标注的Contexto进化搜索环境中，通过200次运行、五种配置和三个模型家族产生12,249份自我报告，检验三项假设：置信度校准、理性继承影响后续提案、适应度选择提升报告质量，结果三项假设全部失败。智能体高估top-100成功率达4.8至9.3倍，理性继承的实际收益仅为约250个排名，选择差异和亲子传播均无法检测。结论：智能体自我报告应视为待验证的主张而非其可靠性的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型智能体越来越多地根据外部反馈行动并解释自身行为，其置信度和理由是便利的监控信号，但便利不等于验证。现有研究常将智能体的自我报告视作可靠的监控依据，缺乏环境级验证，可能掩盖系统性的过度自信和失真。

Method: 设计环境锚定审计：在Contexto进化搜索环境中，反馈函数为每个有效猜测分配精确排名且无需人工注释。智能体在200次运行中覆盖五种配置和三个模型家族，生成四种报告的12,249份自我报告。对每项假设设计受控实验：检验置信度校准、通过754个继承理由的受控干预量化其真实影响、比较适应度选择与随机选择在报告准确性和传播上的差异。

Result: 三项假设全部失败：运营者将top-100成功率高估4.8到9.3倍，校准与判别力在不同模型家族间分离；754个继承理由的受控干预表明真实理由的任何可测量收益仅为约250个排名；无论适应度选择还是随机选择，报告准确性上均无显著选择差异或亲子传播，尽管搜索行为差异明显。

Conclusion: 语言模型智能体的自我报告（置信度和推理）不具备内在可靠性，不能作为自身行为的可信监控信号。智能体自我报告应被视为需要对照环境验证的主张，而非其自身可靠性的证据。

Abstract: Language model agents increasingly propose actions, observe external feedback, and explain their own behavior. Their confidence and rationales are convenient monitoring signals, but convenience is not verification. We introduce an environment-grounded audit in which every intermediate proposal receives an exact outcome. A language model operates an evolutionary Contexto search whose feedback function assigns every valid guess an exact rank without human annotation. Across 200 runs spanning five configurations and three model families, four reporting configurations produce 12,249 self-reports. We test three assumptions: stated confidence is calibrated, inherited rationales affect later proposals, and fitness-based selection improves report quality. All three fail. Operators overstate top-100 success by factors of 4.8 to 9.3, while calibration and discrimination dissociate across model families. Controlled interventions on 754 inherited rationales bound any measured benefit of the genuine rationale to roughly 250 ranks. Neither fitness-based nor random selection produces a detectable selection differential or parent-to-offspring transmission in report accuracy, despite sharply different search behavior. Agent self-reports should therefore be treated as claims to verify against the environment, not as evidence of their own reliability.

</details>


### [302] [SciTrue: Reliable Scientific Claim Validation with Frontier and Open Language Models at the NTCIR SciClaimEval Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00654)
*Qiming Bao,Neşet Özkan Tan,Siyuan Wang,Mark Gahegan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍SciTrue团队在NTCIR-19 SciClaimEval任务两个子任务中的参与。团队采用诚实、逐样本协议对11个前沿和开源多模态模型进行基准测试，并结合轻量透明后处理。在官方盲测排行榜上，SciTrue在四个证据类别/子任务组合中有三个以明显优势获得第一，并在第四个中并列第一。三个发现解释了这一结果：1) 强指令微调模型已具备竞争力（Claude Opus 4.8和Gemma-4-31B超过最强公开基线o4-mini）；2) 任务配对结构是最重要的杠杆——一个无泄漏的配对先验（仅从可见字段claim文本恢复Supported/Refuted配对，将Supported分配给更高置信度证据）将Subtask-1配对准确率从72.2提升至93.5，远超任何模型替换或集成加权；3) 逐案例审计发现大多数残余错误是视觉上无法检测的标签映射交换或数据集标签噪声，因此测量准确率低估了真实能力。此外还发现了数据打包排序编码标签的测量泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是参与NTCIR-19 SciClaimEval任务，该任务要求系统根据论文的表格和图片验证科学声明。团队选择不单纯调优单一模型，而是通过基准测试多个多模态模型并结合轻量后处理来系统性地探索该任务的最佳方法，同时关注测量泄漏等问题。

Method: 方法包括：(1) 在统一的诚实逐样本协议下对11个前沿和开源多模态模型进行基准测试；(2) 使用轻量、透明的后处理；(3) 利用无泄漏的配对先验——仅从claim文本（可见字段）恢复Supported/Refuted配对结构，并将Supported分配给更高置信度的证据；(4) 进行逐案例审计、受控微调、蒸馏和代理一致性检查来验证结论；(5) 记录并分析了数据打包排序编码标签的测量泄漏现象。

Result: 在NTCIR-19 SciClaimEval官方盲测排行榜上，SciTrue在四个证据类别/子任务组合中三个以明显优势排名第一，第四个并列第一。具体结果包括：GPT-5.5和Claude Fable 5在Subtask-2上达到97.7；配对先验将Subtask-1配对准确率从72.2提升至93.5，效果远超任何模型替换或集成加权。审计发现大部分残余错误源于视觉无法检测的标签映射交换或数据集标签噪声。

Conclusion: 主要结论：(1) 强指令微调开源模型在科学声明验证任务上已具竞争力；(2) 任务固有的配对结构是比模型选择更有效的能力杠杆；(3) 数据集实际质量和标签噪声使得测量准确率低估真实模型能力，且可通过建模修复的空间很小；(4) 测量泄漏（标签通过数据打包而非内容到达系统）是评测任务设计中需要警惕的系统性问题。

Abstract: We describe the SciTrue team's participation in both subtasks of the NTCIR-19 SciClaimEval task~\cite{sciclaimeval}, which asks systems to verify scientific claims against the tables and figures of a paper. Rather than tuning a single model, we benchmark eleven frontier and open multimodal models under one honest, per-sample protocol and combine them with light, transparent post-processing. On the official, blind test leaderboard (Section~\ref{sec:results}), SciTrue placed first by a clear margin in three of the four evidence-category/subtask combinations, and tied for first on the primary metric in the fourth. Three findings explain the result. First, strong instruction-tuned models are already competitive: Claude Opus~4.8 and Gemma-4-31B each exceed the strongest public baseline (o4-mini), and GPT-5.5 and Claude Fable~5 lead both subtasks (97.7 on Subtask~2). Second, the task's pairing structure is the largest lever: a \emph{leak-free pair prior} that recovers the Supported/Refuted pairing from the claim text alone (a visible field) and assigns Supported to the higher-confidence evidence raises Subtask-1 pair-accuracy from 72.2 to 93.5, far more than any model swap or ensemble weighting. Third, a case-by-case audit finds that most residual errors are visually-undetectable label-mapping swaps or dataset label noise, so measured accuracy understates the true ability and the fixable-by-modeling headroom is small. Controlled fine-tuning, distillation, and agentic consistency-checking support the same conclusions, and we document throughout a measurement leak---label information reaching a system through the packaging of the data rather than its content---in which the released file ordering encodes the label, including one instance that briefly misled our own pipeline.

</details>


### [303] [Drift-Aware LLM Routing with Sparse Contexts and Shared Budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00662)
*Cheung Hao Lee,Patrick Wong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究多模型语言服务中的上下文路由问题，提出漂移感知稀疏路由（DRS）算法。该问题被建模为非平稳稀疏上下文路由，带有多重背包约束（计算、延迟、内存、货币成本）和可选的影子审计流。DRS利用滚动审计窗口估计奖励与资源使用，采用悲观奖励与乐观成本估计来做出路由决策，通过在线更新影子价格并施加硬计量器来满足预算约束。在理论上，DRS在稀疏线性模型与有界漂移假设下，其遗憾界被分解为控制与统计两部分，获得了含稀疏度、审计率和窗口长度参数的自适应界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模型语言服务需要根据每个请求动态路由到合适的模型，以满足工作负载级的多种资源预算约束。与静态模型选择不同，该问题面临两个核心挑战：(1) 提示表示是高维的，只有少数嵌入方向能预测模型的增量价值（稀疏性）；(2) 请求分布和模型前沿（模型发布、微调、量化变化、系统更新）会随时间漂移（非平稳性）。因此需要一个能够同时应对高维稀疏特征与漂移环境的在线路由算法。

Method: 提出DRS（Drift-Aware Sparse Routing）算法。核心设计包括：(1) 从滚动审计窗口估计奖励和资源消耗；(2) 使用悲观奖励估计和乐观成本估计进行路由决策以保障预算；(3) 在线更新资源影子价格；(4) 在提交前施加硬性计量器（hard meter）以确保约束满足。理论分析将控制问题与统计估计分离：在均匀预测半径{β_t}的事件上，相对于有节奏的动态流体基准的遗憾由半径之和、容量缓冲区项和O(√T)节奏项界定。在稀疏线性模型和有界漂移V_T假设下，通过优化窗口长度W，当无漂移时恢复平稳O(√(sT/ρ))速率，存在漂移时获得O(T^(2/3)(s/ρ)^(1/3)V_T^(1/3))自适应项。

Result: DRS在理论上获得了双层遗憾界：(1) 在通用的均匀预测半径事件上，遗憾依半径之和、容量缓冲项与O(√T)节奏项界定；(2) 在稀疏线性模型下，滚动估计给出ÑO(T√(s/(ρW)) + WV_T + √T)的遗憾界，其中s为稀疏度，ρ为审计率，W为窗口长度。通过优化W：无漂移时达到O(√(sT/ρ))的平稳速率；有漂移时获得O(T^(2/3)(s/ρ)^(1/3)V_T^(1/3))的适应项，体现了统计学习与控制/优化之间的清晰分离。

Conclusion: 本文首次将多模型语言服务路由形式化为非平稳稀疏上下文路由问题，提出DRS算法作为解决方案。其核心贡献在于理论上的严密分析——将控制（路由决策优化）与统计（环境估计）分离，以获得清晰的遗憾分解；并通过优化窗口长度在平稳与非平稳场景间取得权衡。结果表明DRS在保持预算约束的同时，能够有效适应模型前沿和请求分布的漂移，且遗憾上界中自适应项以T^(2/3)的速率增长，优于朴素方法。

Abstract: A multi-model language service must route each request while preserving workload-level budgets for compute, latency, memory, or monetary cost. Two features make this problem materially harder than static model selection. Prompt representations are high dimensional, so only a small subset of embedding directions may predict the incremental value of a model, and both the request mix and the model frontier drift after launches, fine-tunes, quantization changes, and system updates. We formulate nonstationary sparse contextual routing with multiple knapsack constraints and an optional shadow-audit stream that evaluates a small fraction of prompts on several models.
  We propose Drift-Aware Sparse Routing (DRS). The policy estimates reward and resource use from a rolling audit window, routes using pessimistic reward and optimistic cost estimates, updates resource shadow prices online, and applies a hard meter before commitment. The analysis separates control from statistics. On any event with uniform prediction radii $\{β_t\}$, regret against a paced dynamic fluid benchmark is bounded by the sum of the radii, a capacity-buffer term, and an $O(\sqrt{T})$ pacing term. Under a sparse linear model and bounded drift $V_T$, rolling estimation gives \[ \widetilde O\left( T\sqrt{\frac{s}{ρW}}+WV_T+\sqrt{T} \right), \] where $s$ is sparsity, $ρ$ is the audit rate, and $W$ is the window length. Optimizing $W$ yields the usual stationary $O(\sqrt{sT/ρ})$ rate when $V_T=0$ and a $O(T^{2/3}(s/ρ)^{1/3}V_T^{1/3})$ adaptation term under drift.

</details>


### [304] [Triple-Bottom-Line Sustainability of Language Models for Edge AI: A Comparison Between SLMs and Quantized LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00665)
*Jainil Dharmil Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可复现的HSS综合可持续性评分体系（涵盖经济、环境、社会三大支柱），系统比较了原生训练的小语言模型（SLM）与通过后训练量化压缩的大语言模型（LLM）在边缘部署中的综合表现。通过对5个BF16 SLM和5种量化方式的LLM构成的30种配置实测，发现量化后LLM（如Qwen3-30B-A3B/GGUF Q4，93.38分）在整体可持续性上可超越原生SLM，但SLM在资源需求方面仍具竞争力，从而否定了"原生SLM必然最可持续"的普遍假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘AI模型选择通常仅由单一指标（准确率、延迟、内存、能耗或安全）驱动，而可部署的语言模型必须同时平衡这五个维度。此前缺乏一个综合框架来回答一个关键问题：原生训练的小语言模型（SLM）与经后训练量化压缩的大语言模型（LLM），哪一种能为边缘部署提供更可持续的权衡方案。

Method: 构建围绕三重底线的Holistic Sustainability Score（HSS）评分体系，包含：经济支柱（能力与系统效率）、环境支柱（GPU运行能耗）和社会支柱（有害提示鲁棒性）。实验设计为5个BF16 SLM和5个LLM采用BF16、INT8、NF4 4-bit、GPTQ 4-bit和GGUF Q4共5种量化方式，形成30种实测配置。能力基于5个零样本基准评估；效率考量延迟、吞吐量、峰值VRAM和能耗；安全性以5种有害提示的攻击成功率近似衡量。

Result: Qwen3-30B-A3B/GGUF Q4综合排名第一（93.38分），Mistral-Small-24B/GGUF Q4位列第二（92.40分），而Phi-4-mini/BF16是综合池中排名最高的SLM（89.49分）。量化后LLM在整体可持续性上可超过原生SLM，量化方式的选择是一种系统级决策而非单调的精度-效率权衡。

Conclusion: 原生SLM必须是最可持续的边缘部署选择的假设并非普遍成立；经过优化的量化LLM可以整体胜出，而SLM仍可通过更低的资源需求保持竞争力。HSS评分是相对比较池和代理指标定义而言的，提示其结果解读需结合具体部署场景。

Abstract: Edge-AI model selection is commonly driven by one isolated metric - accuracy, latency, memory, energy, or safety, even though a deployable language model must balance all five. Our work focuses on answering the question whether na- tively trained small language models (SLMs) or large language models (LLMs) compressed through post-training quantization offer the more sustainable edge- deployment trade-off. We introduce a reproducible Holistic Sustainability Score (HSS) organized around the triple bottom line: an economic pillar for capability and systems efficiency, an environmental pillar for operational GPU energy and a social pillar for harmful-prompt robustness. Five BF16 SLMs and five LLMs under different quantization approaches - BF16, INT8, NF4 4-bit, GPTQ 4-bit, and GGUF Q4 produce 30 measured configurations. Capability is assessed on five zero-shot benchmarks; efficiency uses latency, throughput, peak VRAM and energy; and safety is approximated by attack success rate on five harmful prompts. Qwen3-30B-A3B/GGUF Q4 ranks first in the combined pool (93.38), followed by Mistral-Small-24B/GGUF Q4 (92.40), while Phi-4-mini/BF16 is the highest- ranked SLM in that pool (89.49). Thus, the hypothesis that native SLMs must be the most sustainable edge choice is not supported universally; optimized quantized LLMs can win overall, while SLMs remain competitive through lower resource demand. Quantization is a systems-level choice rather than a monotonic precision- efficiency trade-off and HSS remains relative to its comparison pool and proxy definitions.

</details>


### [305] [Value Over Language Model: Detecting Original Contribution in Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00700)
*Vibhhu Sharma,Thorsten Joachims,Sarah Dean*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出VOLM（Value Over Language Model）框架，用于衡量用户在LLM辅助写作中的真实信息贡献，而非检测表面文本是否由AI生成。该框架无需训练或标注数据，通过在不同粒度级别提取文档内容、让LLM从部分表示重建文档，并与仅基于任务描述生成的重建结果进行对比，从而计算文档相对LLM基线输出的增量价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM文本检测工具主要衡量文档表层文本有多少由LLM生成，而非衡量信息内容或思想中有多少真正源自LLM本身、多少由用户在提示中提供。这导致无法准确评估用户在LLM辅助写作中的真实贡献，容易受风格混杂因素干扰。

Method: VOLM框架：（1）从文档中提取不同粒度级别的内容表示；（2）使用LLM从每个部分表示重建完整文档；（3）将这些重建结果与仅基于任务描述生成的重建文档进行比较。该方法不使用文档表层文本评分，从而排除文体风格干扰；无需训练数据或标注。

Result: 在新闻文章、ICLR同行评审和议论文三个领域评估中，VOLM能有效区分人类撰写的文档与从通用任务描述生成的LLM文档，同时对内容保持变换（如LLM重建和往返翻译）表现显著不变性。此外，越受约束的内容提取器越能减少LLM生成文本与人工改写文本之间的残余差异，表明该方法能有效解耦信息内容与风格变化。

Conclusion: VOLM提供了一种无需训练的框架来评估用户在LLM辅助写作中的信息贡献，并与风格脱钩。研究希望鼓励未来在专业化的框架实例化以及更广泛地评估LLM辅助写作中人类贡献方面开展进一步工作。

Abstract: LLMs have been rapidly adopted across writing tasks, prompting the development of tools for detecting LLM-generated text. Yet, these tools largely measure how much of a document's surface text was written by an LLM and aren't fundamentally designed to measure how much of the information content or ideas originated from the LLM itself rather than being supplied by the user in the prompt. In this work, we design a framework that measures how much value a person adds on top of what a language model could have easily produced by itself. The method requires no training or labeled data and never scores the document's surface text, insulating it from stylistic confounders. Instead, it extracts the document's content at increasing levels of granularity, uses an LLM to reconstruct the document from each partial representation, and compares these reconstructions with those produced from the task description alone. We call this framework Value Over Language Model (VOLM), which measures a document's contribution relative to a replacement-level document that an LLM could produce from the task description alone. We evaluate VOLM with a specific instantiation of this framework across three domains: news articles, ICLR peer reviews, and argumentative essays. VOLM separates human-authored documents from matched LLM-generated documents produced from generic task descriptions, while remaining substantially invariant to content-preserving transformations, including LLM-based reconstruction and round-trip translation. We further find that increasingly constrained content extractors reduce residual differences between LLM-generated and humanized text, demonstrating the importance of disentangling informational content from stylistic variation. We hope these results encourage further work on specialized instantiations of the framework and on assessing human contributions in LLM-assisted writing more generally.

</details>


### [306] [ChatDev 2.0: A No-Code Multi-Agent Platform for Developing Everything](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00714)
*Yufan Dang,Shu Yao,Bowen Lai,Chenting Xu,Ruijie Shi,Wai-Shing Leung,Huatao Li,Chen Qian,Zhiyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DevAll（ChatDev 2.0）是一个无代码的多智能体系统（MAS）平台，通过声明式可执行图抽象与循环感知执行引擎结合可视化界面，同时实现了高表达力与易用性，并在三个代表性任务上以竞争性能复现了最先进的MAS，无需任务特定的编排代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的多智能体系统在解决复杂任务时面临两难困境：代码框架表达力强但工程开销大，而无代码构建器简化了创作但将智能体交互限制在作者定义的工作流中。因此需要一个平台能同时兼顾高表达力与易用性。

Method: DevAll采用两层设计：在表达力层面，将声明式可执行图抽象与循环感知执行引擎（cycle-aware execution engine）配对，使异构智能体以及动态和循环交互能够在单一框架内被表示和执行；在易用性层面，集成可视化界面支持用户无需编写代码即可编排、运行、监控和检查MAS，包括人机协同步骤。

Result: 实验表明，DevAll在三个代表性任务上以竞争性能复现了最先进的MAS，且无需任务特定的编排代码，证明了其作为通用LLM-based MAS平台的有效性。

Conclusion: DevAll成功弥合了MAS开发中表达力与易用性之间的鸿沟，为异构智能体及动态循环交互提供了无需编码的通用构建、执行与检查平台，相关代码已开源。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems (MAS) have shown strong potential for solving complex tasks, yet their development forces a tradeoff: code frameworks are expressive but engineering-intensive, while no-code builders simplify authoring but constrain agent interactions to author-defined workflows. We present ChatDev 2.0: DevAll (hereafter DevAll), a no-code platform for building, executing, and inspecting heterogeneous MAS that delivers both high expressiveness and ease of use. In terms of expressiveness, DevAll pairs a declarative executable graph abstraction with a cycle-aware execution engine, so that heterogeneous agents and dynamic and cyclic interactions can be represented and executed within a single framework. For ease of use, an integrated visual interface lets users author, run, monitor, and inspect MAS, including human-in-the-loop steps, entirely without writing code. Experiments demonstrate that DevAll reproduces state-of-the-art MAS across three representative tasks at competitive performance and without task-specific orchestration code, highlighting its effectiveness as a general-purpose platform for LLM-based MAS. DevAll is available at https://github.com/OpenBMB/ChatDev.

</details>


### [307] [A Closed-Loop Evaluation of Capability Loss and Recovery in Compressed Driving Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00718)
*Ahmad Alfan Alfian Irfan,Nur Ahmad Khatim,Mansur Arief*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一个分阶段闭环评估方法，在Gym-Duckietown中将自动驾驶策略（由PPO训练的信念状态策略）逐阶段压缩（剪枝→蒸馏→量化），并在五个驾驶课程上评估，发现结构化剪枝是最先导致驾驶能力丧失的阶段，蒸馏只能有限恢复，而整数量化在剪枝后的模型上会丢失需要停车再启动的课程，但在未剪枝模型上则能保留全部课程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 汽车和出行公司将学习型驾驶策略部署在资源有限的嵌入式计算机上，常用剪枝、知识蒸馏和量化来减小模型规模和推理成本，但这些压缩方法的评价通常依赖聚合数值指标，而数值指标无法反映策略在真实道路交互中的安全驾驶能力，因此需要更贴近闭环驾驶任务的评估方法。

Method: 将驾驶任务建模为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP），使用近端策略优化（PPO）在Gym-Duckietown环境中训练信念状态策略；然后提取actor网络，逐阶段依次进行结构化剪枝、知识蒸馏和整数量化，并在五个驾驶课程上进行闭环评估，逐级追踪每一步压缩对驾驶能力的影响。

Result: 结构化剪枝是最先导致驾驶能力丧失的阶段；蒸馏能改善剪枝后的actor，但改善受限于其rehearsal数据；整数量化会丢失需要车辆停车再启动的课程；有趣的是，同一量化流程应用于未剪枝actor时能保留全部五个课程。

Conclusion: 不同压缩阶段对驾驶性能的影响具有非对称性和交互效应，压缩策略的接受与否不能仅依赖聚合数值指标，必须通过分阶段闭环评估在具体驾驶任务中验证，才能实现安全且统计可靠的自动驾驶部署。

Abstract: Many automobile and mobility companies deploy learned driving policies on embedded computers with limited memory and power. Pruning, knowledge distillation, and quantization are the standard methods to reduce the size and the inference cost of these policies. However, these methods are commonly assessed by aggregate numerical scores, and such scores may not reflect the ability of the policy to drive safely when interacting with other road users. In this study, we propose a stage-wise closed-loop evaluation approach to follow a driving policy through a compression pipeline. We formulate the driving task as a partially observable Markov decision process (POMDP) and train a belief-state policy with proximal policy optimization (PPO) in Gym-Duckietown. We then extract the actor, compress it one stage at a time, and evaluate it on five driving curricula. We show that structured pruning is the stage at which the driving capability is first lost. Meanwhile, distillation improves the pruned actor, but the improvement is limited by its rehearsal data. Integer quantization of the improved actor loses some of the curricula that require the vehicle to stop and then resume. Interestingly, the same procedure on the unpruned actor preserves all five curricula. Our study thus provides an empirical analysis aiming to answer the currently active discussions on how to accept a compressed driving policy, so as to achieve a safe and statistically reliable deployment of automated driving functions.

</details>


### [308] [SOVER: Formal Certification of Optimization Reformulations via LLM-Assisted SMT Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00728)
*Swapnil Bhattacharyya,Mayank Baranwal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SOVER是一个基于LLM辅助的SMT验证框架，用于正式认证数学优化问题在不同建模语言间转换的语义等价性。该框架通过Z3和dReal分别处理混合整数线性与连续非线性公式，并引入NLEquiv-150基准（100个等价对+50个困难的非等价对），在LLM提取映射后达到99.33%的分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在跨建模语言翻译/重构优化问题时，仅依赖经验性求解器执行来验证转换结果并不可靠——求解器可能受局部最小值、结构超时、数值伪影及公式间微妙语义分歧影响，缺乏形式化的等价性保证。

Method: SOVER框架将语义映射与正式认证分离：(1) LLM用于提取两个公式间的语义映射关系；(2) Z3检查混合整数线性公式的跨域可行性（domain cross-feasibility）和全局目标序保持（global objective-order preservation）；(3) dReal为连续非线性公式提供容忍感知的可行性/范围检查以及ε-argmin验证。同时构建NLEquiv-150基准集用于评估。

Result: 在LLM提取的映射条件下，SOVER正确分类149/150对（99.33%），包括全部50个困难负样本；唯一的错误源于映射提取不完整。

Conclusion: SOVER通过分离语义映射与自动定理证明的两阶段策略，有效弥补了纯求解器验证的不可靠性，为优化问题跨语言变换提供了可扩展的形式化认证框架。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown remarkable promise in translating and reformulating complex mathematical optimization problems across modeling languages. However, validating such transformations through empirical solver executions alone is unreliable, as solver outcomes may be affected by local minima, structural timeouts, numerical artifacts, and subtle semantic divergence between formulations. We introduce SOVER, an LLM-assisted SMT framework that separates semantic mapping from formal certification: Z3 checks domain cross-feasibility and global objective-order preservation for mixed-integer linear formulations, while dReal provides tolerance-aware feasibility/range and $ε$-argmin checks for continuous nonlinear formulations. We also introduce NLEquiv-150, a public benchmark of 100 equivalent and 50 deliberately hard non-equivalent nonlinear reformulation pairs. With LLM-extracted mappings, SOVER classifies 149/150 pairs (99.33%) correctly, including all 50 hard negatives; the sole error is an incomplete mapping extraction.

</details>


### [309] [Agentic Empirical Asset Pricing: Methodological Foundations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00731)
*Yingjian Pan,Xiaowei Ding,Kay Giesecke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"智能代理实证资产定价"(AEAP)新范式，指能够自主完成科学发现全过程的LLM智能体系统。论文聚焦因子发现任务，提出参考架构与严格的因子评估标准，并采用滚动重执行方法对发现系统本身进行样本外回测。以SEADS为实例，在两组美国股票面板上与五个基线系统对比，发现没有单一指标能一致排名，需多维评估；同时揭示若干负面结果与评估陷阱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体在资产定价中的应用已有进展，但评估实践仅回测系统产出的结果(因子或交易)，而非生成这些结果的自主发现系统本身，缺乏对发现系统可靠性、可重复性的系统性评估框架与标准。

Method: 1) 定义AEAP范式及核心构建模块；2) 提出参考架构，聚焦于因子发现任务；3) 建立严格的已发现因子评估标准；4) 提出对发现系统进行样本外回测的方法(滚动重执行)；5) 以SEADS为具体实例，在两组美国股票面板上，依据新评估标准与五个重实现的基线系统对比，并进行多维评估与滚动重执行验证。

Result: 没有任何单一指标能对系统进行一致排名，表明需要同时在多个维度上评估；滚动重执行回答了"发现过程本身是否可靠"的补充性问题；同时报告了负面发现和局限性，揭示了AEAP系统未来评估中存在的更多陷阱。

Conclusion: AEAP为资产定价研究开辟了新范式，但需要从"回测输出"转向"回测发现系统"的评估理念。多维度同时评估与过程级样本外回测是必要的，且公开负面结果有助于建立更严谨的未来评估标准。

Abstract: Recent advances in LLM agents enable a new paradigm for asset pricing, which we call Agentic Empirical Asset Pricing (AEAP): systems that autonomously conduct the scientific discovery process itself. We define AEAP and identify its core building blocks. Existing evaluation practices backtest only the outputs (factors or trades), not the autonomous discovery system that produced them. We focus on factor discovery, contributing a reference architecture, a rigorous evaluation standard for discovered factors, and a method for out-of-sample backtesting the discovery system. As a concrete instance of that architecture, we evaluate SEADS against five re-implemented baselines on two US equity panels using this standard: no single metric ranks the systems consistently, motivating evaluation on multiple axes at once. A separate rolling re-execution then asks the complementary question of whether the discovery process itself, not one static output, is reliable. We also report negative findings and limitations that surface further evaluation pitfalls for future AEAP systems.

</details>


### [310] [Escaping Redundant Reasoning: Structure-Aware Search for Inference-Time LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00738)
*Lu Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BASIN，一种无需训练、结构感知的选择方法，用于缓解大语言模型推理时搜索中的"推理盆地坍缩"问题——即搜索过度集中于结构或语义相似的轨迹。BASIN将推理状态分组为盆地并惩罚对同一策略的重复访问，从而在固定计算预算下将搜索重新分配到真正不同的推理路径上。在相同推理预算下，BASIN相比ToT在Game of 24上提升高达+22pp，在MuSR上提升+6.7pp。此外，QA-BASIN变体通过保留高质量盆地进一步提升鲁棒性，并引入冗余间隙Δ来理论化盆地感知选择的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的推理时搜索常常集中于一小部分结构或语义相似的轨迹，导致大量多样性不足的搜索浪费计算资源，而真正不同的替代方案未被充分探索，作者将此失败模式命名为"推理盆地坍缩"。

Method: 提出BASIN：一种训练无关（training-free）、结构感知的选择方法。其核心思想是将推理状态按结构或语义特征分组为盆地，对访问同一盆地的行为施加惩罚，从而在固定计算预算下迫使搜索重新分配至截然不同的推理路径。同时提出质量感知变体QA-BASIN，在无条件多样化可能导致过度探索时保留高质量盆地，平衡探索与利用。并引入冗余间隙Δ作为衡量正确与错误预测上搜索集中度差异的指标。

Result: 在匹配的推理预算下，BASIN相较Tree of Thoughts（ToT）在Game of 24基准上提升高达+22个百分点，在MuSR基准上提升+6.7个百分点。QA-BASIN进一步提升了鲁棒性。冗余间隙分析显示，标准ToT通常在Δ≈0附近运行，而BASIN持续将Δ推向正值，证实其有效缓解了盆地坍缩。

Conclusion: 结构感知选择（structure-aware selection）是一种简单且通用的提升推理时推理质量的方法。BASIN作为训练无关方法可广泛适用于现有推理框架，代码已开源。

Abstract: Inference-time search with large language models (LLMs) often concentrates on a small set of structurally or semantically similar trajectories, leaving alternatives underexplored---a failure mode we call \textit{reasoning basin collapse}. We introduce BASIN, a training-free, structure-aware selection method that groups reasoning states into basins and penalizes repeated visits to the same strategy, thereby reallocating search across genuinely distinct reasoning paths under a fixed compute budget. Under matched inference budgets, BASIN improves over Tree of Thoughts (ToT) by up to $+22$pp on Game of 24 and $+6.7$pp on MuSR. A quality-aware variant, QA-BASIN, further improves robustness by preserving high-quality basins when unconditional diversification over-explores. To explain when basin-aware selection helps, we introduce the redundancy gap $Δ$, which measures how differently search concentrates for correct versus incorrect predictions: standard ToT often operates near $Δ\approx 0$, while BASIN consistently shifts $Δ$ positive. More broadly, BASIN suggests structure-aware selection as a simple and general approach to improving inference-time reasoning. Code can be found at https://github.com/GitHubLuCheng/basin.

</details>


### [311] [ContextPipe: Database-Inspired Context Assembly for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00749)
*Peng Xu,Zuyu Zhang,Yuze Sun,Feng Tian,Long Wang,Chen Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ContextPipe，一个将LLM代理的上下文组装问题视为关系数据库查询执行的多阶段流水线框架（Plan-Bind-Optimize-Execute-Feedback），支持可审计、可重放和故障隔离的上下文管理。初步实验（SWE-bench Pro Qutebrowser子集）表明，相比append-only策略，ContextPipe在token总量上减少31%、LLM调用数减少23%、响应时间减少9%，代价是KV缓存命中率有所降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视界LLM代理需要在硬性上下文窗口预算和字节敏感的提示缓存约束下，决定每个提示中应包含哪些上下文、以何种顺序组装以及何时压缩历史。然而，在真实生产系统中，这种上下文组装逻辑散落在提示构建器、临时压缩例程、缓存中断变通方案和各家提供商适配器中，缺乏统一的系统化处理范式。

Method: 作者将上下文组装类比为关系数据库中的查询执行，二者均在硬预算下运行、利用分层缓存并依赖统计信息。据此设计ContextPipe，包含五个阶段：Plan（规划）、Bind（绑定）、Optimize（优化）、Execute（执行）、Feedback（反馈），并配备结构化数据源目录、确定性缓存感知优化器以及EXPLAIN ANALYZE追踪工具。

Result: 在SWE-bench Pro的Qutebrowser子集上，与append-only上下文构建策略相比：总token量减少31%，LLM调用减少23%，响应时间降低9%；但KV缓存命中率下降，即优化用户级token成本是以缓存效率为代价的。

Conclusion: 将数据库查询执行的系统化原则引入LLM代理的上下文组装，能够带来可观的成本与延迟收益，同时赋予上下文管理可审计性、可重放性和故障隔离能力。未来需进一步权衡缓存命中率与token成本的矛盾，并扩展到更大规模的验证。

Abstract: Long-horizon large language model (LLM) agents require context assembly: the runtime must decide what to include in each prompt, in what order, and when to compact history under a hard context-window budget and a byte-sensitive prompt cache. In production agentic systems, this logic is scattered across prompt builders, ad hoc compaction routines, cache-break workarounds, and per-provider shims. We argue that context assembly is structurally isomorphic to query execution in a relational database: both execute under a hard budget, exploit a tiered cache, and leverage statistics. We adopt this discipline in ContextPipe: a five-phase pipeline (Plan Bind Optimize Execute Feedback) backed by a structured data-source catalog, a deterministic cache-aware optimizer, and an EXPLAIN ANALYZE trace. We show that context in ContextPipe is auditable, replayable, and failure-isolated. A preliminary evaluation using the SWE-bench Pro Qutebrowser subset shows that, compared with the append-only context construction policy, ContextPipe reduces total token volume by 31%, LLM calls by 23%, and response time by 9%, at the cost of a lower KV cache-hit ratio.

</details>


### [312] [S^3martCirc: Self-supervised Smart Circuit Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00755)
*Wendy Zheng,Yinhan He,Liang Wu,Jundong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出S^3martCirc，一个统一框架，通过抽象节点行为为两种通用计算角色并定义定量指标，同时进行电路发现与功能解释，优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机制可解释性（MI）方法对LLM采用两阶段范式：先电路发现（识别重要组件），再功能解释（确定角色）。但这种顺序化方法忽略了组件重要性与功能角色之间的相互依赖关系。此外，功能角色常绑定于特定节点难以泛化，且依赖主观解释而非可量化指标。

Method: 提出S^3martCirc（自监督智能电路发现）框架，将节点行为抽象为两种跨任务泛化的通用计算角色，并定义可量化的指标来分配这些角色，从而实现重要性与功能角色的联合发现（而非顺序发现）。

Result: 大量实验表明，S^3martCirc在电路发现任务上优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过将电路发现与功能解释统一为联合优化过程，S^3martCirc克服了两阶段范式的局限性，证明了组件重要性与功能性角色的内在一致性可以更好地指导电路发现。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable performance across diverse tasks, from text summarization to question answering. Despite these capabilities, their black-box nature obscures internal decision-making processes. Mechanistic interpretability (MI) aims to address this by reverse-engineering neural networks into human-understandable algorithms. Current MI approaches for LLMs typically follow a two-stage paradigm: first identifying important components (circuit discovery), where components are typically individual nodes such as an attention head or feedforward neuron, and second determining the role they play in a certain task (functional interpretation). However, this sequential approach overlooks a fundamental insight: a component's importance and its functional role are inherently codependent. Unifying these stages presents two key challenges: (1) functional roles are often tied to specific nodes or components, limiting generalization, and (2) their identification relies on subjective interpretation rather than quantifiable metrics. To address these challenges, we propose S^3martCirc (Self-supervised Smart Circuit Discovery), a unified framework that simultaneously discovers circuits and interprets functionality. S^3martCirc abstracts node behavior into two general computational roles that generalize across tasks and defines a quantitative metric for assigning them, enabling importance and functional role to be discovered jointly rather than in sequence. Extensive experiments show that our framework outperforms existing methods in circuit discovery.

</details>


### [313] [Automated Tree Knowledge Graph Construction using Ontology Expansion and Retrieval from Vietnamese History Textbooks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00763)
*Ket Doan Nguyen,Minh N. H. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个端到端流水线，用于越南语低资源环境下的层级知识图谱构建与检索策略评估。KG构建采用三阶段混合关系抽取流水线（Union-Find批内去重、近似跨批搜索、带质心过滤的LLM抽取及五步双LLM验证器），并设计双层架构（不可合并结构节点+可合并内容节点）。在越南高中历史教科书构建的树状知识图谱（约400页，750节点，4,341语义边，本体仅从40扩至41类）上，评估了Top-Down、Horizontal、Bottom-Up三种图遍历策略。实验表明Top-Down策略在NDCG@10上比向量基线高4.7个百分点，但结构信息在查询无需结构上下文时反而降低性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有层级知识图谱RAG存在两大挑战：(i)缺乏利用本体扩展实现低资源语言（如越南语）自动KG构建的方法；(ii)缺乏针对层级结构的知识检索策略的系统性评估。作者旨在填补这两项空白，验证树状结构信息在RAG中的实际价值。

Method: 提出端到端流水线：(1) KG构建采用三阶段混合关系抽取：批内Union-Find去重、近似跨批搜索合并、LLM抽取结合质心过滤器减少prompt数量并配以五步双LLM验证器防止本体膨胀；(2) 采用双层架构区分不可合并的结构节点与可合并的内容节点；(3) 检索评估设计三种图遍历策略（Top-Down、Horizontal、Bottom-Up），在由109个子图生成的1,210个越南语查询基准上，覆盖五种查询方向进行分类评估。

Result: 在近400页的越南高中历史教科书上构建出750个节点和4,341条语义边的树状知识图谱，本体从40类受控增长至41类。Top-Down策略在NDCG@10上比向量基线提升4.7个百分点。但结果显示，当查询不需要结构上下文时，引入结构信息反而会降低检索性能。

Conclusion: 树状结构信息能为RAG提供超越扁平余弦相似度的额外价值，但其有效性取决于查询本身是否需要结构上下文。这一发现提示未来的层级KG-RAG系统应能自适应判断是否需要利用结构信息，而非对所有查询一律施加图遍历策略。

Abstract: Hierarchical Knowledge graph (KG)-based retrieval augmented generation (RAG) has emerged as a powerful approach for supporting large language models with structured knowledge. However, there are primary challenges: (i) the lack of methods for automatic KG construction using ontology expansion for low-resource languages such as Vietnamese, (ii) the absence of systematic evaluation for knowledge retrieval strategies leveraging the hierarchical structures. In this paper, we propose an end-to-end pipeline for KG construction and retrieval strategies evaluation. In the KG construction, we employ a three-phase hybrid relation extraction pipeline: intra-batch deduplication via Union-Find, approximate cross-batch search, and LLM extraction with a centroid filter that reduces prompts combined with a five-step dual-LLM validator to prevent bloated ontology. A two-tier architecture consists of unmergeable structural nodes to preserve the document structure and mergeable content nodes. The retrieval evaluation consists of three graph traversal strategies: Top-Down, Horizontal, and Bottom-Up, which are evaluated on a synthetically generated benchmark of 1,210 Vietnamese queries from 109 subgraphs, categorized by five query directions. In this paper, we construct the tree knowledge graph from Vietnamese high school History textbooks (nearly 400 pages) to produce 750 nodes and 4,341 semantic edges with controlled ontology growth from 40 to 41 types. Among experimental graph traversal strategies, the Top-Down strategy with structure surpasses the vector baseline by 4.7 percentage points in NDCG@10. As a result, tree-structural information provides valuable information beyond flat cosine similarity but degrades performance when the query does not require structural context.

</details>


### [314] [DiagEvo: Diagnosis-Guided Self-Evolution via Hierarchical Error Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00768)
*Xincheng Wei,Yifan Ding,Yoshua Li,Dongsheng Ma,Rongxiang Weng,Xunliang Cai,Wenjian Ding,Yao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DiagEvo框架，通过从求解器自身失败历史中提取并记忆重复的错误原因来指导自博弈课程方向，无需外部任务资源。诊断器维护一个分层错误原因记忆，将相关原因分组到技能节点并用自一致性追踪掌握状态，挑战者据此平衡定向生成与自由探索，配合双置信度过滤保留高确定性中等难度问题，在九个基准上全面超越所有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型自我进化的自博弈范式在没有引导时会出现平台期或性能下降。现有无引导方法仅以难度、可学性或多样性等信号驱动问题生成，无法指定后续轮次应针对哪些未解决的推理弱点；而有引导方法依赖外部任务资源（人类样例、文档语料、难度目标），在自博弈循环之外引入信息。研究者希望证明所需方向可仅从求解器自身失败历史中获取，从而消除对外部资源的依赖。

Method: DiagEvo包含三个核心组件：(1) 诊断器（diagnostician）从求解器的失败历史中提取重复出现的错误原因，并存入分层错误原因记忆（hierarchical error-cause memory），该记忆将相关原因分组到技能节点下，并根据在定向问题上的自一致性标签将每种原因标记为Active或Mastered；(2) 挑战者（challenger）利用这些状态与复发次数在目标定向生成和自由探索之间取得平衡，形成自适应的课程；(3) 双置信度过滤（double-confidence filtering）仅保留中等难度问题中最常见求解答案具有明确投票领先优势的问题，确保问题质量。

Result: 在三个求解器（Qwen3-4B、Qwen3-8B、OctoThinker-8B）上，使用默认4B诊断器的DiagEvo在全部九个基准上平均准确率均优于所有基线。在Qwen3-8B上，五个数学推理基准的平均准确率达到72.3%，比R-Zero高出4.5个百分点；全部九个基准的平均准确率为57.4%，比DARC高出1.1个百分点。消融实验证实分层错误原因记忆与双置信度过滤都贡献了性能提升。

Conclusion: 求解器自身的失败历史可以替代外部任务资源提供自博弈所需的方向引导。通过诊断器提取错误原因并维护分层记忆，结合挑战者的定向生成与双置信度问题过滤，DiagEvo实现了无需外部资源的自博弈自我进化，并在多个基准上持续超越现有方法，验证了该范式的有效性与通用性。

Abstract: Self-play is an effective paradigm for language-model self-evolution, but without guidance, solver performance can plateau or decline across rounds. Unguided methods steer question generation with signals such as difficulty, learnability, or diversity. These signals keep questions challenging and varied but do not specify which unresolved reasoning weaknesses later rounds should target. Guided methods obtain direction from external task resources, including human examples, document corpora, or specified difficulty targets, and therefore rely on task information supplied outside the self-play loop. We show that the needed direction can instead be derived from the solver's own failure history. We introduce DiagEvo, whose diagnostician extracts recurring error causes from this history and stores them in a hierarchical error-cause memory. The memory groups related causes under skill nodes and tracks each as Active or Mastered according to self-consistency on targeted questions. The challenger uses these states and recurrence counts to balance cause-targeted generation with free exploration. Double-confidence filtering retains intermediate-difficulty questions only when the most common solver answer has a clear vote lead. DiagEvo derives its curriculum from information produced during self-play, without external task resources. With the default 4B diagnostician, DiagEvo outperforms every baseline in mean accuracy across all nine benchmarks for each of the three solvers: Qwen3-4B, Qwen3-8B, and OctoThinker-8B. On Qwen3-8B, it reaches 72.3% mean accuracy across five mathematical reasoning benchmarks, 4.5 percentage points above R-Zero. Its mean accuracy across all nine benchmarks is 57.4%, 1.1 percentage points above DARC. Ablations show that the hierarchical error-cause memory and double-confidence filtering both contribute to these gains.

</details>


### [315] [When Features Become Instances: Inverted Contrastive Learning for Unsupervised Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00782)
*Utsab Ghosh,Roshni Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ICLFS，一种基于倒置对比学习的无监督特征选择方法。该方法将数据矩阵转置使每个特征由其样本向量表示，构造掩码正视图和打乱的负视图，在投影空间中用InfoNCE目标学习特征表示，并以嵌入幅度作为显著性信号排序特征，再用拉普拉斯门控校正去除局部冗余特征。在12个基准数据集上，标准聚类评估协议下10个数据集取得最优聚类精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督特征选择缺乏类别标签，特征效用难以直接定义。现有方法依赖间接结构准则（如相似性保持、局部性、稀疏性、聚类几何或重构质量），缺乏统一的表征一致性视角。因此需要探索UFS作为表征学习问题的新范式。

Method: 1) 倒置数据矩阵，使每个特征由其样本画像向量表示；2) 构建多个掩码正视图与一个打乱负视图，在投影空间中用InfoNCE目标学习对结构化扰动一致的特征表示；3) 利用投影空间嵌入幅度（基于余弦与InfoNCE训练对嵌入范数影响的发现）作为特征排名信号；4) 通过Laplacian-Gated Ranking Correction（拉普拉斯门控排序校正）抑制局部冗余候选并保留重要特征。

Result: 在12个基准数据集上，ICLFS在标准聚类评估协议下于10个数据集取得最优聚类精度（超越经典与神经网络基线），在其他2个数据集中保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 特征维度的对比表征一致性为基于邻域、聚类和重构的UFS方法提供了一种强而有效的替代方案，表明UFS可被重新构想为特征空间中的表征学习问题。

Abstract: Unsupervised feature selection seeks a compact subset of informative features without access to class labels, making feature utility difficult to define. Existing UFS methods therefore rely on indirect structural criteria, such as similarity preservation, locality, sparsity, cluster geometry, or reconstruction quality. In this paper, we instead study UFS through representation consistency and propose Inverted Contrastive Learning for Unsupervised Feature Selection (ICLFS), a feature-wise contrastive framework that reformulates UFS as a representation learning problem over features rather than samples. ICLFS first inverts the data matrix so that each feature is represented by its sample-profile vector, then constructs multiple masked positive views together with a shuffled negative view, and learns projector-space representations that remain consistent across these structured perturbations under an InfoNCE-based objective. Motivated by recent findings that cosine-based and InfoNCE-based training affect embedding norms, we use projector-space embedding magnitude as the saliency signal for ranking features. The resulting norm-based ranking is subsequently refined through Laplacian-Gated Ranking Correction, which suppresses locally redundant candidates while preserving salient ones. Extensive experiments on 12 benchmark datasets show that ICLFS achieves the best clustering accuracy on 10 datasets against both classical and neural baselines under the standard clustering-based UFS evaluation protocol, while remaining competitive on the other two. These results show that feature-wise contrastive representation consistency provides a strong and effective alternative to neighborhood, cluster, and reconstruction-based UFS formulations.

</details>


### [316] [StudyBench: Can Self-Evolution Squeeze Textbooks for Olympiad Capability?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00787)
*Yinghao Chen,Zixi Chen,Bingxiang He,Ziqing Qiao,Huan-ang Gao,Yinuo Xu,Yuxin Zuo,Zeyuan Liu,Yuhao Zhan,Chaojun Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出StudyBench，一个受控的物理学基准，用于直接衡量自我进化方法将训练材料转化为可迁移解题能力的效率。测试集分为应用集（吸收能力）和迁移集（迁移能力）。实验发现：应用集上的改进很少能迁移到更难的迁移集；存在“指导差距”（Guidance Gap）——“计算高原”（Compute Plateau），说明剩余差距是方法问题而非数据或计算问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理想的自我进化方法应像人类一样，仅从少量优质教科书自学即可掌握学科并解决最难的问题。但当前缺乏一个直接度量方法如何高效地将原始训练材料转化为可迁移解题能力的基准，因此需要设计一个可控的基准来量化这一过程。

Method: 构建StudyBench受控物理基准：组织测试集为两个子集——Application Set（难的教科书级问题，评估吸收能力）和Transfer Set（奥林匹克级问题，评估迁移能力）。在三个基础模型上对多种代表性自我进化方法进行基准测试，并通过指导消融实验（guidance ablation）分析能力差距的来源。

Result: 应用集上的性能提升很少迁移到更难迁移集；指导消融暴露了“指导差距”，即使最强方法也只能闭合相同材料作为上下文指导时解锁能力的一小部分；所有方法都遭遇“计算高原”，在耗尽计算预算前就达到饱和。

Conclusion: 剩余的性能差距本质上是方法问题，而非数据或计算问题。StudyBench提供了一个干净可控的基准，将自我进化研究从开放式的追求转变为可量化的目标。代码已开源。

Abstract: Humans need to study only a handful of well-written textbooks to master a discipline and attempt its hardest problems. We argue that an ideal self-evolution method should share the same property, that is autonomously learning from raw training material for transferable problem-solving capability. However, we still lack a direct measurement for it. We introduce StudyBench, a controlled physics benchmark that directly measures how efficiently a self-evolution method converts training material into capability. We organise the test set into an Application Set, consisting of difficult textbook problems and evaluating absorption ability, and a Transfer Set, consisting of olympiad-level problems and evaluating transfer ability. Benchmarking representative self-evolution methods across three base models, we find that improvements on the Application Set rarely translate to the harder Transfer Set. A guidance ablation exposes a Guidance Gap: even the strongest method closes only a small fraction of what the same material unlocks when supplied as in-context guidance. Besides, every method hits a Compute Plateau, saturating well before exhausting its compute budget. The remaining gap is therefore a method problem rather than a data or compute problem. By offering a clean and controlled benchmark, StudyBench turns self-evolution progress from an open-ended pursuit into a measurable target for future research. Our code is released at https://github.com/thunlp/StudyBench.

</details>


### [317] [Towards a Reliable and Practical Eval Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00805)
*Emma Thuong Nguyen,Abhishek Ghose*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个端到端的大语言模型（LLM）评估流水线，将评估清单创建与基于学习的聚合方法相结合，以提升多个LLM法官之间的一致性以及相对人类判断的准确性，同时提供自一致性、解释和预测不确定性等实用特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的软件系统在开发生命周期中越来越需要有效的"评估器"（evals）作为质量门禁。然而，现有工作通常只解决评估可靠性的某个单一维度，而非完整的实际需求集合，缺乏一个系统性的端到端解决方案。

Method: 提出一个端到端的评估流水线，包含两个核心组件：（1）评估清单创建（eval checklist creation），用于生成结构化的评估标准；（2）针对清单响应的学习型聚合（learned aggregation），用于融合多个LLM法官的输出。此外还引入自一致性、解释机制和预测不确定性估计。

Result: 通过实证实验展示了该框架的有效性，具体表现为在LLM法官之间的一致性（agreement）和与人类判断的准确性（accuracy）方面均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 该端到端评估流水线能够全面满足LLM软件系统开发中评估环节的实际需求，为评估可靠性的多维度问题提供了统一的解决方案。

Abstract: LLM-based software systems increasingly require effective "evals" as quality gates in the development lifecycle. However, existing work typically addresses individual aspects of eval reliability rather than the full set of practical requirements. We present an end-to-end eval pipeline that combines eval checklist creation, with learned aggregation for checklist responses, to improve agreement across LLM judges and accuracy against human judgments. The framework additionally pro- vides self-consistency, explanations, and prediction uncertainty, and we empirically demonstrate its effectiveness.

</details>


### [318] [One Policy, Any Budget: Internalizing Budget-Aware Search via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00813)
*Xiaowei Sun,Jin Li,Yili Hong,Yikun Fu,Yanghua Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AnySearch提出了一种训练框架，使单个LLM搜索策略能够在任意预算约束下进行自适应搜索，通过两阶段训练（先注入预算状态与结构化推理提示，再移除支架进行自主适应学习）和复合奖励（结合准确率与预算效率）实现跨预算尺度的泛化。在七个QA基准上超越基线，并推广到训练范围之外的未见约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的LLM搜索代理在固定预算下训练，无法在部署时适应变化的预算约束（如查询次数限制或token成本上限）。这限制了搜索智能体在真实场景中的灵活性与可用性。

Method: AnySearch采用两阶段训练范式：(1) 第一阶段引入显式预算状态注入与结构化推理提示，在线性衰减预算下引导智能体进行高效资源分配；(2) 第二阶段移除训练支架，在自适应采样的预算约束下让智能体自主学习，匹配推理条件。两阶段均使用复合奖励函数，通过绝对和相对信号耦合并结合准确率提升与预算效率，并采用自适应权重设计——对高准确率查询放大效率信号，对低准确率查询减弱效率信号。

Result: 在7个通用及多跳QA基准上的广泛实验表明，该方法在所有预算规模下均优于基线，能够泛化到超出训练范围的未见预算约束，并在不产生过量token开销的情况下实现了优越的工具生产力。

Conclusion: AnySearch证明了通过课程强化学习与支架式训练，可以使单一LLM搜索策略具备跨任意预算约束的自适应能力，为部署场景中预算动态变化的LLM工具调用智能体提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: While reinforcement learning has enabled LLM-based search agents to invoke external tools, existing methods train under fixed budgets and cannot adapt when constraints vary at deployment. We propose AnySearch, a framework that enables a single policy to perform budget-aware search under any budget constraint through a training scaffold and curriculum reinforcement learning. In the first phase, we train the agent with explicit budget state injection and structured reasoning prompts that guide efficient allocation under linearly decaying budgets. In the second phase, the scaffold is removed and the agent learns to operate autonomously under adaptively sampled budget constraints, matching inference conditions. Both phases are optimized with a composite reward that couples answer accuracy with budget efficiency through absolute and relative signals, where an adaptive weight amplifies the efficiency signal for high-accuracy queries and attenuates it for low-accuracy ones. Extensive experiments on seven general and multi-hop QA benchmarks show that our method outperforms baselines across all budget scales, generalizes to unseen constraints beyond the training range, and achieves superior tool productivity without excessive token overhead. Our code is available at https://github.com/xwsun01/AnySearch.

</details>


### [319] [AnalysisBank: An Expert Analysis Pattern Library for Financial Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00818)
*Yajing Yang,Yunshan Ma,Kelvin J. L. Koa,Min-Yen Kan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 AnalysisBank，一种在分析层面而非结构层面生成金融报告的方法。它将专家报告提炼为可复用的"分析"库，每个分析包含数据信号、分析动作和来源专家文本片段。推理时通过匹配输入信号检索库中条目并应用动作来撰写报告。在550篇专家报告上蒸馏发现信号类型呈47-52种的重尾分布覆盖13种动作类型。在四个LLM骨干的两个金融基准上，AnalysisBank将新颖、基于数据洞见的比例提高了1.7-3.7倍，且该方法可泛化到科学写作领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融报告生成方法在结构层面运作，即以高层的主题或章节为单位组织内容，但作者认为这脱离了报告生成的核心——应基于数据推导出的洞见进行分析级的内容组织与创作，从而实现更深入的、由数据驱动的财务分析。

Method: 通过两个关键阶段操作：(1) 蒸馏阶段：从专家报告中提取"分析"（Analysis），每一分析细分为数据信号（data signal）、分析动作（analytical move）及对应的专家文本片段，构建可复用库；(2) 推理阶段：将输入信号匹配到库中条目，检索并应用对应的分析动作组合生成报告内容。作者从550篇专家报告中蒸馏出47-52种信号类型和13种动作类型，分布呈重尾特征。

Result: 在两个金融基准和四个LLM骨干上，AnalysisBank相比结构性级别的基线方法，使新颖且基于数据洞见的生成内容比例提升1.7-3.7倍。此外，将方法迁移到科学写作场景也验证了该区别方案的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 金融报告生成应优先在分析层面（数据信号+分析动作）而非结构层面（主题/章节）组织内容，这有助于提高报告洞见的新颖性和数据根植性。相关代码和蒸馏的分析库已开源。

Abstract: We argue that financial report generation should operate at the analytical rather than structural level, composing content from data-derived insights rather than high-level topics or sections. To this end, we propose AnalysisBank, which distills expert reports into a reusable library of Analyses, each pairing a data signal, an analytical move, and the expert span it was derived from. At inference time, AnalysisBank matches input signals to library entries and applies the retrieved moves to compose the report. A study of Analyses distilled from 550 expert reports reveals a heavy-tailed distribution of 47-52 signal types spanning 13 move types. On two financial benchmarks across four LLM backbones, AnalysisBank increases the proportion of novel, data-grounded insights by 1.7-3.7x over structural-level baselines. Transfer to scientific writing suggests that the distinction generalizes beyond finance. Code and the distilled Analysis library are available at https://github.com/yajingyang/AnalysisBank.

</details>


### [320] [Polished but Unresolved: Identifying Late-Stage Pressure States in Long-Horizon Tool-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00823)
*Haoyang Chen,Yi Liu,Jianzhi Shao,Xiaozhou Xu,Zhe Sun,Wei Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究长时程工具使用智能体在"后期压力状态"下的早提交问题。作者先训练线性探针证明该压力状态可从隐藏状态中识别，再利用激活干预验证压力方向可影响智能体是否继续工具使用或提前提交。通过受控上下文操作发现压力可通过约束清晰度和动作映射来缓解。基于此提出PSPR插件方法，在中等压力下施加轻量压力缓解方向，在高风险下采用结构化组织策略。实验表明该方法能持续增强多种智能体方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程工具使用智能体不仅需要搜索和规划，还需要决定何时结束任务。晚期压力状态下，智能体倾向于提交看似完整但其实关键约束未解决的答案。理解并干预这种状态对提升智能体可靠性至关重要。

Method: 1) 训练线性探针来识别智能体隐藏状态中的压力表征；2) 利用激活干预沿压力方向调整隐藏状态，观察对提交/继续行为的影响；3) 通过受控上下文操纵分析约束清晰度和动作映射对压力缓解的作用；4) 提出PSPR插件：在中等压力下施加轻量压力缓解方向，在高压力风险下切换为结构化组织策略。

Result: 在多个长时程基准上的实验表明，PSPR能持续增强现有智能体方法的性能。

Conclusion: 压力状态可从隐藏状态中检测并可通过激活干预进行调节；约束清晰度和动作映射能有效缓解压力；PSPR插件方法能够通用地增强已有智能体方法在长时程任务上的表现。

Abstract: Long-horizon tool-use agents need not only to search and plan, but also to decide when to finalize. We study late-stage pressure states, in which an agent is biased toward submitting a final answer that appears complete and polished while key constraints remain unresolved. We first train a linear probe to show that this pressure state is identifiable from the agent's hidden states. Then, we use activation interventions along this pressure direction and find that shifting the hidden states changes both the pressure score and whether the agent continues tool use or submits early. Through controlled context manipulations, we further see that the pressure is mitigated by constraint clarity and action mapping. Based on these findings, we propose Probe-Sensed Pressure Relief (PSPR), a plugin that applies lightweight pressure relief direction under moderate pressure and moves to structured organization under high pressure risk. Experiments on multiple long-horizon benchmarks show that our method consistently strengthens existing agent methods.

</details>


### [321] [FLaG: Frequency-Domain Latent-attention Gated Pooling for Token Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00831)
*Kewei Li,Rongying Zhang,Xueli Wang,Xiwen Gong,Zhongjian Wang,Qiuchen Zhao,Lan Huang,Ruochi Zhang,Fengfeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出FLaG（频域潜在注意力门控池化），一种即插即用的聚合模块，将编码器输出重表达为傅里叶域后执行最终池化。在AMP活性预测（ESM2）、图像分类（ResNet18）及RoBERTa语言任务上验证，FLaG在多数指标上取得最佳结果，表明其在不同模态间提供了可迁移的频域聚合偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有token聚合（池化）方法大多仅在原始token空间中操作，忽略了频域中可能存在的结构化和判别性信息。作者旨在引入一种频域聚合机制，以利用傅里叶域的非冗余频谱表征，提升跨模态（蛋白、视觉、文本）下游任务的性能。

Method: FLaG首先对编码器输出执行rFFT，用拼接的实部与虚部分量表示非冗余频谱；通过可学习的潜在查询（latent queries）对频谱token进行汇总；推导样本条件通道门（sample-conditioned channel gate）；重建被调制的token表示以用于下游聚合。整个模块作为plug-in接入现有架构。

Result: 跨四个AMP骨干-物种设置，FLaG在macro-Spearman、RMSE和Recall@50上取得最佳；在CIFAR-10上取得最高top-1准确率；在七个语言指标中五个获得最佳均值（但STSBenchmark上均值池化仍最强）。机制分析显示AMP侧多数编码器层为低频敏感预测，最终层高频相对敏感性上升，且有肽特异性位置响应；残差门普遍放大频谱通道但保持低频主导能量分布，潜在交叉注意力呈现样本/物种特异频谱分配。

Conclusion: FLaG提供了一种可迁移的频域聚合偏置，适用于蛋白、视觉和文本表示，但其收益取决于具体骨干模型和下游任务。代码和数据已开源。

Abstract: Token aggregation converts token-level representations into fixed-dimensional sample representations, but most pooling methods operate only in the original token space. We introduce Frequency-Domain Latent-attention Gated Pooling (FLaG), a plug-in aggregation module that re-expresses encoder outputs in the Fourier domain before final pooling. FLaG represents the nonredundant rFFT spectrum through concatenated real and imaginary components, summarizes spectral tokens with learnable latent queries, derives a sample-conditioned channel gate, and reconstructs modulated token representations for downstream aggregation. We evaluate the same architecture across ESM2-based antimicrobial peptide (AMP) activity prediction, ResNet18 image classification on CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100, and three RoBERTa-based language tasks. FLaG achieves the best macro-averaged Spearman correlation coefficient, RMSE, and Recall@50 across four AMP backbone-species settings and the highest top-1 accuracy on CIFAR 10. It also achieves the best mean results on five of seven language metrics, although mean pooling remains strongest on STSBenchmark. AMP-side mechanistic analyses reveal low-frequency prediction sensitivity across most encoder layers, with increased relative high-frequency sensitivity in the final layer, and pronounced peptide-specific positional responses. The residual gate broadly amplifies spectral channels while preserving the low-frequency-dominated energy profile, whereas latent cross-attention exhibits sample- and species-specific spectral allocation. Overall, FLaG provides a transferable frequency-domain aggregation bias across protein, visual, and textual representations, with benefits that depend on the backbone and downstream task. Supplementary materials, source code, and data are available at https://www.healthinformaticslab.org/supp/ and https://github.com/Kewei2023/AMPCliff/tree/FLaG.

</details>


### [322] [Towards Generalizable Visually Grounded Exploration of Household Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00845)
*Linhao Zheng,Zeming Liu,Wangke Chen,Li Zeng,Wanxiang Che,Heyan Huang,Yuhang Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出VGEBench基准，用于评估视觉-语言模型（VLM）在通用视觉锚定探索（Generalizable Visually Grounded Exploration）方面的能力。不同于依赖静态数据集和人工标注轨迹的现有基准，VGEBench基于逻辑驱动状态机（Logic-Driven State Machine）框架，模拟多轮交互循环，迫使智能体通过主动视觉感知和反馈驱动的修正来达成目标。实验表明现有VLM在将语义知识转化为物理执行及维持长时程状态追踪方面面临显著挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的具身探索范式严重依赖人类标注轨迹的模仿学习，限制了智能体的泛化能力。通用自主具身智能体的核心瓶颈在于通用视觉锚定探索——无需手册或专门训练即能主动将抽象世界知识锚定到细粒度视觉可供性上。然而现有基准通常依赖显式文档和标注轨迹，忽略了功能设备操作中必不可少的动态假设-交互-修正过程，无法评估这一关键能力。

Method: 作者构建了逻辑驱动状态机（Logic-Driven State Machine）框架的VGEBench基准。该框架不依赖静态数据集，而是模拟多轮交互循环，强制智能体通过主动视觉感知和反馈驱动修正实现目标，从而系统评估VLM在视觉锚定探索中的表现。

Result: 实验结果表明，现有VLM在将语义知识转化为物理执行操作以及维持长时程状态追踪方面面临显著挑战，暴露了当前模型在通用视觉锚定探索能力上的明显不足。

Conclusion: VGEBench填补了现有基准在评估具身智能体通用视觉锚定探索能力方面的空白，揭示了VLM从高层语义推理到物理世界执行之间的鸿沟，为该领域未来的模型设计和发展提供了重要的评估工具和方向指引。

Abstract: Recent advancements in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated impressive capabilities in static visual recognition and high-level semantic reasoning. However, current embodied exploration paradigms still heavily rely on imitation learning from human-annotated trajectories, which severely limits agents' generalization ability. The key bottleneck of realizing general autonomous embodied agents lies in Generalizable Visually Grounded Exploration: the ability to operate novel devices without manuals or specific training by actively grounding abstract world knowledge into fine-grained visual affordances. Yet, existing benchmarks fail to evaluate this capability: they generally rely on explicit documents and annotated trajectories, neglecting the dynamic Hypothesis-Interaction-Refinement process essential for functional device operation. To bridge this gap, we introduce VGEBench, a comprehensive benchmark designed to evaluate the generalizable visually grounded exploration capabilities of VLMs. Unlike static datasets, we construct a Logic-Driven State Machine framework. This framework simulates multi-turn interaction loops, compelling agents to achieve goals by active visual perception and feedback-driven correction. Experimental results demonstrate that existing VLMs face significant challenges in translating semantic knowledge into physical execution and maintaining long-horizon state tracking.

</details>


### [323] [Verifiable Disaster Storylines and Causal Knowledge Graphs: A Citation-Grounded Pipeline from Heterogeneous Humanitarian Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00858)
*Ivan Decostanzi,Michele Ronco,Sergio Consoli,Christina Corbane,Lorenzo Bertolini,Indaco Biazzo,Daria Mihaila,Manuel Garcia-Herranz,Felix Schwebel,Yelena Mejova,Kyriaki Kalimeri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合结构化灾难记录（EM-DAT）与非结构化文档（ReliefWeb和EMM）的流水线，利用检索增强生成（RAG）技术生成有来源依据的灾难故事线（storylines）和因果知识图谱（causal knowledge graphs），支持响应者和分析人员的情境感知。系统在三个多样化危机用例上通过9名领域专家和9名非专家的评估，验证了高检索精度和对因果关系的忠实提取，专家明显偏好有引文依据的组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人道主义响应需要快速综合异质、高容量的信息源，但这一任务在危机发生的关键最初几小时内常常超出人类分析能力，因此需要自动化系统来辅助灾难信息的情境感知与因果分析。

Method: 该流水线融合三大多样化数据源（EM-DAT结构化数据、ReliefWeb与EMM非结构化文档），采用检索增强生成（RAG）技术：1) 提取结构化故事线——覆盖17个字段的表格化事件档案（从严重程度、关键驱动因素到儿童敏感影响指标）；2) 构建因果知识图谱，其中每个节点和边均附有引文依据的解释性叙述，确保完全可追溯到原始来源。

Result: 在三个多样化危机用例上进行了包含9名领域专家和9名非专家的类人评估。结果表明：检索精度高、因果关系的提取忠实性强，且专家明确偏好有引文依据的组件而非无依据的替代方案。

Conclusion: 该流水线设计为可扩展至完整的EM-DAT目录，目标是公开发布一个经叙述增强（narrative-enriched）的数据库版本，以促进更广泛的人道主义响应与灾害分析应用。

Abstract: Effective humanitarian response depends on the rapid synthesis of heterogeneous, high-volume information sources - a task that routinely exceeds human analytical capacity in the critical early hours of a crisis. We present a pipeline that combines structured disaster records from EM-DAT with unstructured documents from ReliefWeb and the European Media Monitor (EMM) to produce source-grounded disaster storylines and causal knowledge graphs supporting situational awareness for responders and analysts. Using Retrieval-Augmented Generation, the pipeline extracts structured storylines - tabular event profiles covering 17 fields, from severity and key drivers to child-sensitive impact indicators - and constructs causal knowledge graphs where each node and edge is enriched with citation-grounded explanatory narratives, enabling full traceability back to primary sources. We evaluate the system on three diverse crisis use cases through a human evaluation involving 9 domain expert and 9 non-expert evaluators. Results confirm high retrieval precision, strong faithfulness of extracted causal relations, and a clear expert preference for citation-grounded components over ungrounded alternatives. The pipeline is designed to scale to the full EM-DAT catalogue, with the goal of publicly releasing a narrative-enriched version of the database.

</details>


### [324] [Reinforcement Learning Enhanced LLM Agents for Complex Vehicle Routing Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00859)
*Yi Chen,Zikang Yu,Jiahai Wang,Jinbiao Chen,Jianpeng Zhou,Zizhen Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RLEA（Reinforcement Learning Enhanced LLM Agents），一个多智能体框架，用于自动化构建复杂的车辆路径问题（VRP）优化模型。RLEA利用基于Soft Q-learning训练的轻量级神经Planner来编排LLM智能体的行动，并配备进化记忆模块和检索增强生成技术，使智能体能够利用累积经验和外部求解器知识来生成和优化程序。在48种不同VRP变体上的实验表明，RLEA优于先前最先进方法，成功率提高16.67%，同时显著减少运行时错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高级优化求解器虽然能有效解决VRP问题，但为复杂VRP变体建模需要大量领域专业知识，这限制了先进优化技术的可及性。作者旨在降低VRP建模的技术门槛，使非专家用户也能高效利用优化技术。

Method: RLEA包含三个核心组件：(1) 基于Soft Q-learning训练的轻量级神经Planner，用于高效编排LLM智能体的行动；(2) LLM-based agents负责程序生成与精化；(3) 进化记忆模块和检索增强生成技术，使智能体能够利用累积经验与外部求解器知识。

Result: 在48种不同的VRP变体上评估，RLEA相较先前最先进方法在成功率上提升16.67%，并显著降低运行时错误，验证了强化学习与LLM推理相结合对自动化优化建模的有效性。

Conclusion: 将强化学习与基于LLM的推理结合，是实现自动化优化建模的高效途径，RLEA成功拓展了高级求解器在复杂VRP问题上的可访问性。

Abstract: Vehicle Routing Problems (VRPs) are fundamental combinatorial optimization problems with widespread applications in various scenarios. The advanced optimization solvers can effectively solve such problems. However, modeling complex VRP variants for solvers often requires substantial domain expertise, which limits the accessibility of advanced optimization technologies. In this paper, we propose Reinforcement Learning Enhanced LLMAgents(RLEA), a multi-agent framework designed to automate the modeling of complex VRPs. RLEA introduces a lightweight neural Planner trained with Soft Q-learning to efficiently orchestrate the actions of LLM-based agents. In addition, we equip the system with an evolutionary memory module and retrieval-augmented generation, enabling the agent to leverage both accumulated experience and external solver knowledge during program generation and refinement for solving VRPs. We evaluated 48 distinct VRP variants across various solvers. The experimental results demonstrate that RLEA outperforms the previous state-of-the-ar method, achieving a 16.67% higher success rate while significantly reducing runtime errors. These results validate that integrating reinforcement learning with LLM-based reasoning is highly effective for automated optimization modeling. The appendix is available at: https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.19134435.

</details>


### [325] [Beyond the Clock: Measuring the Value of Adaptive Revision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00874)
*Ayushi Chadha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究分层智能体系统中"元层控制"（何时保留或替换下层过程的策略）问题。在固定的检查点上，学习到的修订时机策略虽然在不同随机种子下产生截然不同的行为分布（从确定性时钟到状态条件调度），但均无法超越最优强制时机策略。通过干预研究、穷举枚举和反事实PERSIST/REPLAN诊断，作者分离了"状态依赖性"与"决策价值"：控制器的行为可以随内部状态变化，但这种变化未必转化为可复现的任务性能收益。结果表明，应沿三个独立轴评估元层控制：分数是否依赖状态、该依赖是否改变实际行为、这些改变是否超越强非自适应策略捕获结果价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能体系统日益复合化，越来越多的重要决策从任务执行本身上升到元层：高层控制器何时应保留引导另一流程的策略，何时应修订它？现有研究缺乏对这一元层控制问题的系统分析，特别是未能区分控制器的"行为变化"与"决策价值"之间的差异。

Method: 在分层潜在推理器（hierarchical latent reasoner）上设定实验，其管理者可以保留或替换支配低层计算的承诺。使用三个预提交的训练种子学习修订时机的策略，并与最强的强制时机（forced timing）策略在相同冻结检查点上比较。此外，在原检查点上进行干预研究（验证时序的敏感性与顺序依赖性）、穷举枚举（扫描所有固定调度），以及反事实PERSIST/REPLAN诊断以剖析分数证据的误导性来源。

Result: （1）学习到的修订时机在不同种子下产生质性不同的策略，从近似确定性的早期时钟到高度状态条件的调度分布，但均未超越最优强制时机策略；（2）干预研究表明时机本身具有后果性和顺序敏感性；（3）穷举枚举显示在给定的决策预算下，一个强的固定调度即可捕获大部分可测得的时机价值；（4）反事实诊断揭示当预测性由决策位置而非位置内判别主导时，分数级证据具有误导性。

Conclusion: 学习到的元层控制应沿三个独立轴进行评估：分数是否依赖于状态、该依赖是否改变实际行为、以及这些行为改变是否能在强非自适应策略之外捕获结果价值。该研究提供了"状态依赖≠决策价值"的实证分离框架，为未来元层控制系统的评价标准和方法论提供了基础。

Abstract: As agentic systems become compound systems, increasingly important decisions move above task execution itself: when should a higher-level controller preserve the strategy guiding another process, and when should it revise it? We study this meta-level control problem in a hierarchical latent reasoner whose manager can retain or replace a commitment governing lower-level computation. Across three precommitted training seeds, learned revision timing produces qualitatively different policies, ranging from an almost deterministic early clock to substantially more state conditioned schedule distributions, yet none outperforms the best forced timing policy evaluated on the same frozen checkpoint. This separates state dependence from decision value: a controller can vary its actions with internal state without turning that variation into a reproducible task-performance benefit. A deeper intervention study on the original checkpoint shows that timing itself is consequential and order-sensitive, while exhaustive enumeration reveals that a strong fixed schedule captures most of the measurable value available from timing at this decision budget. Counterfactual PERSIST/REPLAN diagnostics further show why score-level evidence can be misleading when predictability is dominated by decision position rather than within-position discrimination. Together, these results argue that learned meta-level control should be evaluated along three separate axes: whether its score depends on state, whether that dependence changes realized behavior, and whether those changes capture outcome value beyond a strong non-adaptive policy.

</details>


### [326] [FractalNet-Based Heterogeneous Federated Learning for Orbital Edge Intelligence in Satellite Mega-Constellations: A Wildfire Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00875)
*Sai Puppala,Koushik Sinha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于FractalNet架构的异构联邦学习方法，用于卫星巨型星座的轨道边缘智能，通过分布式路径调度器按SWAP-C约束、星际联系窗口和训练统计分配模型深度，并采用三层代理控制平面管理在轨调度、异常升级和策略治理。野火检测案例研究表明不同轨道层自然学习不同级别的语义情境感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星巨型星座正成为大规模感测、通信和计算的基础设施，但其学习架构仍沿用地面联邦学习和以地面为中心的任务操作范式，与卫星在尺寸、重量、功率和成本(SWAP-C)、辐射耐受性、链路可用性和传播延迟方面相差多个数量级的特性不匹配。

Method: 提出异构联邦学习方法：(1)形式化接触窗口约束下的深度异构联邦优化问题；(2)引入分布式路径调度器，根据SWAP-C约束、预测的星际联系和训练统计信息分配模型深度；(3)每个层级周期性池化更新而非每次接触机会都进行，以减少消息开销和能耗；(4)构建三层代理控制平面管理在轨调度、异常升级和策略治理。

Result: 在模拟巨型星座上的野火检测案例中验证了方法在不同轨道层学习不同语义级别（LEO学习像素级热异常，MEO学习区域火线动态，GEO/HEO学习大规模风险传播），并从收敛性、通信效率、能量适应性、调度池化节省、鲁棒性和延迟等维度进行了实验验证。

Conclusion: 所提出的FractalNet异构联邦学习方法能够有效适应卫星巨型星座的异构特性和以联系窗口为核心的通信约束，实现了轨道边缘智能的高效协作学习。

Abstract: Satellite mega-constellations are emerging as large-scale sensing, communication, and computation fabrics, yet their learning architectures remain largely inherited from terrestrial federated learning and ground-centric mission operations--- ill-suited to satellites that differ by orders of magnitude in Size, Weight, Power, and Cost (SWAP-C), radiation tolerance, link availability, and propagation delay. We propose a heterogeneous federated learning method based on the FractalNet architecture for orbital edge intelligence. We formalize contact-window-constrained, depth-heterogeneous federated optimization and introduce a distributed path scheduler that assigns model depth as a function of SWAP-C constraints, predicted inter-satellite contacts, and training statistics. To reduce message overhead and energy consumption, each tier pools updates periodically rather than at every contact opportunity, and a three-tier agentic control plane governs in-space scheduling, anomaly escalation, and policy-governed autonomy. As a case study, we apply the framework to wildfire detection, where each orbital shell naturally learns a different semantic level of situational awareness: pixel-scale thermal anomalies at low Earth orbit (LEO), regional fire-front dynamics at medium Earth orbit (MEO), and larger-scale risk propagation at geostationary or high Earth orbit (GEO/HEO). Experiments on simulated mega-constellations validate the approach across convergence, communication efficiency, energy adaptation, scheduled-pooling savings, robustness, and latency.

</details>


### [327] [Towards reliable multimodal disaster severity assessment through preference optimization and explainable vision-language reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00879)
*Yuanjun Zhang,Fuzel Ahamed Shaik,Suvojit Acharjee,Fahad Khalid,Mourad Oussalah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段训练框架，结合监督微调（SFT）和直接偏好优化（DPO），在统一数据构建流水线中生成两个互补数据集（ReasoningSet和PreferenceSet），同时提升灾害多模态模型的分类准确率和解释质量。实验表明SFT将准确率从73.64%提升至78.29%，Macro-F1提升29%，解释质量提升约25%；DPO进一步增强了可解释性，并在InternVL-3-8B和LLaVA-1.5-7B上验证了泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灾害损害评估需要既准确又可解释的模型，但现有多模态方法受限于标注数据稀缺以及对推理质量评估不足，无法为应急管理提供可信且可审计的决策支持。

Method: 提出两阶段训练框架：(1) 单一Human-in-the-Loop (HITL) 标注流程生成两类互补数据集——ReasoningSet（含验证过的推理链，用于SFT）和PreferenceSet（含配对的推理链，用于DPO对齐）；(2) 先通过SFT提升基础性能，再通过DPO优化推理解释与人类判断的对齐；评估维度涵盖自动指标、基于模型的评分及人工排序。

Result: SFT使准确率从73.64%提升至78.29%，Macro-F1较基线提升29%，解释质量提升约25%；DPO在PreferenceSet上进一步改善可解释性。跨模型验证（InternVL-3-8B和LLaVA-1.5-7B）证明方法具有鲁棒性和泛化性，有效改善轻度损坏案例的检测并降低高风险误分类。

Conclusion: 该框架为构建可靠多模态灾害评估系统提供了可复现的路径，增强了模型推理与人类判断的一致性，有望为应急管理部门输出可审计、可操作的灾害洞察。

Abstract: Reliable disaster damage assessment requires models that provide both accurate predictions and transparent explanations. However, existing multimodal approaches are limited by scarce annotated data and insufficient evaluation of reasoning quality. This study proposes a two-stage training framework that integrates Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) within a unified data construction pipeline. From a single Human-in-the-Loop (HITL) annotation workflow, two complementary datasets are derived, namely ReasoningSet, which contains validated rationales for SFT, and PreferenceSet, which comprises paired rationales for DPO-based alignment. The framework evaluates both classification performance and explanation quality using automatic metrics, model-based scoring, and human ranking. Experimental results show that SFT improves accuracy from 73.64% to 78.29% and increases Macro-F1 by 29% compared to the baseline, while explanation quality improves by approximately 25%. Subsequent DPO alignment further enhances interpretability on the PreferenceSet. Cross-model validation on InternVL-3-8B and LLaVA-1.5-7B demonstrates the robustness and generalizability of the approach. The proposed framework improves detection of underrepresented mild damage cases, reduces high-risk misclassifications, and strengthens alignment between model reasoning and human judgment. Overall, it provides a reproducible pathway to develop reliable multimodal systems that deliver auditable, actionable disaster insights for emergency management.

</details>


### [328] [Denoising Diffusion Generative Models Secretly Calculate Attentions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00885)
*Farzan Haddadi,Leila Monfared,Ebrahim Rezaii,Mohammadreza Malek-Mohammadi,Pejman Zakalvand,Narges Mokhtari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了扩散模型与Transformer的注意力机制之间的内在等价性，表明注意力机制源于通用的训练目标，并在扩散模型和Transformer中自然涌现。同时指出自编码器与注意力模型在基本功能原理上具有相似性，基于这些等价关系，作者将扩散框架重新表述为简化的基于注意力的图像生成算法，以更少的计算资源实现可比的生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在图像生成中占据主导地位，而Transformer架构（依赖注意力机制）则在自然语言处理中占主导。这两类看似完全不同的架构体系是否需要各自独立发展？作者希望探索它们之间的深层联系，从而为模型设计提供统一的视角和灵活的互换可能。

Method: 本文首先在理论上证明扩散模型内在地使用了与Transformer极为相似的注意力机制，表明注意力是基于通用训练目标涌现的普适机器学习原理。其次，论证了自编码器与基于注意力的模型在基本功能原理上的相似性。基于这些理论等价关系，将扩散框架重新表述为基于注意力机制的简化算法，用于图像生成。

Result: 基于注意力机制的简化图像生成实现能够在显著减少训练时间和计算资源的情况下，达到与原始扩散模型可比的生成性能。

Conclusion: 注意力机制是一种基于通用训练目标而涌现的普适机器学习原理，扩散模型与Transformer共享其内在机制。这些结构之间的等价性使得架构可以根据实际需求互换，例如以注意力替代扩散过程来降低计算开销，同时保持较好的生成质量。

Abstract: Denoising diffusion models are the dominant architecture for image generation, whereas most natural language generation and modeling are primarily handled by well-known transformer architectures employing attention mechanism. Here, we show that diffusion models also inherently use an attention mechanism very similar to that of transformers. Therefore, attention emerges as a universal machine learning principle, based on a general training objective. We also show similarities in basic functional principle of auto-encoders and attention-based models. These equivalences allows us to interchange these designs based on practical requirements. As an example, we can reformulate the diffusion framework to reduce the lengthy training process and computation-intensive image generation. Using this approach, a simplified algorithm is proposed for image generation which is based on attention mechanism. Results show that the attention-based implementation achieves comparable performance with significantly less effort and computational resources.

</details>


### [329] [CacheBridge: Efficient Cross-Model KV Cache Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00891)
*Xingyu Qu,Siyuan Lu,Zhiyu Chen,Sheng Wang,Tao Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CacheBridge框架，用于多模型系统中大语言模型（LLM）之间的跨模型KV缓存迁移。它通过协同设计架构索引支持、注意力对齐校准和有界映射器构建，克服了现有全头映射（Full-Head Mapping）方案对架构差异敏感、存储和计算开销随层数增长的问题，在不损失精度的前提下大幅提升效率和降低存储成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多模型系统中，接收模型需要重放共享前缀以构建其专属KV缓存，造成重复计算开销。虽然已有闭式跨模型KV迁移方案（全头映射）避免了重放，但其设计将每个目标KV头与源模型中选定层的所有KV头关联，导致映射质量对架构差异敏感，且映射器的存储和应用成本随层支持规模线性增长，限制了其可扩展性。

Method: CacheBridge通过三个关键设计解决上述问题：（1）架构索引映射器支持——将每个目标头限制为仅映射到一个匹配的源头，而非所有源头；（2）注意力对齐校准——根据因果注意力敏感度对重建误差进行加权，使校准更聚焦于对输出影响更大的位置；（3）有界映射器构建——使用融合GPU核函数计算加权充分统计量，避免物化完整观测张量。整体仍采用闭式仿射映射接口以支持在线部署。

Result: 在三个迁移方向上，CacheBridge恢复了Full-Head Mapping在Ministral 3两个迁移方向上损失的大量精度，同时在Qwen3上保持了99.83%的平均目标保持率。在Qwen3 14B→32B迁移中，映射器存储减少8倍，应用速度最高提升3.0倍，仅需十分之一的校准数据即可匹配Full-Head的精度，并将500序列的构建时间从92.63秒降至8.63秒（10.7倍加速）。

Conclusion: CacheBridge通过"一个目标头对应一个源头"的稀疏匹配策略、注意力感知加权校准以及高效的有界映射器构建，在保持闭式仿射映射接口的同时，显著降低跨模型KV迁移的存储与计算开销，提升精度对架构差异的鲁棒性，为多LLM系统中高效上下文共享提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Sharing context between LLMs in a multi-model system requires the receiving model to prefill the shared prefix because KV caches are model-specific. Recent closed-form cross-model KV transfer, hereafter Full-Head Mapping, avoids this replay by fitting a training-free affine mapper from source to target caches. However, its full-head design maps each target KV head from every source KV head in the selected layers, making transfer quality sensitive to architectural differences and causing mapper storage and application cost to grow with layer support. To this end, we introduce CacheBridge, which co-designs architecture-indexed mapper support, attention-aligned calibration, and bounded mapper construction while retaining a closed-form affine interface for online deployment. CacheBridge restricts each target head to a matched source head, weights reconstruction errors by causal attention sensitivity, and uses a fused GPU kernel to construct weighted sufficient statistics without materializing full observation tensors. Across three transfer directions, CacheBridge recovers the two Ministral 3 transfer directions where Full-Head Mapping loses substantial accuracy while preserving 99.83\% mean target retention on Qwen3. On Qwen3 $14\mathrm{B}\to32\mathrm{B}$, it reduces mapper storage by $8\times$, accelerates application by up to $3.0\times$, matches \fullhead with one tenth of the calibration data, and reduces 500-sequence construction from 92.63 to 8.63 seconds ($10.7\times$).

</details>


### [330] [CARE: Contrastive Anchor-based Rubric Evolution for Large Language Model Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00892)
*Siyuan Li,Xinxin Song,Chen Ruinian,Jingjing Fan,Tingxiong Xiao,Yangen Hu,Ke Zeng,Jinli Suo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CARE（对比锚点基的评测标准演化）是一种基于评分标准的强化学习方法，通过在每次训练步骤中将最高得分rollout与前沿模型生成的锚点响应进行对比，实现自适应的修复奖励误设定和主动的追逐前沿质量差距的双分支机制，在高奖励区域维持判别精度，解决了静态评分标准被利用和现有动态方法的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于评分标准的强化学习（Rubric-based RL）优于可验证奖励的强化学习，适合开放任务的后训练。但静态评分标准会随策略演化被攻击（hack），而现有动态方法存在三个问题：评分标准提取无方向性、黑客检测不可靠、评分标准无界增殖。需要一个能持续锚定高质量方向且可扩展的评分标准演化方法。

Method: 每一训练步骤将最高打分rollout与条件于提示和当前评分标准的前沿模型生成的锚点响应进行对比。对比产生两个互补分支：(1) Adaptive分支——反应性修复奖励误设定；(2) Chase分支——主动将前沿级质量差距转化为更锐利的评分标准。两个分支共同在高奖励区域维持判别精度。

Result: 在WildChecklist-9K上使用Qwen2.5-7B-Base和Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct训练，CARE在Arena-Hard-2.0、InfoBench和FollowBench上达到SOTA；是唯一在300训练步中相对GPT-4.1锚点响应的胜率持续提升的方法；在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct和Qwen3-8B上的结果进一步表明CARE跨模型家族泛化。

Conclusion: CARE通过锚定高质量响应来引导评分标准演化，同时兼顾反应性修复与主动性追逐，有效在高奖励区域维持判别精度，实现跨模型家族的稳定性能提升，是对对现有动态评分标准方法的关键改进。

Abstract: Rubric-based reinforcement learning decomposes open-ended instructions into prompt-specific, flexible rubrics, making it better suited than reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards for post-training LLMs on open-ended tasks. However, static rubrics are inevitably hacked as the policy evolves, and existing dynamic approaches introduce new problems: undirected rubric extraction, unreliable hack detection, and unbounded rubric proliferation. We propose $\textbf{CARE}$ ($\textbf{C}$ontrastive $\textbf{A}$nchor-based $\textbf{R}$ubric $\textbf{E}$volution), which grounds every rubric evolution step in a high-quality anchor response generated by a frontier model conditioned on the prompt and its rubrics. At each training step, CARE contrasts the highest-scoring rollout against the anchor, enabling two complementary mechanisms: an Adaptive branch that reactively repairs reward misspecification; and a Chase branch that proactively converts frontier-level quality gaps into sharper rubrics. Together, the two branches $\textbf{maintain discriminative accuracy in the high-reward region}$---the precise region where reward over-optimization mostly originates. Experiments on WildChecklist-9K with Qwen2.5-7B-Base and Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct show that CARE achieves state-of-the-art performance on Arena-Hard-2.0, InfoBench, and FollowBench, and is the $\textbf{only}$ method whose win rate against GPT-4.1 anchor responses shows sustained improvement throughout 300 training steps; additional results on Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct and Qwen3-8B further indicate that CARE generalizes across model families.

</details>


### [331] [In-Context Neurofeedback: Can LLMs Control Their Internal Representations through Privileged Access?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00904)
*Koshiro Aoki,Ryota Takatsuki,Gouki Minegishi,Yusuke Haruki,Daisuke Kawahara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文重新设计了针对大语言模型的神经反馈范式，使控制目标满足"特权访问"要求（第三方无法从提示中推断），在该更严格设置下模型无法可靠控制特权内部表征，表明以往研究声称的内部控制可能依赖表层机制而非真正的内部访问。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究通过神经反馈声称LLMs可以控制自身内部表征，但其控制目标并非特权的（第三方可从提示推断），因此无法排除模型依赖表层机制的可能性。为严格评估LLMs的机器元认知能力，需要设计满足特权访问要求的评估范式。

Method: 重新设计神经反馈范式，使控制目标满足与人类认知神经科学中神经反馈实验一致的特权访问要求，即第三方无法从提示直接推断出控制目标，在此更严格条件下测试模型对特权内部表征的控制能力。

Result: 在满足特权访问要求的更严格设置下，模型未能表现出对特权内部表征的可靠控制，说明先前研究报告的控制结果无法排除依赖表层机制的可能性。

Conclusion: 要严格评估LLMs的元认知能力，必须采用要求特权访问的评估方法，否则研究结论可能被表层机制所混淆。

Abstract: Whether large language models (LLMs) can control their own internal representations matters for both machine metacognition and AI safety. A recent study applied neurofeedback to LLMs and claimed that they can control their internal representations. However, the reported control may rely on superficial mechanisms rather than genuine internal access because the control targets in that study are not privileged, meaning that a third party can infer them from the prompt. We redesign the neurofeedback paradigm for LLMs so that the control target satisfies the privileged access requirement, which is closer to neurofeedback experiments in human cognitive neuroscience. Under this stricter setting, the models do not demonstrate reliable control over privileged internal representations, suggesting that previously reported control cannot exclude the possibility that it relies on superficial mechanisms. Our results indicate that rigorous assessments of metacognition in LLMs require evaluation methods that demand privileged access.

</details>


### [332] [RPCBench: A Benchmark for Proactive Premise Critique in LLM-based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00918)
*Zhongru Chen,Yuan Wu,Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RPCBench，一个用于评估大语言模型在推荐场景中检测、诊断和处理虚假前提能力的基准。基准涵盖5个推荐域和10种前提失败类型，设计了细粒度评估框架衡量主动检测、错误定位、后检测处理策略和证据忠实度。对11个LLM的系统评估发现主动检测是主要瓶颈，欠明确前提错误表现最差，中等推理长度效果最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推荐基准主要评估排名、生成或偏好满足，缺乏对推荐请求中错误前提的检测能力评估；已有错误检测基准通常不基于推荐特有的用户和候选证据。为填补这一空白，需要构建一个证据驱动的推荐前提批判基准。

Method: 构建RPCBench基准，包含来自5个推荐域的基于证据的测试实例，覆盖10种前提失败类型。每个实例提供可见的推荐上下文和损坏的用户查询。设计细粒度评估框架，从主动检测、错误定位、后检测处理策略和证据忠实度四个维度评估。

Result: 对11个LLM的系统评估显示：主动检测是推荐前提批判的主要瓶颈；模型在欠明确前提错误上表现最差；目标关键信息密度比冗余证据更重要；推理长度与批判质量呈非线性关系，在中等推理长度达到最优，过长的推理伴随过度思考惩罚。

Conclusion: RPCBench揭示了当前LLM在推荐前提批判任务上的关键局限，指出主动检测能力不足是最核心的瓶颈，且模型对欠明确前提的错误处理能力最弱。该研究对提升推荐助手的可靠性和安全交互具有重要意义。
