Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as interactive recommender assistants. Their evaluation should therefore go beyond plausible item recommendation and test whether they can recognize flawed recommendation requests. Existing recommender benchmarks mainly assess ranking, generation, or preference satisfaction, while existing error-detection benchmarks are usually not grounded in recommendation-specific user and candidate evidence. To address this gap, we introduce RPCBench, a benchmark for evaluating Recommender-Premise Critique: the ability to detect, diagnose, and properly handle faulty premises in natural-language recommendation requests. RPCBench contains evidence-grounded test instances from five recommendation domains and covers ten types of premise failures. Each instance provides a visible recommendation context and a corrupted user query. We further design a fine-grained evaluation framework that measures proactive detection, error localization, post-detection handling strategy, and evidence faithfulness. Through a systematic evaluation of 11 LLMs, we find that proactive detection is the main bottleneck in Recommender-Premise Critique, and models perform worst on underspecified-premise errors. We also observe that target-critical information density matters more than redundant evidence, and that longer reasoning does not monotonically improve critique quality: performance peaks at intermediate reasoning length, while overly long reasoning is accompanied by an overthinking penalty. The code is available at https://github.com/ZhongruChen/RPCBench.

</details>


### [333] [VIBE-Bench: Evaluating Personalized Large Language Models When Profiles Don't Mean Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00921)
*Yiwen Jiang,Yang Deng,Stephanie Fong,Zimu Wang,Yaling Shen,Wei Feng,Hongxi Yang,Xiangyu Zhao,Zhongxing Xu,Deval Mehta,Xuelian Cheng,Zongyuan Ge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出现有PLLM个性化基准大多假设偏好可从语义相关的历史中检索，但实际中存在"画像-偏好概念错位"(PRCM)这一未充分探索但重要的场景，即用户的画像线索与查询特定偏好位于不同概念空间，导致语义检索无法有效个性化。作者提出VIBE-Bench基准，包含两个基于心理学的任务、3,504个画像和12,239个对话（含人工验证的黄金测试集），要求跨概念偏好推理。实验表明当前PLLM主要依赖浅层语义相关性，无法获得稳健的跨概念映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PLLM个性化基准存在一个关键假设缺陷：它们假设用户的查询相关偏好可以通过语义相关的历史记录检索得到。然而在实际场景中，用户可观察的画像特征（如职业、背景）与其特定查询下的偏好（如对回复风格的喜好）往往位于不同概念空间，语义检索在这种"概念错位"情况下无法可靠地进行个性化。这一问题此前未被系统研究，因此需要专门的基准和方法探索。

Method: 作者提出VIBE-Bench，一个以心理学为理论基础的基准测试集，包含两个任务、3,504个人物画像（persona）和12,239个对话，并附带人工验证的黄金测试集。该基准设计用于测试模型在画像线索与查询偏好之间存在概念错位时，能否进行超越表面语义重叠的跨概念偏好推理。

Result: 对多种个性化方法的实验表明，当前的PLLM在很大程度上依赖浅层语义相关性进行个性化，在面对画像与偏好概念空间不一致的情况时，未能建立稳健的跨概念映射，导致个性化效果明显下降。

Conclusion: PRCM被确立为PLLMs中一个独立的失败模式（failure regime），VIBE-Bench为推进超越语义匹配的偏好推理研究提供了一个聚焦的测试平台，有助于推动未来的个性化模型向更深层的推理能力发展。

Abstract: Personalized Large Language Models (PLLMs) aim to tailor responses to individual users, where a central challenge is preference reasoning: inferring query-relevant preferences from user-related history. Existing benchmarks, however, largely assume that such preference can be retrieved from semantically related history. We study an underexplored but practically important regime, profile-preference conceptual misalignment (PRCM), where observable profile cues and query-specific preferences lie in different concept spaces, making semantic retrieval inconsistent for personalization. We introduce VIBE-Bench, a benchmark with two psychology-grounded tasks, 3,504 personas and 12,239 dialogues, including a manually verified gold test set, and requires cross-concept preference reasoning beyond surface semantic overlap. Experiments with several personalization methods show that current PLLMs largely rely on shallow semantic correlations and fail to acquire robust cross-concept mappings. These findings establish PRCM as a distinct failure regime in PLLMs and position VIBE-Bench as a focused testbed for advancing preference reasoning beyond semantic matching.

</details>


### [334] [Few-Shot Out of Domain Intent Detection with Covariance Corrected Mahalanobis Distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00961)
*Jayasimha Talur,Oleg Smirnov,Paul Missault*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究对话代理中的域外（OOD）意图检测问题。作者分析了Podolskiy等人（2021）提出的Mahalanobis距离方法在少样本设置下性能不佳的原因，并提出了一种协方差校正的Mahalanobis距离用于OOD意图检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的OOD意图检测方法（如基于Mahalanobis距离的方法）在标准设置下表现优异，但在实际中重要的少样本（few-shot）场景下无法超越基线方法，因此需要分析性能低下的原因并提出改进方案。

Method: 作者首先分析Mahalanobis距离方法在少样本设置下性能低下的原因，然后提出一种协方差校正的Mahalanobis距离（covariance corrected Mahalanobis distance）用于检测域外意图。

Result: 论文表明所提出的协方差校正Mahalanobis距离方法在少样本OOD意图检测任务中有效改进了性能。

Conclusion: 通过分析少样本场景下Mahalanobis距离方法失效的原因，并引入协方差校正机制，所提出方法成功地提升了少样本OOD意图检测的性能。

Abstract: Conversational agents like chatbots and voice assistants are trained to understand and respond to user intents. On encountering an utterance with an intent different from the ones they have been trained on, these agents are expected to classify the intent as `unknown' or `out of domain'. This problem is known as out of domain (OOD) intent detection. Podolskiy et al. (2021), showed that Mahalanobis distance can be used effectively for identifying OOD intents, outperforming competing approaches. However, their method fails to outperform the baselines in the practically important few-shot setting. In this paper we analyze the reason for low performance and propose a covariance corrected Mahalanobis distance for detecting out-of-domain intents.

</details>


### [335] [CoBRA: Learning Tool-Use Boundaries via Counterfactual Margins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00967)
*Wenhao Zou,Xianglong Liu,Wendong Bi,Hanjie Wang,Simin Zhao,Gong Zhi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CoBRA是一个反事实边界学习框架，用于决定大语言模型何时调用外部工具。它通过构建同基座模型的内部与外部专家，收集配对轨迹，估计使用/不使用工具之间的奖励边际，将数据划分为内偏、外偏和模糊三种情况；利用清晰边际样本进行边界感知冷启动SFT，再通过MARS-RL（参考分裂轨迹与反事实边际优势）优化边界决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具调用触发方法主要依赖绝对信号（如难度、置信度、最终任务奖励），缺乏对实例级工具使用边际收益的显式估计，导致不必要的工具调用（引入延迟、成本、检索噪声和错误传播）或遗漏必要的工具调用（损害知识密集型或需要最新证据的查询）。

Method: 1) 从同一基座模型构建内部与外部专家，收集配对轨迹；2) 估计"用工具与不用工具"之间的奖励边际，据此将数据划分为内偏（internal-favored）、外偏（external-favored）和模糊（ambiguous）三类；3) 使用清晰边际样本进行Boundary-Aware Cold-Start SFT；4) 采用MARS-RL方法，包含参考分裂（reference-split）rollouts和反事实边际优势，优化工具调用边界决策。

Result: 以检索为主要工具、在Qwen3-4B上进行的实验表明，CoBRA提升了工具使用效率和边界敏感的回答准确性，同时在工具依赖的分布外（out-of-distribution）问题上保持了较强性能。

Conclusion: CoBRA通过反事实边际估计显式建模工具调用的实例级收益，实现更精准的工具调用时机决策，在效率与准确性之间取得更好平衡，且具备一定的分布外泛化能力。

Abstract: As large language models increasingly act through external tools, deciding when to call a tool has become a central problem alongside deciding how to use it. Unnecessary tool calls introduce latency, cost, retrieval noise, and error propagation, while missed calls hurt knowledge-intensive queries or questions requiring up-to-date evidence. Existing methods typically trigger tools from absolute query or generation signals, such as difficulty, confidence, or final task reward, and therefore lack an explicit estimate of the instance-level marginal benefit of tool use. We propose CoBRA, a counterfactual boundary-learning framework for tool-augmented language models. CoBRA first constructs internal and external experts from the same base model, collects paired trajectories, and estimates the reward margin between answering with and without tools. This margin partitions data into internal-favored, external-favored, and ambiguous cases. CoBRA then uses clear-margin samples for Boundary-Aware Cold-Start SFT, followed by MARS-RL with reference-split rollouts and counterfactual marginal advantages to optimize boundary decisions. Experiments with retrieval as the main tool on Qwen3-4B show that CoBRA improves tool-use efficiency and boundary-sensitive answer accuracy while maintaining strong performance on tool-dependent out-of-distribution questions.

</details>


### [336] [Figures as Programs: Recursive Generation of Editable Scientific Figures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01006)
*Yepeng Liu,Dasen Dai,Chengzhi Liu,Yiren Song,Hai Ci,Yu Zhang,Qi Zhang,Mike Zheng Shou,Xin Eric Wang,Yuheng Bu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出FigTree，一个多智能体系统，将科学论文自动转化为结构化的矢量图（SVG）。系统将图表内容锚定在原文中，将图表分解为层级的局部区域，每个区域生成短SVG程序后再组装。通过渲染-批评的迭代循环联合检查渲染图和底层程序，实现视觉缺陷的词法级定位与精确修复。实验表明FigTree能生成高质量图形，且比现有光栅图方法支持更有效的编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学方法学图表对清晰传达复杂方法至关重要，但制作过程劳动密集且需多轮打磨。现有图像生成模型虽可合成美观的光栅图，但单次生成难以达到令人满意效果，且对光栅图的精确编辑对人类和模型都具有挑战性。

Method: 将科学图形生成形式化为递归SVG程序构建。FigTree为多智能体系统，核心流程：1）将图形内容锚定于源论文；2）将图形分解为层级化的局部区域层次；3）为每个区域生成短SVG程序；4）组装各片段。使用渲染-批评（render-critic）迭代优化循环，联合检查渲染结果及其底层程序，将视觉缺陷追溯到具体语句并精准修复。

Result: 对FigTree在图形质量和可编辑性上进行了广泛评估，结果表明FigTree能产生高质量的图形，且在编辑效力上优于现有的基于光栅图的方法。

Conclusion: FigTree通过将科学图形生成建模为递归SVG程序构建并引入多智能体协作与渲染-批评闭环，在生成质量和可编辑性上均优于光栅方法，为科学图形自动生成提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Scientific methodology figures are essential for communicating complex methods clearly, yet creating them remains labor-intensive and typically requires multiple rounds of refinement. Recent image-generation models can synthesize visually appealing raster figures, but producing a human-satisfactory result in a single generation step remains difficult. Moreover, precise edits to raster figures are challenging for both humans and models. We formulate scientific figure generation as recursive SVG program construction and propose \textsc{FigTree}, a \textit{multi-agent} system that automatically transforms a scientific paper into a structured vector figure. \textsc{FigTree} grounds figure content in the source paper, decomposes a figure into a hierarchy of local regions, generates each region as a short SVG program, and assembles the resulting fragments. A render-critic refinement loop jointly inspects the rendered figure and its underlying program, enabling visual defects to be traced to specific statements and accurately repaired. We conduct extensive evaluations of \textsc{FigTree} on figure quality and editability, showing that \textsc{FigTree} produces high-quality figures, while also enabling more effective editing than existing raster-based methods.

</details>


### [337] [Spawn Freely, Act Sparingly: Progressive Risk Vesting for Recursive LLM-Agent Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01035)
*Molly Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出渐进式风险归属（PRV）框架，管理递归LLM智能体扩展现时分支跨越不可逆动作边界时的代理权限。PRV以轨迹级风险预算作为托管，并在分支激活时扣减预算。文中证明了自适应生成树的无时时刻伤害上界，并表明在延迟归属条件下保留了不可撤销支出计费下所有可用的策略。在一种简化分支模型中，作者引入权威再生产数R_A来刻画轨迹伤害的相变：低于临界值时伤害与p成正比，临界时与√p成正比，高于临界值时保持正下限。此外，有限类型占用模型给出了风险与计算的影子价格及阈值规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归LLM智能体通过生成专家分支来扩展搜索，但某些分支最终需要请求发送数据或部署代码的工具，即跨越不可逆的动作边界。核心问题是：何时应当赋予分支执行权威？该文旨在严格界定并回答"分支何时应获得行动权限"这一问题，在安全边界与能力扩展之间寻求平衡。

Method: 提出渐进式风险归属（PRV）机制，将轨迹级风险预算置于托管中，随分支激活而递减扣款。该方法通过沙箱孕育与能力激活的区分来处理安全问题；要求每个局部认证在其完整预激活历史条件下保持有效（处理依赖分支结果）；对自适应生成树进行全时伤害界的理论证明；并通过有限类型占用模型推导风险与计算的影子价格，进而建立阈值规则。

Result: 证明了任意时刻伤害界使得PRV在理论上可被安全使用；当激活门槛、分支费用和计算约束固定时，延迟归属保留了不可撤销立即计费所有可获得的策略；在简化分支模型中，随R_A跨越临界值1，轨迹伤害呈相变特性——亚临界时与p线性、临界时与√p量级、超临界时存在正下限；有限类型占用模型得到风险与计算的影子价格；在嵌套扇出模式下生成阈值决策规则；通过分支计算与分裂样本实验证实上述理论结果。

Conclusion: 分析表明存在一条设计原则：在沙箱中广泛搜索，但应吝啬地授予递归权威，并为每次授权明确收取风险费用。作者同时强调，这些合成研究不能用于估计已部署智能体的真实安全水平。

Abstract: Recursive LLM agents can broaden their search by spawning specialists. Some branches later request tools that send data or deploy code. When should a branch receive authority to act? We distinguish sandbox spawning, in which external controls prevent the specified harm, from capability activation, in which a selected branch crosses an irreversible-action boundary. Progressive Risk Vesting (PRV) holds a trajectory-level risk budget in escrow and debits it as branches are activated. We prove an anytime harm bound for adaptively generated trees. Branch outcomes may be dependent, but each local certificate needs to remain valid conditional on the full pre-activation history, including the information used to select the request. When activation gates, branch charges, and compute constraints are held fixed, delayed vesting preserves every policy available under irrevocable spawn charging. Marginal risk estimates can still fail after branch selection. In a stylized branching model, trajectory harm changes as the authority reproduction number $\mathcal{R}_A$ crosses one. As local risk $p$ approaches zero, trajectory harm is proportional to $p$ below criticality, proportional to $\sqrt{p}$ at criticality, and retains a positive floor above it. A finite-type occupancy model yields risk and compute shadow prices. For nested fanout modes with decreasing marginal value per unit risk, these prices produce a threshold rule. Branching calculations and a split-sample experiment illustrate the results. These synthetic studies do not estimate safety in deployed agents. The analysis suggests a design rule: search broadly in the sandbox and grant recursive authority sparingly, with an explicit risk charge.

</details>


### [338] [Data-Driven Persona-Conditioned Agents for A/B Test Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01038)
*Ziyad Benomar,Weronika Łajewska,Leonardo Perelli,Saab Mansour*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个利用LLM智能体模拟A/B测试结果的仿真框架。与传统依赖合成或规则驱动的用户画像不同，该方法从真实用户行为数据（活动模式、参与信号、推断人口统计）中构建数据驱动的用户画像，将A/B测试模拟视为结构化问答任务，系统研究了问题设计格式、画像数据源与领域对齐、画像个体深度与群体多样性之间的权衡，以及高效群体子采样。在覆盖40个A/B测试、两种指标类型的基准上，最佳配置实现了0.75-0.90的方向准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: A/B测试是评估产品变更的黄金标准，但每次实验都需要真实用户流量、工程投入和数周测量周期，成本高昂且效率低下。现有仿真工作依赖合成或规则驱动的用户画像，缺乏对真实用户群体的忠实建模能力，因此亟需一种低成本、快速且能预测A/B测试结果的前置筛选方法。

Method: 提出基于LLM智能体的A/B测试仿真框架：(1) 从匿名化的真实用户行为数据（包括活动模式、参与信号和推断的人口统计特征）构建数据驱动的用户画像，而非合成或规则画像；(2) 将A/B测试模拟形式化为结构化问答任务；(3) 系统研究四个关键维度：问题设计格式、画像数据源及领域对齐的影响、单画像行为深度与群体多样性之间的权衡、以及高效的群体子采样策略。

Result: 在包含40个A/B测试及两种指标类型的基准数据集上，最佳配置的方向准确率根据测试指标不同达到0.75-0.90，验证了数据驱动画像在A/B测试快速前筛中的有效性。

Conclusion: 数据驱动的LLM智能体用户画像是实现快速、低成本实验预筛查的可行路径，能够在无需真实流量和数周测量的情况下提供较好的A/B测试方向预测能力。

Abstract: A/B testing is the gold standard for evaluating product changes, but each experiment requires real user traffic, engineering effort, and weeks of measurement. We propose a simulation framework that predicts A/B test outcomes using LLM-powered agents conditioned on data-driven personas grounded in real user behavioral signals. Unlike prior work that relies on synthetic or rule-based personas, our agents are constructed from anonymized behavioral data-activity patterns, engagement signals, and inferred demographics-enabling more faithful population modeling. We frame A/B test simulation as a structured question task and systematically study (i) question design formats, (ii) the impact of persona data source and domain alignment, (iii) the trade-off between per-persona behavioral depth and population diversity, and (iv) efficient population subsampling. On a benchmark of 40 A/B tests spanning two metric types, our best configuration achieves 0.75-0.90 directional accuracy depending on the test metric, demonstrating that data-driven personas are a viable path toward fast, low-cost experiment pre-screening.

</details>


### [339] [AgentFactory: Towards Automated Agentic System Design and Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01045)
*Enci Zhang,Haofeng Wang,Yuesheng Zhu,Xiaole Cui,Guibo Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentFactory提出了一种自动化的智能体系统优化框架，联合优化基础模型与工作流结构，同时考虑性能、成本与效率等多目标。框架采用三阶段优化流程，利用高级LLM作为优化器搜索配置空间，在八个跨域基准上平均提升9.1%，在MedQA和FinEval等垂直领域任务中提升高达19.6%和18.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体系统设计高度依赖人工工程，可扩展性和自适应性受限。虽然已有工作探索工作流自动优化，但这些方法往往忽视模型能力维度的重要性，且只关注单一性能指标，未能满足真实部署中对成本与效率的约束。

Method: AgentFactory采用联合优化策略，同时优化基础模型选择（含微调模型）与工作流结构。核心机制包括：(1)利用高级LLM作为优化器来导航庞大的配置搜索空间；(2)采用三阶段优化流程自动发现有效的微调模型与优化工作流组合；(3)通过迭代优化过程系统性探索和评估不同智能体系统设计，兼顾任务特定需求与运营效率。

Result: 在横跨五个领域（通用推理、编程、数学、医学、金融）的八个基准上评估，AgentFactory相比手工设计方法和现有自动化方案均有更优表现，平均改进9.1%，其中MedQA上取得19.6%的提升，FinEval上取得18.7%的提升，在领域特定任务上的增益尤为显著。

Conclusion: AgentFactory证明了一种将模型能力优化与工作流结构搜索相联合、并纳入多目标约束的自动化优化范式，是开发更强大、更具成本效益的智能体系统的有前景的途径。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities as powerful components in agentic systems, enabling sophisticated reasoning and complex task execution. However, current approaches to manually designing and optimizing agentic systems heavily rely on manual effort, limiting their adaptability and scalability. Recent work has explored the automated optimization of workflow designs. However, these approaches often overlook the crucial role of model capabilities and focus on single performance metrics, failing to address real-world deployment constraints. In this paper, we present AgentFactory, a framework that jointly optimizes both foundation models and workflow structures in agentic systems while considering multiple objectives including performance, cost, and efficiency. AgentFactory leverages advanced LLMs as optimizers to navigate the vast search space of possible configurations, employing a three-stage optimization pipeline to automatically discover effective combinations of fine-tuned models and optimized workflows. Through an iterative optimization process, our framework systematically explores and evaluates different agentic system designs, adapting to task-specific requirements while maintaining operational efficiency. We evaluate AgentFactory across eight benchmarks spanning five domains, including general reasoning, coding, mathematics, medicine, and finance. Our experiments demonstrate that AgentFactory consistently outperforms both manually designed methods and existing automated approaches, achieving an average improvement of 9.1% across all benchmarks, with particularly significant gains in domain-specific tasks (19.6% on MedQA and 18.7% on FinEval). These results establish AgentFactory as a promising approach for developing more capable and efficient agentic systems through automated optimization.

</details>


### [340] [QILP-0: Constructing Observational Declarative Twins of Quantum Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01049)
*Marina de la Cruz Echeandía,César Luis Alonso,Tony Ribeiro,Alfonso Ortega de la Puente*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出QXymb——一种构建量子电路可观察声明式孪生模型的通用框架，并实现其首个完整的0阶特化QILP-0。该框架从电路观测行为出发构建有限多值命题逻辑程序，将逻辑精确性与数值/后端等不确定性分离，并通过两个QML设置（Bars & Stripes和MNIST）验证了理论与观测数据的完全精确重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子电路行为的可观察性建模缺乏一般化的声明式框架。现有方法未能系统地将电路观测数据转化为可审计、可验证的符号逻辑理论，也未能将逻辑层面的精确性与实验层面的不确定性（数值、后端、提供商、离散化）有效分离。

Method: QXymb框架通过结构化的可复现分级与观测参考视界增量遍历量子可观测对象家族；采用目标无关几何组织观测响应，并将潜在结构确定性映射回原始可观测列以保持语义可溯源；通过目标无关的可容许离散化将选定的可观测轮廓转换为有限关系；目标仅在后续阶段用于审计孪生可容许性并诱导声明式理论。

Result: 在Bars & Stripes实验中（16到100量子比特，穷举比较乘积与grid-CZ两种嵌入）和Low-Depth MNIST实验中（全部14,708个数字0/1样本，含训练前后变分量子变换），QILP-0在所有报告的关系中均实现完全无冲突的重构，严格准确率为1。这证实了理论作为观测性声明式孪生模型的精确性，且完整性与正确性均达到最优。

Conclusion: QILP-0证明了一个量子电路可以由有限离散观测完全而正确地重构为一个声明式逻辑理论，逻辑精确性可与数值及实现层面的不确定性在审计元数据中分离。该方法为量子机器学习系统的可验证性、可审计性和可解释性提供了新的范式路径。

Abstract: This paper introduces QXymb, a general framework for constructing observational declarative twins of quantum circuits, and develops QILP-0, its first complete order-0 specialization. QILP-0 constructs a finite multi-valued propositional logic program from observed circuit behaviour within a declared observational scope.
  The pipeline traverses a declared family of quantum observables incrementally according to a reproducible structural grading and a declared observational reference horizon. Progress is quantified through reference-relative coverage against a fixed target-independent reference. Observable responses are organized through target-independent geometry, while retained latent structure is mapped deterministically back to original observable columns before symbolic processing, preserving observational semantics and provenance.
  Selected observable profiles are converted into a finite relation through admissible target-independent discretization. The target is used only afterwards to audit twin-admissibility and induce the declarative theory. A theory is certified as an exact observational declarative twin when it completely and correctly reconstructs the resulting finite task-conditioned discrete relation. Logical exactness is therefore separated from numerical, backend, provider, and discretization uncertainty, which is retained as audit metadata.
  Validation uses two complementary QML settings. Exhaustive Bars & Stripes experiments compare product and grid-CZ embeddings from 16 to 100 qubits and exercise the native-discrete branch. Low-Depth MNIST analyses all 14,708 digit-0/1 instances before and after a trained variational quantum transformation and exercises continuous discretization. In every reported relation, the induced QILP-0 theory achieves complete, conflict-free reconstruction with strict accuracy equal to one.

</details>


### [341] [WorldBench: Culturally Grounded Benchmark for Multilingual Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01056)
*Leonardo Ranaldi,Sherrie Shen,Jushi Kai,Alexandra Birch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WorldBench是一个覆盖七种语言、八种文化的多语言真实世界工作流基准，包含1,600个任务。作者提出Constrained Task Success (CTS)评估指标，结合自然语言指令和测试台来综合评定任务完成度、最小环境改动等指标。实验表明，前沿模型仅达到49.2%的CTS，且所有模型在正确性与环境保持之间存在显著差距，证明当前智能体在多语言agentic场景下仍然脆弱，尤其在长时程任务和状态保持约束下表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的智能体在解决复杂多步骤任务中应用日益广泛，但已有基准很少测试状态保持能力、跨语言性能以及真实接地场景的适用性。作者希望通过提出WorldBench来解决这些评估空白，推动语言智能体在实际多语言环境中的发展。

Method: 论文构建了一个多语言、多文化的真实工作流基准WorldBench，包含1,600个任务，覆盖七种语言和八种文化；任务经过具有语言和文化专长的人类标注者的反馈筛选与精炼。在评估方面，作者扩展了先前工作的指标并提出Constrained Task Success (CTS)，它结合自然语言指令与测试台，通过确定性评估和LLM-as-a-Judge两种方式综合评定任务完成度、最小修改及其他互补指标。

Result: 实验结果显示，前沿模型仅达到49.2%的CTS，且所有模型均在任务正确性与环境状态保持之间表现出显著差距，表明当前智能体在多语言agentic场景中仍显脆弱，尤其在长时程任务以及状态保持约束下问题更为突出。

Conclusion: 当前语言智能体在处理多语言、长期任务的agentic场景下仍存在明显局限，特别是在约束环境改动的情况下表现最为薄弱，这揭示了未来需要在状态保持和跨语言泛化能力上取得突破，以推动智能体在实际世界中更可靠地执行任务。

Abstract: Despite the growing use of LLM-powered agents to solve multi-step tasks in complex environments, existing benchmarks rarely test state preservation, performance across languages, and application to realistic, grounded scenarios. To address these concerns, we present WorldBench: a comprehensive, multilingual benchmark of genuine, persona-grounded everyday workflows, where agents can act in a sandbox via structured actions. WorldBench comprises 1,600 tasks across seven languages and eight cultures, filtered and refined through feedback from human annotators with language- and culture-specific expertise. For evaluation, we extend metrics from previous works and introduce Constrained Task Success (CTS), which combines natural language instructions and testbeds to score task completion, minimal modification, and other complementary metrics through deterministic and LLM-as-a-Judge evaluations. Our experiments show that frontier models reach only 49.2% CTS, with all models demonstrating large gaps between correctness and environment preservation. We thereby show that current agents remain brittle in multilingual, agentic scenarios, especially for long-horizon tasks and under state-preservation constraints

</details>


### [342] [User Representation via Cross Multi-source Behavior Pre-training for Mobile Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01057)
*Chengqi Yang,Yiran Qiao,Feng Liu,Xingyu Lou,Zijun Zhou,Xiaoyun Mo,Changwang Zhang,Jiayuan Xu,Jun Wang,Xiang Ao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CM-PTM，一种面向移动端游戏场景的跨多源行为预训练模型，通过对设备级行为日志进行分层级联的"掩码-预测"预训练任务，实现跨行为源依赖和细粒度行为动态的统一建模，在下游移动游戏推荐任务上取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有用户表示预训练研究主要集中在单一应用或应用级行为建模，忽视了移动设备上用户活动的跨源和多重粒度特性。设备层面用户的意图来源于异构行为源与层级动作结构的复杂交互，传统的以应用为中心的建模方法无法有效处理这一挑战。

Method: 提出CM-PTM，采用分层级联的掩码-预测代理任务：先推断下一个行为的来源（source），再逐步在应用-动作（app-action）级别细化预测。该设计使单一预训练范式能够同时统一建模跨源依赖关系和细粒度行为动态。

Result: 在大规模真实移动数据集上的大量实验表明，CM-PTM能有效捕获用户内生兴趣，并在下游移动游戏推荐任务中持续带来显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 分层级联的mask-then-predict预训练范式能够有效解决设备级跨多源行为建模问题，为移动游戏用户表示学习提供了一种优于传统应用中心建模的新方案。

Abstract: User representation pre-training has become a fundamental paradigm for alleviating data sparsity in downstream personalization tasks. However, existing studies predominantly focus on single-app or app-level behaviors, overlooking the inherently cross-source and multi-granular nature of user activities on mobile devices. At the device level, user intent emerges from complex interactions among heterogeneous behavior sources and hierarchical action structures, posing challenges that cannot be addressed by conventional app-centric modeling. To tackle this issue, we propose CM-PTM, a novel Cross Multi-source Behavior Pre-Training Model tailored for mobile game user representation learning on device-level behavioral logs. CM-PTM employs hierarchical cascaded mask-then-predict proxy tasks that first infer the source of the next behavior and then progressively refine predictions at the app-action level. This design enables unified modeling of cross-source dependencies and fine-grained behavioral dynamics within a single pre-training paradigm. Extensive experiments on large-scale real-world mobile datasets demonstrate that CM-PTM effectively captures users' endogenous interests and consistently delivers significant performance gains on downstream mobile game recommendation tasks.

</details>


### [343] [ARISE-RL: Agentic Rubric-Grounded Iterative Self-Evolution with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01058)
*Fanrui Zhang,Ruixue Ding,Qiang Zhang,Xi Chen,Boli Chen,Shihang Wang,Qiuchen Wang,Hongmin Zhan,Jinxin Bian,Li xingchao,Peijin Zheng,Hao cheng,Pengjun Xie,Kaipeng Zhang,Jiawei Liu,Zheng-Jun Zha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ARISE-RL 是一个面向开放环境智能体的全周期自进化强化学习框架，通过任务/评分基准生成器与推理求解器的耦合式协同进化，配合奖励门控自进化蒸馏（RG-SED）机制，在缺乏可验证金标准的环境中实现稳定训练，并在提出的 ECR-Bench 基准套件上取得全面最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放环境智能体通过强化学习训练面临两大障碍：一是缺乏可验证金标准和可扩展评分基准；二是即使接近模型能力边界，长时域开放任务仍会产生脆弱且不稳定的奖励，导致分组策略学习的对比信号弱或含噪声，掩盖了细粒度优化信号。

Method: 提出 ARISE-RL 全周期自进化框架：(1) Generator 生成任务与评分基准，将工具相关评分标准锚定在真实工具观测中，并被奖励以产出与 Solver 能力边界对齐的合法中等难度任务；(2) Solver 通过多步推理与工具使用，在细粒度评分满足信号上学习；(3) RG-SED（奖励门控自进化蒸馏）仅在记忆增强变体带来经验性奖励提升时，选择性地将该变体蒸馏回原策略，以减少分布偏移并避免盲目模仿含噪声的指导；(4) 构建 ECR-Bench 专家校准评分基准套件，覆盖单工具深度研究与多工具旅行规划。

Result: 大量实验表明，ARISE-RL 在所有评估的基准上实现稳健且稳定的整体最先进性能（SOTA）。

Conclusion: ARISE-RL 通过评分基准介导的协同进化与选择性自蒸馏，有效解决了开放环境智能体 RL 训练中奖励不稳定与信号噪声问题，为该领域提供了可扩展的训练方案和可靠评测基准（ECR-Bench）。

Abstract: Training open-ended agents via reinforcement learning (RL) is hindered by the lack of verifiable gold answers and scalable rubrics. Moreover, even near the model's capability boundary, long-horizon open-ended agentic tasks often yield brittle and unstable rewards, resulting in weak or noisy rollout contrast that obscures fine-grained optimization signals for group-based policy learning. To address these challenges, we propose ARISE-RL, a novel full-cycle self-evolution framework that couples a task/rubric Generator and a reasoning Solver through rubric-mediated co-evolution. The Generator grounds tool-related rubric criteria in real tool observations and is rewarded for producing valid, intermediate-difficulty tasks aligned with the Solver's evolving capability boundary. The Solver, in turn, learns from fine-grained rubric satisfaction signals through multi-step reasoning and tool use. We further introduce Reward-Gated Self-Evolution Distillation (RG-SED), which selectively distills a memory-augmented variant of the same policy back into itself only when the memory yields empirical reward improvement, thereby reducing distribution mismatch and avoiding blind imitation of noisy guidance. Finally, to support rigorous evaluation, we present ECR-Bench, an expert-calibrated rubric benchmark suite covering single-tool deep research and multi-tool travel planning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ARISE-RL consistently achieves robust and stable overall state-of-the-art performance across all evaluated benchmarks.

</details>


### [344] [Space Generative AI with Solar Energy Harvesting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01062)
*Jierui Zhang,Jianhao Huang,Zhanwei Wang,Kaibin Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向太阳能供电的空间生成式AI框架。卫星接收用户提示词后，在严格时间窗口内执行基于扩散模型的图像生成任务并将压缩结果下行传输。作者识别出由共享太阳能能量预算支配的"计算-通信"（C²）根本性权衡，并利用确定性轨道运动导致的预测性太阳能能量收集动力学，开发了一种联合C²资源优化框架。通过两步法（先刻画固定生成深度下连续太阳能EH的最大下行吞吐量并建立分离原则，再求解联合C²效用最大化问题得到闭式低复杂度步数选择策略），实现了在真实轨道动力学下较静态计算/通信中心基线的显著端到端性能增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星正成为向缺乏地面基础设施的偏远地区扩展生成式AI服务的新兴平台，但空间生成式AI部署受到太阳能能量收集所提供的有限且时变的机载能量的根本性约束。生成步数（计算）与下行传输（通信）之间需要共享收获的太阳能预算，存在本质权衡，因此需要一种能自适应平衡两者的联合优化框架。

Method: 作者开发了联合C²资源优化框架，采用可分解的两步方法：(1) 针对固定生成深度，在连续太阳能EH下刻画最大下行吞吐量，确立将等待时间选择与最优发射功率控制解耦的分离原则；(2) 构建联合C²效用最大化问题，在主控恒功率机制下推导出闭式、低复杂度的生成步数选择策略。该方法利用了轨道运动确定性带来的可预测太阳能EH动力学特性。

Result: 在真实轨道动力学下的广泛实验表明，所提出的策略能动态平衡生成质量与传输可靠性，在不同太阳能EH状态下，相比静态计算中心基线和通信中心基线，获得了显著的端到端性能增益。

Conclusion: 通过将太阳能EH的可预测性与计算-通信权衡相结合，所提出的联合优化框架能够有效最大化卫星上部署扩散式生成AI的端到端生成性能，实现了生成质量与传输可靠性的动态平衡，证明了这种两步式低复杂度策略在空间场景中的有效性。

Abstract: Satellites are emerging as promising platforms to extend generative \emph{artificial intelligence} (AI) services to remote areas lacking terrestrial infrastructure. However, deploying space generative AI is fundamentally constrained by the limited, time-varying onboard energy supplied by solar \emph{energy harvesting} (EH). This paper presents a framework for solar-powered space generative AI in which a satellite receives a user prompt, executes a diffusion-based image-generation model, and downlinks the compressed result within a strict time window. We identify the fundamental \emph{computation--communication} (C$^2$) trade-offs governed by the shared harvested-energy budgets. Specifically, increasing the number of generation steps improves intrinsic image quality but depletes energy and time available for downlink transmission, whereas prioritizing communication guarantees reliable delivery but sacrifices semantic quality. To balance these trade-offs and maximize \emph{end-to-end} (E2E) generative performance, we exploit the predictable solar-EH dynamics induced by deterministic orbital motion and develop a joint C$^2$ resource-optimization framework using a tractable two-step approach. First, we characterize the maximum downlink throughput for a fixed generation depth under continuous solar EH. This establishes a separation principle that decouples waiting-time selection from optimal transmit-power control. Next, we formulate a joint C$^2$ utility-maximization problem and derive a closed-form, low-complexity step-selection policy in the dominant constant-power regime. Extensive experiments under realistic orbital dynamics demonstrate that the proposed policy dynamically balances generation quality and transmission reliability. This yields significant E2E performance gains over static computation- and communication-centric baselines across diverse solar-EH states.

</details>


### [345] [Latent Recurrent Thoughts: Recurrent Refinement of Proposed Latents for Reasoning with Frozen LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01117)
*Zhaoliang Chen,Jie Fu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Latent Recurrent Thoughts (LRT)框架，将推理从离散token空间转移到模型的连续表示空间中。LRT保持LLM冻结，仅用于建模和解码序列，同时由一个小的辅助网络（任务专用proposer提供基础latents，递归reasoner逐步精化）在连续空间中迭代生成latent thoughts。该方法在符号推理（Countdown-4, Sudoku）和自然语言推理（HumanEval, MBPP, StrategyQA）任务上显著优于先前同条件下的连续空间推理方法，并以极少推理计算超过无思维链提示的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 链式思维（CoT）推理在离散token空间中进行，存在三个核心问题：每一步被提交为文本导致错误传播、良好推理轨迹需要预先存在的轨迹进行模仿学习。在模型的连续表示空间中进行推理可以规避这些约束，但如何计算这些潜在状态（latent states）尚未解决。本文的核心动机是探索一种更高效、更鲁棒的连续空间推理范式，将推理深度与模型规模解耦。

Method: LRT框架沿两个轴设计：(1) 保持LLM冻结并利用其建模与解码序列的能力，由一个小型辅助网络以连续潜在thoughts作为输入提供给LLM；(2) 通过递归方式生成latents——一个微型递归reasoner在众多步骤中逐步精化潜在状态，使用有界残差修正，使latents成为迭代处理的产物而非单次前向传播的结果。具体包含三个组件：任务专用proposer提供base latents、递归reasoner进行精化、冻结的LLM解码最终答案。

Result: 在符号推理任务（Countdown-4, Sudoku）和自然语言推理任务（HumanEval, MBPP, StrategyQA）上，LRT在相同解码器、提示词、数据和训练预算条件下，大幅优于之前的冻结解码器连续空间推理方法，并且以很小比例的推理计算超越了同一骨干网络上无思维模式的链式思维提示方法。

Conclusion: LRT证明了连续空间推理可以作为离散CoT的高效替代方案：通过递归reasoner的迭代精化，推理深度与模型规模解耦，同时冻结LLM避免了反向传播到大型模型所需的计算开销。该方法在多个推理基准上展现出显著优势，表明将推理过程从token空间转移到表示空间的范式是可行且有效的。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought reasoning unfolds in discrete token space: each step is committed as text, errors propagate, and eliciting good traces presupposes traces to imitate. Reasoning instead in a model's continuous representation space - where intermediate states are vectors rather than words - sidesteps these constraints, but leaves open how those latent states should be computed. We approach this along two axes. First, we keep a large language model (LLM) frozen and use it for what it is already good at - modeling and decoding sequences - while a small auxiliary network supplies continuous latent thoughts as input. Second, we produce those latents by recurrence: a tiny recurrent reasoner refines them over many steps, decoupling the depth of computation from the size of the model, so that the latents are a product of iterative processing rather than a single forward pass. We instantiate this as Latent Recurrent Thoughts (LRT): a task-dedicated proposer supplies base latents, a recurrent reasoner refines them through bounded residual corrections, and the frozen LLM decodes the answer. On symbolic reasoning with answer supervision but no reasoning traces (Countdown-4, Sudoku) and on natural-language reasoning (HumanEval, MBPP, StrategyQA), LRT substantially outperforms prior frozen-decoder continuous-space reasoning methods under an identical decoder, prompt, data, and training budget, and outperforms non-thinking-mode chain-of-thought prompting on the same backbone at a small fraction of its inference compute.

</details>


### [346] [Jailbreaking Text-to-Image Models Through Cracks: Navigating Heterogeneous Safety Filters via Multi-Agent Debate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01168)
*Kaiyan Wen,Shijie Zhang,Lu Yu,Guangdong Bai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Detection Surface几何框架，用于刻画异构T2I安全过滤器的决策边界及其联合效应，并在此基础上提出CRACK多智能体辩论框架，通过攻击者、防御者和裁判三个智能体的迭代协作，自适应地搜索越狱提示，在复合防御下达到高达99.63%的攻击成功率，同时减少查询次数并保持语义保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I模型虽然配备了多层异构安全堆栈（文本过滤器、图像分类器、跨模态检测器），但仍易受到越狱攻击。目前的研究要么只针对单个过滤器优化，要么通过聚合反馈查询整个管道，难以识别当前活跃的约束并适应各安全层之间的冲突，导致越狱搜索效率低下。

Method: 1) 提出Detection Surface统一几何框架，刻画异构安全过滤器的决策边界及跨层交互对越狱搜索空间的影响，揭示成功规避受限于跨层冲突驱动的稀疏非凸区域。2) 提出CRACK多智能体辩论框架，将越狱搜索分解为探索、诊断和仲裁三个阶段，协调Attack Agent、Defense Agent和Judge Agent：Attack Agent迭代生成提示突变，Defense Agent获取各层特异性诊断反馈，Judge Agent通过奖励引导优化突变策略，在重复辩论中自适应地调整搜索方向以适应不断演化的跨层约束。

Result: 在多个T2I模型、数据集和安全配置上的广泛实验表明，CRACK在复合防御下达到高达99.63%的攻击成功率，比现有方法需要更少的查询次数，同时保持语义保真度。

Conclusion: Detection Surface框架揭示了跨层安全过滤器冲突对越狱搜索空间的几何影响，而CRACK通过多智能体辩论实现了对复合安全防御的高效自适应攻击，证明了当前多层防御体系仍存在可被系统性利用的漏洞。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) models remain vulnerable to jailbreak attacks that elicit Not-Safe-For-Work (NSFW) content, despite increasingly being guarded by heterogeneous, multi-layer safety stacks combining text filters, image classifiers, and cross-modal detectors. Existing jailbreak studies either optimize against individual filters or query the complete pipeline with aggregate feedback, making it difficult to identify the active constraint and adapt to conflicts across safety layers.In this paper, we introduce the \emph{Detection Surface}, a unified geometric framework that characterizes the decision boundaries induced by heterogeneous T2I safety filters and their joint effect on the jailbreak search space. This formulation reveals that successful evasion is governed by a sparse and non-convex region shaped by cross-layer conflicts, where mutations that bypass one filter may increase exposure to another. Motivated by this analysis, we propose \emph{CRACK}, a multi-agent debate framework for adaptive jailbreak search that decomposes jailbreak search into exploration, diagnosis, and arbitration. CRACK coordinates an Attack Agent, a Defense Agent, and a Judge Agent to iteratively generate prompt mutations, obtain layer-specific diagnostic feedback, and optimize mutation strategies through reward-guided refinement. Through repeated rounds of debate, CRACK adapts its search direction to the evolving cross-layer constraints while preserving the original harmful intent. Extensive experiments across multiple T2I models, datasets, and safety configurations show that CRACK achieves Attack Success Rates (ASR) of up to 99.63\% under composite defenses, while requiring fewer queries than existing methods and maintaining semantic fidelity.

</details>


### [347] [FinLifeBench: Exhaustive Life-Event History and Financial-State Reconstruction from Longitudinal Banking Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01198)
*Hangyeul Lee,Juyoung Oh,Jaeyong Ko,Sunmin Kim,Jaeik Park,Hyunkyu Kim,Jungmin Son,Pilsung Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FinLifeBench基准，用于评估对话式银行助手的长期纵向记录重建能力。该基准包含6,000个韩语银行会话，涵盖两个任务：重建每个生活事件实例及其首次建立的会话锚点，以及在连续检查点重建完整的34路径金融状态。实验发现事件锚点召回率从15个会话的0.591降至300个会话的0.445，最佳GCA@15仅0.470，且两个重建任务的表现关联微弱，说明模型能够定位证据却难以维护完整且时间有效的纵向记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准聚焦于问答、有界回合或针对性回忆，缺乏对跨越多轮会话、随生活事件不断变化而进行的完整且可追溯的纵向客户记录穷尽性重建评估，因此需要新的基准来测试助手在长期重复交互中维护完整、即时且可追踪记录的能力。

Method: 构建FinLifeBench基准：包含来自20条独立合成轨迹的6,000个八回合韩语银行会话，提供确定性、穷尽性的24种事件类型和34条状态路径的黄金标注，并采用共识质量保证机制。在完整上下文条件下，对11个LLM评估两个子任务——(1)重建每个生活事件实例及其首次建立会话的事件锚点召回率；(2)在连续检查点重建完整34路径金融状态，以GCA@N衡量。

Result: 事件锚点召回率从15个会话的0.591下降到300个会话的0.445，错误主要由事件遗漏而非锚点定位不佳导致；金融状态重建频繁将已取代或可能过时的信息当作当前信息，最佳GCA@15仅为0.470；两个重建任务的表现仅存在微弱关联。

Conclusion: LLM能够为已恢复事件定位证据，但在维护完整且时间上有效的纵向记录方面仍存在系统性失败——事件遗漏与信息时效性处理是两个亟待克服的核心挑战，模型性能与所处理历史会话的数量成反比。

Abstract: Repeated banking interactions require assistants to maintain complete, current, and traceable customer records as life changes emerge incidentally in routine requests. Existing benchmarks emphasize question answering, bounded episodes, or targeted recall rather than exhaustive longitudinal reconstruction. We introduce FinLifeBench, which evaluates two tasks over the same cumulative dialogue: reconstructing every life-event instance with its first-establishing session and reconstructing a complete 34-path financial state at consecutive checkpoints. The benchmark contains 6,000 eight-turn Korean banking sessions from 20 independent synthetic trajectories, with deterministic, exhaustive gold for 24 event types and 34 state paths and consensus quality assurance. Across eleven LLMs under a full-context condition, event-anchor recall falls from 0.591 at 15 sessions to 0.445 at 300. Errors are driven primarily by omitted events rather than poor anchor localization, while financial-state reconstruction frequently treats superseded or potentially outdated information as current; the best GCA@15 reaches 0.470. Performance on the two reconstruction tasks is only weakly associated. These results show that models can localize evidence for recovered events while still failing to maintain complete and temporally valid longitudinal records.

</details>


### [348] [H2Table: Hierarchical Hypergraph-Enhanced Large Language Models for Complex Table Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01216)
*Jia Ling,Yangfan Wang,Chen Tang,Haoming Tan,Yang Yang,Yi Guan,Jingchi Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: H2Table是一个将复杂表格表示为层次化嵌套超图的框架，通过定制的超图编码器在超边（表头）与节点（单元格）间进行消息传递，并使用可学习的查询向量作为轻量级桥梁，将代表性结构嵌入注入LLM，从而有效提升复杂表格问答的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM处理表格推理的方式通常将表格线性化为序列，忽略了表格固有的二维层次结构，导致在处理具有嵌套表头的复杂表格时性能不佳。

Method: 1) 将复杂表格表示为层次化的嵌套超图结构；2) 设计定制超图编码器，在超边（表头）和节点（单元格）之间进行消息传递以感知语义蕴含关系；3) 引入一组可学习的查询向量作为轻量级桥梁，从编码器中提取代表性结构嵌入注入LLM。

Result: 在HiTab数据集上，对于嵌套深度为四的高复杂表格，H2Table相较于最先进基线实现了平均22.88%的提升。

Conclusion: H2Table通过超图表示与层次化消息传递，有效应对了具有层次嵌套表头的复杂表格问答任务，验证了保留表格二维结构信息对表推理的重要性。

Abstract: Tables are ubiquitous across diverse domains, yet reasoning over them remains a significant challenge for modern large language models (LLMs). Current approaches typically linearize tables into sequences, inherently overlooking their intrinsic two-dimensional and hierarchical structure. To address this, we propose H2Table (Hierarchical Hypergraph-Enhanced Table Reasoning), a novel framework that represents complex tables as hierarchical nested hypergraphs. To process this representation, we design a tailored hypergraph encoder to facilitate message passing between hyperedges (headers) and nodes (cells), thereby perceiving the semantic entailment relationships between them within complex tables. Furthermore, we introduce a set of learnable query vectors acting as a lightweight bridge to extract representative structural embeddings from the encoder into the LLM. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach effectively handles complex table question answering tasks with hierarchical nested headers. Notably, on the HiTab dataset, H2Table achieves an average improvement of 22.88% over state-of-the-art baselines on highly complex tables with a nesting depth of four. Our code is available at: https://github.com/lila120/h2table.

</details>


### [349] [Prompt-Robust Language Models: Which Training Strategies Work?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01217)
*Frederic Sadrieh,Michal Štefánik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在受控条件下复现并比较了应对大语言模型（LLM）提示敏感性的两种主要策略——改进数据构建和专用鲁棒性目标（如CoIN对比对齐与PPCL一致性正则化）。研究发现，现有鲁棒性微调方法虽优于标准微调和上下文学习，但最优与最差提示之间的性能差距仍高达40-57%；且CoIN和PPCL等先进鲁棒性方法往往无法胜过最简单的数据构建策略（每批次仅使用单一模板训练）。诊断结果表明，辅助目标仅能优化其所惩罚的指标而无法泛化，同时混合不同提示模板的批次因57-64%参数上逐模板梯度符号冲突，迫使优化器在相互竞争的目标间妥协，无法找到共享且不依赖提示的最优解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型对提示表述（prompt formulation）高度敏感，即使模型性能很强，细微的提示变化也可能导致显著性能波动。此前的相关工作分别从数据构建和专用鲁棒性训练目标两个方向解决这一问题，但缺乏在统一受控条件下的系统比较，无法确定各策略的真实效果及其背后的机制。

Method: 在受控条件下复现并比较三种策略：(1) 标准微调与上下文学习作为基线；(2) 先进鲁棒性方法，包括CoIN（对比对齐）与PPCL（一致性正则化）；(3) 数据构建策略（如每批次单个模板训练）。同时引入诊断机制来分析辅助目标的作用范围以及不同模板间梯度的符号冲突情况。

Result: (1) 现有鲁棒性微调方法优于标准微调与上下文学习；(2) 最优与最差提示之间的性能差距仍高达40-57%；(3) CoIN与PPCL未能胜过最简单的数据构建策略——每批次使用单一模板训练；(4) 诊断发现辅助目标只能移动其惩罚的量，无法泛化到其他方面；(5) 57-64%的参数在不同模板下梯度方向冲突，混合模板的批次迫使优化器在冲突更新之间妥协。

Conclusion: 当前鲁棒性微调方法对提示敏感性的缓解效果有限，提示敏感性依然是LLM部署中的核心瓶颈。数据构建策略优于专用鲁棒性目标的关键在于：混合模板批次会因梯度方向冲突（57-64%参数）而妨碍优化器找到共享、与提示无关的解。研究揭示了基于每批次单模板训练的数据构建方式为何简单有效，并为未来设计提示鲁棒性训练方法提供了诊断依据。

Abstract: Despite their strong performance, large language models remain highly sensitive to prompt formulation. Prior work addresses this through refined data construction or through dedicated robustness objectives. We reproduce and compare these strategies under controlled conditions, and measure how effective they are in addressing models' prompt sensitivity. We find the current robustness fine-tuning methods improve over standard fine-tuning and in-context learning, but the best-to-worst prompt gap remains as high as 40-57% of performance. Moreover, the recent robustness-enhancing methods we test - CoIN for contrastive alignment and PPCL for consistency regularization - often fail to outperform the simplest data construction strategy: training on one template per batch. Our diagnostics explain these results. The auxiliary objectives move the quantity they penalize, but do not generalize beyond it. Additionally, data construction strategies differ due to the conflicting signs of per-template gradients on 57-64% of parameters. Thus, batches that mix formulations force the optimizer to reconcile competing updates instead of finding a shared, prompt-agnostic one.

</details>


### [350] [Measuring the Behavioral Fidelity of Long-Horizon Human Activity Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01257)
*Yi Fei Cheng,Fan Yang,Iremsu Bas,Koichiro Niinuma,Narishige Abe,David Lindlbauer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个评估LLM人类模拟器在长时程真实活动模拟中行为保真度的框架，横跨时间粒度和分析层次。以43小时多摄像头办公室活动数据集为案例，比较了三种trace导出的条件机制（人物描述符、少样本示例、统计转换和时段先验），发现统计先验在活动分布和序列分布上最接近真实行为，但过度碎片化日常流程并抑制个体内变异性，因此需要多维度的整体评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的人类模拟器越来越多地用于政策评估、人类行为评估和训练场景，这些应用要求模拟器忠实地复现真实行为模式。然而，先前工作主要集中在调查问卷回答和对话行为的保真度上，长时程的真实世界活动（long-horizon real-world activity）行为保真度在很大程度上未被探索。本文旨在填补这一空白，建立评估长时程活动模拟中行为保真的框架。

Method: 作者提出一个跨时间粒度（temporal granularities）和分析层次（levels of analysis）的行为保真度评估框架。作为案例研究，收集了包含43小时多摄像机数据的野外办公室活动数据集（in-the-wild office activity），并比较了三种从行为轨迹（trace）导出的条件机制：（1）人物描述符（persona descriptors），（2）少样本示例（few-shot exemplars），（3）统计转换和时段先验（statistical transition and time-of-day priors）。

Result: 研究发现行为保真度在不同指标上并不一致：统计先验机制使活动分布和序列分布最接近真实行为，但这种机制会过度碎片化日常流程（over-fragment routines）并抑制个体内变异性（within-person variability）。这表明没有一个单一的调节机制在所有维度上都能达到最佳保真度，保真度是多维度的。

Conclusion: 研究结论强调需要进行更全面的评估——不应仅依赖单一指标，而应跨越多个指标、时间粒度和分析层次来综合评判LLM模拟器的行为保真度。该工作为长时程人类活动模拟的保真度评估奠定了基础，并为未来改进模拟策略提供了方向。

Abstract: As LLM-based human simulators are increasingly used for policy, evaluation, and training, they must faithfully reproduce real behavioral patterns. While prior work has examined behavioral fidelity in survey responses and dialogue, longer-horizon real-world activity remains largely unexplored. We introduce a framework for evaluating behavioral fidelity in long-horizon activity simulations across temporal granularities and levels of analysis. As a case study, we collect a 43-hour multi-camera dataset of in-the-wild office activity and compare trace-derived conditioning mechanisms: persona descriptors, few-shot exemplars, and statistical transition and time-of-day priors. We find that behavioral fidelity is not uniform across metrics: statistical priors bring activity and sequence distributions closest to real behavior, yet over-fragment routines and suppress within-person variability. These findings motivate a more holistic evaluation that spans multiple metrics, temporal granularities, and levels of analysis.

</details>


### [351] [Dual Process Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01260)
*Jiayi Yan,Francesco Fabiano,Alessandro Abate*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于神经符号视角的双过程架构，将符号求解器（System-2）与经验驱动的学习模块（System-1）通过元认知控制器动态结合，用于非线性运动规划，在多个基准环境中显著提升规划效率、准确性与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典控制与规划方法提供强保证但计算效率低、适应性差；学习型方法虽能加速决策并处理难题却缺乏结构性保证。作者旨在弥合两者差距，通过神经符号融合实现兼具鲁棒推理与学习优势的机器人运动规划。

Method: 受Thinking Fast and Slow启发，提出双过程架构：包含作为System-2的符号求解器与作为System-1的经验驱动模块，由元认知控制器动态编排二者交互，在快速直觉与慢速精确推理之间智能切换。

Result: 在多个非线性基准环境中验证，该架构在规划效率、准确性和泛化能力上取得一致性提升，并促进跨任务的知识复用。

Conclusion: 将学习与结构化推理紧密耦合，为构建更强大、适应性更强的机器人系统提供了可扩展的路径。

Abstract: Robotic systems are deeply embedded in both industry and everyday life, where they are expected to act with speed, precision, and reliability. Classical control and planning methods have long delivered strong guarantees, but often at the cost of computational efficiency and adaptability. More recently, learning-based approaches have shown promise in overcoming these limitations, enabling agents to leverage experience to accelerate decision-making and address previously intractable problems. In this work, we bridge these two approaches through a neuro-symbolic perspective on nonlinear motion planning. Inspired by the Thinking Fast and Slow paradigm, we introduce a dual-process architecture that combines the strengths of robust reasoning and learning. Our framework integrates state-of-the-art symbolic solvers as a ``System-2'' component with experience-driven ``System-1'' modules. A metacognitive controller dynamically orchestrates their interaction, selecting when to rely on fast intuition versus slower, more precise reasoning. By evaluating the framework across diverse nonlinear benchmark environments, we demonstrate that this architecture yields consistent gains in planning efficiency, accuracy, and generalization, while promoting reuse across tasks. The results suggest that tightly coupling learning with structured reasoning offers a scalable path toward more capable and adaptive robotic systems.

</details>


### [352] [Making Prospective Memory SLM-Shaped: Typed Intention Stores for Small-Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01272)
*Jinqing Zhao,Chengcan Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出前瞻性意图存储（PIS）脚手架，将前瞻记忆（PM）任务视为受模式约束的状态跟踪而非开放式推理。PIS将生命周期逻辑置于代码中，仅在模型上执行有范围的语言工作，无需微调或轨迹蒸馏。在PM-Bench上，DeepSeek-Chat+PIS达到82.9% Set-F1，刷新SOTA；在Gemma-E2B上达66.2%，远超无存储（4.2%）和回顾性记忆方法（≤6.6%）；在另一任务上达70.1%，超越回顾性方法上限54.4%，使小模型超越已发布的大型模型脚手架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在PM-Bench等前瞻记忆基准上表现不佳（最佳发布脚手架仅65.1% Set-F1），现有方法依赖参数规模或训练增强，但作者论证PM实为受模式约束的状态跟踪问题，而非开放式推理，因此需要验证小模型在类型化动作空间中能否有效执行该任务。

Method: 提出前瞻性意图存储（PIS）架构，将意图生命周期管理（状态跟踪逻辑）编码在外部代码与存储中，让模型仅承担有范围限定的语言生成工作；采用agentic、训练无关的脚手架设计，不做选择器微调，不做轨迹蒸馏，依赖typing（类型化动作空间）来约束模型行为。

Result: 在PM-Bench上，DeepSeek-Chat+PIS达82.9% Set-F1，从65.1%提升显著；在Gemma-E2B上，无存储仅4.2% Set-F1，七种回顾性记忆方法最多6.6%，而PIS达66.2%；在另一项任务上PIS达70.1%（回顾性方法最高仅54.4%）。PIS刷新PM-Bench SOTA，并使小模型超越已发布的大模型脚手架。

Conclusion: 结果表明前瞻记忆任务的核心难点并非开放式推理能力，而是受模式约束的状态跟踪；将生命周期管理外置到代码中即可让小模型在此类基准上超越大模型，为PM任务提供了一条训练无关的高效新路径。

Abstract: Prospective memory means carrying out a deferred intention at the right future cue while other work continues. Benchmarks now isolate it as an agent skill, yet frontier LLMs still struggle: the best published PM-Bench scaffold reaches only 65.1% Set-F1. We argue that this loop is schema-constrained state tracking rather than open-ended reasoning, and that small models can execute it when the action space is typed. We propose the Prospective Intention Store (PIS) that puts lifecycle logic in code and scoped language work on the model. The scaffold is agentic and training-free: no selector fine-tuning and no trajectory distillation. On PM-Bench, DeepSeek-Chat with PIS reaches 82.9% Set-F1. On Gemma-E2B, Set-F1 is only 4.2% without a store and at most 6.6% under seven retrospective memories, while PIS reaches 66.2%. PIS further reaches 70.1% Set-F1, where retrospective memory methods stay at most 54.4%. PIS sets a new state of the art on this benchmark and enables small models to surpass the published large-model scaffold.

</details>


### [353] [Analog-DB: An Agent-First Analog Integrated Circuit Database, From Blocks to Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01286)
*Danial Noori Zadeh,Mohamed B. Elamien*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出analog-db，一个开源、版本化的模拟集成电路设计数据库，基于可共享的设计表示（DSL），将每个设计捕获为工艺无关的拓扑、可复用的测试台与机器可读的数据手册；通过参数化方案实现子模块与器件尺寸的命名参数及约束传递，使电路可组合与可重定向。在regulator语料上23个电路-工艺绑定全部满足自身规格带，10/23满足公共类规格带；17/23的失败导入在gm/ID参数化子模块闭环中1-3次迭代内收敛。监督案例中，编码代理在开放130nm工艺上仅基于发布产物完成斩波仪表放大器运放核设计，定位了4处手工错误及缺失的共模反馈环路。数据库包含16类68个电路，附录发布在GitHub。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟集成电路设计成果难以共享分享：代工厂NDA限制了设计所依赖的工艺细节公开，而发布结果背后的测试台极少发布，导致设计无法被复现、复用或验证。

Method: 提出analog-db开源版本化数据库，采用三层核心机制：(1) 领域特定语言（DSL）将每个设计统一捕获为工艺无关拓扑+可复用测试台+机器可读datasheet于一体的单一schema；(2) 参数化方案将功能子块与器件尺寸暴露为带匹配约束的命名参数，实现电路可组合与可重定向；(3) schema契约与可查询目录供AI设计代理直接发现和复用。配套分层harness、gm/ID尺寸闭环与功率/性能看板。

Result: 在regulator语料上，23个电路-工艺绑定（3个开放工艺套件）均满足各自记录的规格带（典型角、匹配器件、无版图），其中10/23满足公共类规格带；17个导入失败的设计在基于注释子块角色驱动的gm/ID尺寸闭环中1-3次迭代收敛。监督案例中，编码代理仅从发布产物出发在开放130nm工艺上完成了斩波仪表放大器运放核尺寸设计，并定位了4处手工缺陷和缺失的共模反馈环路——而纯尺寸基线无法修复。数据库共含68个电路、覆盖16类。

Conclusion: analog-db证明了一种以完整可共享表示（拓扑+测试台+datasheet + 参数化子块注释）为核心的设计发布范式，可同时支持人工与AI代理的复现、重用与自动尺寸设计，为模拟IC设计成果的开放共享建立了可行基础设施。

Abstract: Sharing analog integrated circuit designs remains difficult: foundry non-disclosure agreements restrict the process details a design depends on, and the testbenches behind published results are rarely released. We present analog-db, an open-source, versioned database built on a shareable design representation. A domain-specific language captures each design as a process-neutral topology, reusable testbenches, and a machine-readable datasheet under one schema, so a design is shared in full and re-simulates on the process kits it is bound to. A parameterization scheme exposes functional sub-blocks and device sizes as named parameters that carry their matching constraints, making circuits composable and retargetable; a schema-governed contract and queryable catalog let AI design agents discover and reuse them directly. Across the regulator corpus, all 23 circuit-kit bindings on three open kits meet their own recorded specification bands (typical corner, matched devices, no layout) and 10 of 23 meet a common class band. Seventeen of the 23 imported sizings failed their testbenches and closed under a gm/ID sizing loop driven by the annotated sub-block roles, typically within one to three iterations. In a supervised case study, a coding agent working from the released artifacts sized the op-amp cores of a chopper instrumentation amplifier on an open 130nm kit, locating four hand-entry defects and a missing common-mode feedback loop that the sizing-only baseline did not repair. The database holds 68 circuits across sixteen classes, verifiable at schematic level under a tiered harness and tracked on a power/performance scoreboard, released at https://github.com/MacAnalog/spicexplorer-release.

</details>


### [354] [A Composable Evaluation System for Reproducible Omni-Modal Foundation Model Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01315)
*Hodong Lee,Sanghee Park,Dohoon Ryu,Jungwhan Kim,Junyeob Kim,Soyoon Kim,Geewook Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OmniEvaluator是一个连接现有推理引擎和评测库的全模态评估工具，通过统一接口暴露四个推理后端、四个评测框架和超过一千个基准测试，支持精确复现、跨模型对比、联邦GPU共享和轻量级内置验证器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态（文本、图像、视频、音频）评估工具彼此推理引擎、提示约定和指标实现互不兼容，开发者为每个工具链维护独立环境且难以跨结果对比。作者在自身模型开发中面临这一痛点，因此构建OmniEvaluator而非重新实现基准测试。

Method: 在高层连接现有推理引擎和评测库（而非重新实现基准），暴露四种推理后端、四种评测框架和超过千个基准测试于单一接口。每次运行记录为包含完整配置的artifact以确保精确复现，结果流入共享仪表板用于跨模型对比。采用联邦模式在并发评估间共享GPU推理服务器，并内置轻量级（可在CPU上运行）的验证器，在基于规则的评分受配置不匹配影响时保持分数稳定，匹配具备成本效益的商业LLM评审但不产生频繁API费用。

Result: 系统实现了跨模态的统一评估接口，支持四个推理后端和四个评测框架、超过千个基准，提供精确复现和共享仪表板，内置CPU可运行的验证器在规则评分波动时保持稳定，达到商业LLM评审的效果而无需持续API成本。系统、演示视频和仪表板均已公开（GitHub链接）。

Conclusion: OmniEvaluator通过高层连接而非重新实现的方式，在多模态统一评估基础设施方面提供了实用的解决方案，兼具可复现性、可共享性和成本效益，已公开放布供社区使用。

Abstract: Building an omni-modal foundation model means evaluating it across text, image, video, and audio. Excellent evaluation toolkits exist for each modality, but their inference engines, prompt conventions, and metric implementations are mutually incompatible, so practitioners end up maintaining separate environments for every toolchain and still struggle to compare results across them. OmniEvaluator grew out of this need in our own model development: rather than reimplementing benchmarks, it connects existing inference engines and curated evaluation libraries at a higher level, exposing four inference backends, four evaluation frameworks, and over a thousand benchmarks through a single interface. Every run is recorded as an artifact capturing the full configuration for exact reproduction, and results flow into a shared dashboard for cross-model comparison. A federated mode shares GPU inference servers across concurrent evaluations, and a built-in verifier, small enough to run on CPU, keeps its score stable across engines and prompts where rule-based scoring fluctuates under configuration mismatch, matching cost-efficient commercial LLM judges without their recurring API cost. The system, demo video, and dashboard are publicly available. (https://github.com/naver-ai/omni-evaluator)

</details>


### [355] [Automated Event Log Generation from Unstructured Text Using Finetuned LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01320)
*Maximilian Seeth,Gabriel Marques Tavares,Daniel Schuster*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用大规模语言模型（LLMs）作为自动数据转换器的可扩展框架，将非结构化文本资源（如工单、手册、文本报告）转换为结构化事件日志，以解决流程挖掘（PM）依赖人工构建事件日志的瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流程挖掘技术的有效性严格依赖于结构化事件日志的可用性，而传统上事件日志需要领域专家和流程挖掘专家耗费大量精力人工构建。这一高昂成本导致大量组织知识（如事故工单、手册、文本报告）未被充分利用，成为流程挖掘应用的瓶颈。

Method: 作者提出利用LLMs作为数据转换器的可扩展框架，在新建的text-to-log数据集上对LLMs进行微调（finetuning），使其能够从非结构化文本资源中提取高保真度的事件日志，并与few-shot和zero-shot提示方法进行对比。

Result: 微调后的LLM模型在从非结构化资源中提取事件日志方面显著优于few-shot和zero-shot提示方法，差异幅度大，表明微调是生成可靠事件数据的必要前提条件。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一个有前景的流水线，使之前未被利用的数据可用于流程挖掘生态系统，有效扩展了流程挖掘在组织工作流分析中的应用可能性。

Abstract: Process mining (PM) provides a powerful framework for discovering and optimizing operational processes from event data. However, the efficacy of PM techniques is strictly predicated on the availability of structured event logs. Thus far, event logs have often been laboriously created by domain and process mining experts. This costly effort causes large portions of organizational knowledge, including incident tickets, manuals, and textual reports, to remain underutilized. We address this bottleneck by investigating the efficacy of Large Language Models (LLMs) as automated data translators. We propose a scalable framework that leverages LLMs as data translators to bridge the gap between unstructured textual resources and structured event data. We finetune LLMs on a newly created text-to-log dataset, demonstrating that the resulting models can extract high-fidelity event logs from unstructured resources. Our results show that this finetuning approach outperforms few-shot or zero-shot prompting by a large amount, highlighting finetuning as a necessary pre-condition for generating reliable event data. We conclude that our method provides a promising pipeline for making previously unused data available to process mining ecosystems, effectively expanding the possibilities of using PM to further investigate organizational workflows.

</details>


### [356] [LEAP: Likelihood Elicitation and Aggregation for LLM-based Probabilistic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01337)
*Yufei Chen,Yiran Zhao,Xiaogang Xu,Qipeng Xie,Jiafei Wu,Zhe Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LEAP通过逐条分析证据并提取似然参数，再结合显式先验和确定性概率模型将其聚合为后验分布，取代了传统的"整体阅读再预测"（单体预测）范式，从而在预测和校准指标上优于现有基于LLM的预测系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的预测系统虽借助更强的搜索和工具使用在金融、体育等真实任务上取得了改进，但其"单体预测"设计——让LLM一次性阅读所有收集到的证据并产出最终预测——会掩盖每条证据对结果的影响方式，并在相互竞争的结局之间混淆不确定性，难以保证证据贡献的可再现性与可控性。

Method: LEAP重组了预测阶段中证据的使用方式：逐条检视每条证据并提取描述其与目标指标关系的似然参数；通过显式先验与确定性概率模型将这些似然组合成后验分布。该方法同时支持连续型、单选项和多选项预测，并保持证据贡献的可复现性。作者构建了涵盖预测、信息检索和浏览任务的基准，并在自研agent循环及多种agent CLI框架上进行评估。

Result: 在给定相同证据的条件下，LEAP在多数预测与校准指标上跨模型取得改进，且在控制先验访问、推理预算与聚合方式的对比实验中仍保持更强性能。

Conclusion: LEAP通过"逐项似然提取+显式概率聚合"的结构化预测流程，克服了单体预测中证据归因不清与不确定性坍塌的局限，是一种更稳健、可复现且更优的概率预测方法。

Abstract: LLM-based forecasting systems have improved on real-world tasks such as financial markets and sports outcomes, largely through stronger search and tool use. Many systems still ask an LLM to read all collected evidence together and produce the final forecast. We call this design Monolithic Prediction. It can obscure how individual evidence items affect the result and collapse uncertainty across competing outcomes. We propose LEAP (Likelihood Elicitation and Aggregation for Probabilistic forecasting), which reorganizes how collected evidence is used in the prediction stage. LEAP examines each evidence item separately and elicits likelihood parameters that describe its implications for the target. An explicit prior and a deterministic probabilistic model then combine these likelihoods into a posterior distribution. This procedure supports continuous, single-choice, and multi-choice forecasts while preserving reproducible evidence contributions. We build a benchmark covering forecasting, information-seeking, and browsing tasks, and evaluate LEAP on our own agent loop and several agent CLI frameworks. Given the same evidence, LEAP improves most prediction and calibration metrics across models and remains stronger under controlled comparisons of prior access, inference budget, and aggregation.

</details>


### [357] [Cheap Verifiers, Large Blind Spots: Measuring the Reliability Cost of Cost-Saving Cascades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01345)
*Dushyant Rajput*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究推理级联（inference cascades）的自我改进闭环：用小模型回答多数查询，将困难样本升级到前沿大模型作为验证器，并对验证器拒绝的样本进行微调以降低升级率。通过对真实LLM的实验发现四个关键结果：(1) 验证器的"盲区"（接受学生错误答案的比例β）大且具有对抗性，随学生能力增长（β从0.12增至0.55），随验证器能力降低，在廉价学生+廉价验证器组合中最严重；(2) 使用前沿验证器可将β降至约0.05，但会对46%的困难查询付费升级（真实错误率仅39%），成本高昂；(3) 在所有教师模型（跨家族和同家族）下，对验证器拒绝的尾部做朴素纠正性微调不仅不能提升小模型，反而使其退化甚至崩溃；(4) 级联自身的仪表盘（所有通过验证器计算的指标）始终显示3%的错误率，而真实错误率高达32%，系统在构造上对自身退化失明。作者给出解释这一盲区的理论——两群体守恒律 ε∞ ≲ q0β0——以及验证机制的合成研究。结论是：自我改进级联的可靠性无法通过其自身验证器计算的任何指标来评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理级联通过廉价模型回答大多数查询、将困难尾部升级到前沿模型来降低成本。自然的扩展是闭环自我改进：在验证器拒绝的样本上微调廉价学生，使升级率和成本逐轮下降。作者试图测量这个闭环在真实LLM上的实际效果。

Method: 在真实LLM上构建推理级联闭环，测量验证器盲区β（学生错误答案被验证器接受的比例）随学生/验证器规模的变化；测量前沿验证器下的升级率与真实错误率；对验证器拒绝的尾部数据进行朴素纠正性微调，评估跨家族和同家族教师的效果；分析级联自身指标与真实错误率的偏差；提出两群体守恒律 ε∞ ≲ q0β0 的理论解释，并做合成实验验证机制。

Result: 四个主要发现：(1) 验证器盲区大且对抗性：β随学生规模从0.5B到32B从0.12增至0.55，随验证器能力增强而减小，最坏情况正是级联存在的初衷场景（廉价学生+廉价验证器）；(2) 用前沿验证器换取低盲区（β≈0.05）代价高昂——对46%的困难MATH查询升级（真实错误仅39%）；(3) 朴素纠正性微调在所有教师下都导致小模型的退化甚至崩溃，自我改进循环自毁；(4) 级联内部指标恒显示3%错误率，真实错误率却高达32%，系统对自身退化在构造上失明。理论给出两群体守恒律ε∞ ≲ q0β0解释该盲区。

Conclusion: 自我改进的推理级联在可靠性上存在根本性缺陷：其内部验证器无法感知自身退化，所有通过自身验证器计算的指标都无法真实反映系统质量。自我改进循环不仅不再自改进（微调反而导致崩溃），而且系统对退化在构造上失明。实际结论：自我改进级联的可靠性不能通过其自身验证器计算的任何指标来衡量。

Abstract: Inference cascades cut cost by answering most queries with a cheap model and escalating a hard tail to a frontier model that acts as verifier. A natural extension closes the loop: fine-tune the cheap student on the verifier's rejections so the escalation rate, and cost, fall each round. We measure this loop on real LLMs and report four findings. First, the verifier's blind spot, the fraction of the student's wrong answers it accepts, is large and moves adversarially: it grows with student capability ($β$ from 0.12 to 0.55 as the student scales 0.5B to 32B) and shrinks with verifier capability, so it is worst in the cheap-student, cheap-verifier regime cascades exist to create. Second, buying it away returns the saving: a frontier verifier drives $β$ to about 0.05 but then escalates on 46% of hard-MATH queries against a 39% true error rate, paying the frontier price on nearly half of all traffic. Third, naive corrective fine-tuning on the verifier-rejected tail does not improve the small student but degrades and ultimately collapses it, across every teacher we tried (cross-family and same-family), so at this scale the self-improving loop is self-defeating. Fourth, through all of this the cascade's own dashboard, every metric computed through the verifier, reads a flat 3% error while true delivered error swings up to 32%: the system is blind to its own degradation by construction. We then give the theory that explains the blindness, a two-population conservation law, $ε_\infty \lesssim q_0 β_0$, under which every in-loop metric improves while true quality does not, and a synthetic study that validates the mechanism. The practical conclusion: the reliability of a self-improving cascade cannot be read from any metric computed through its own verifier.

</details>


### [358] [SymFold: Synergizing Evolutionary and Structural Priors for Accurate Protein Inverse Folding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01353)
*Handong Wang,Jiaxin Qi,Baisheng Lai,Jianqiang Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种对称双路径架构用于蛋白质逆折叠任务，同时利用蛋白质语言模型（PLMs）的预训练序列进化知识和多模态蛋白质语言模型（MPLMs）的预训练结构知识，迭代引导蛋白质序列生成。该方法在标准基准上达到最先进性能，并通过消融研究验证了对称设计的合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蛋白质逆折叠方法采用串行流水线：结构编码器预测粗序列，再由PLMs后处理精修。但PLMs仅做事后序列编辑，精修效果受限于上游预测质量。虽然MPLMs可直接编码结构生成序列，但实验发现其对逆折叠并不有效。因此需要一种能同时发挥PLMs和MPLMs各自预训练优势的方法。

Method: 提出对称双路径架构，其中一条路径利用PLMs的序列进化预训练知识，另一条路径利用MPLMs的结构预训练知识，两条路径通过迭代机制相互引导蛋白质序列生成。与传统串行流水线不同，该方法摒弃了"先编码后精修"的依赖关系，使两条路径在生成过程中协同优化。

Result: 在标准蛋白质逆折叠基准上的大量实验表明，该方法超越了先前所有方法，达到最新最先进性能。消融研究验证了对称设计的合理性，各路径的贡献得到确认。

Conclusion: 对称双路径架构证明了同时利用PLMs和MPLMs预训练知识、以对称而非串行的方式整合是蛋白质逆折叠的有效方向，为社区提供了新的研究思路。

Abstract: Protein inverse folding aims to recover amino acid sequences for a given 3D protein structure, underpinning broad applications such as enzyme engineering and drug discovery.Current methods often follow a serial pipeline, in which a structure encoder predicts a coarse sequence, which is then refined by protein language models (PLMs). However, because PLMs only perform post-hoc sequence edits, the refinement is bounded by the quality of upstream predictions.Thanks to recent multimodal protein language models (MPLMs), we could directly encode structure to generate sequences with pretrained structural knowledge, but we observe that they are not effective for inverse folding. Therefore, we introduce a symmetric dual-path architecture that both leverages PLMs for pretrained sequence evolution knowledge and MPLMs for pretrained structural knowledge to iteratively guide protein sequence generation.Through extensive experiments across standard protein inverse folding benchmarks, our method achieves state-of-the-art performance, surpassing prior approaches, and ablation studies validate the rationale of our symmetric design, revealing a promising direction for the community.

</details>


### [359] [EDGE: Error Dependency Graph-Guided Multi-Error Attribution in Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01360)
*Jun Hou,Priya Pitre,Yi Fang,Xuan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EDGE框架，通过构建错误依赖图并通过反事实推演验证可靠的因果子集，实现多错误归因，超越传统的单一根因定位方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型智能体（LLM agent）的失败往往包含多个相互关联的错误，而现有归因方法通常只识别单一负责主体、步骤或根因，未能显式建模错误之间的依赖关系。这种缺失限制了错误诊断和修复的有效性。

Method: EDGE框架包含三个核心环节：(1) 从观察到的错误事件构建错误依赖图；(2) 通过反事实推演（counterfactual rollout）验证得到可靠的因果子集；(3) 利用推理图引导两阶段LLM-as-judge检测器进行错误归因，干预验证的子图则为解释和修复分析提供更可靠的基础。

Result: 在TRAIL和MAST两个基准上的实验表明，EDGE在大多数评估模型和设置下提升了类别级多错误归因性能。与Who&When风格提示的适配实验表明，依赖图在不同提示策略下均能提供帮助。

Conclusion: 依赖结构是智能体失败诊断中超越孤立根因预测的有用先验，EDGE框架验证了显式建模错误间依赖关系对提高多错误归因准确性的价值。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agent failures often contain multiple related errors rather than a single mistake. Existing attribution methods usually identify a responsible agent, step, or root cause, but do not explicitly model dependency between errors. We introduce EDGE, an Error Dependency Graph-guided multi-Error attribution framework. EDGE constructs an error dependency graph from observed error events and validates a reliable causal subset through counterfactual rollout. The inference graph guides a two-stage LLM-as-judge detector for error attribution, and the intervention-validated subgraph provides a more reliable basis for explanation and repair analysis. Experiments on TRAIL and MAST show that EDGE improves category-level multi-error attribution across most evaluated models and settings. Experiments with adapted Who&When-style prompts show that the graph helps across prompting strategies. These results suggest that dependency structure is a useful diagnostic prior for agent failures beyond isolated root-cause prediction.

</details>


### [360] [A survey of AI-generated voices and their detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15411)
*Chengzhe Sun,Tianle Yang,Siwei Lyu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本篇综述全面概述了AI语音生成与检测方法，涵盖技术基础与最新前沿进展，并识别关键开放挑战、基准资源和未来研究方向，旨在帮助未来研究者应对AI合成语音带来的安全威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI语音生成技术可被用于无障碍工具等良性用途，但也助长了冒充、欺诈和虚假信息等有害行为。最近的语音克隆诈骗事件凸显了建立稳健防护措施的紧迫性。与图像和视频深度伪造不同，由于语音学、韵律学和听觉感知的复杂性，合成语音的检测面临独特挑战，因此需要一篇系统性的综述来整合该领域的进展。

Method: 本文采用综述方法论，系统性地对AI语音生成和检测方法进行全面调研，涵盖技术基础和最新的前沿技术进展（state-of-the-art），并针对该领域的关键开放挑战、基准资源（benchmark resources）以及未来研究方向进行识别与归类。

Result: 该综述成功构建了AI语音生成与检测领域的全面知识框架，覆盖从技术基础到最新前沿进展的全谱系内容，并梳理了该领域面临的关键开放挑战、可供研究者使用的基准资源以及未来可能的研究方向。

Conclusion: 该综述为未来研究者提供了关于AI语音生成与检测领域的系统性参考资源，特别强调了语音检测相比图像和视频深度伪造检测的独特挑战，并指出了该领域亟待解决的开放问题和可能的前进方向。

Abstract: The ability of artificial intelligence (AI) models to generate highly realistic human voices has advanced rapidly. These technologies power accessibility tools, virtual assistants and creative applications, but they also enable harmful uses, including impersonation, fraud and disinformation. Recent incidents of voice cloning scams targeting businesses and political leaders underscore the urgent need for robust safeguards. Unlike image and video deepfakes, the detection of synthetic voices poses unique challenges due to the complexity of phonetics, prosody and auditory perception. This survey offers a comprehensive overview of AI voice generation and detection methods, encompassing both the technical foundations and the latest state-of-the-art advances. This study also identifies key open challenges, benchmark resources and future directions to make this survey useful for future researchers.

</details>


### [361] [Neuro-Symbolic Geometric Abstraction (NeuSOGA): From Observations to Symbolic Mathematical Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01408)
*Qingde Li,Qingqi Hong,Jie Tian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出NeuSOGA（神经符号几何抽象）框架，将几何观测渐进转化为拓扑抽象、几何抽象和符号数学表示，实现从观测到可解释符号的完整通路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI系统通过大规模统计学习获得强大感知能力，但知识被编码在难以检查和操控的潜在参数中。受神经符号AI和人类抽象理论启发，作者希望弥合"可感知但不可解释"的鸿沟，建立从几何观测到显式符号数学表示的转化机制。

Method: NeuSOGA采用四阶段渐进架构：(1)基于欧氏距离变换的拓扑引导结构发现；(2)利用Segment Anything进行基础模型感知；(3)自适应多尺度几何抽象；(4)通过隐式面积样条实现符号综合。最终生成支持任意阶平滑、可加性组合和闭式求值的解析隐式模型。

Result: 在ModelNet40点云、任意视角投影和分割光学观测上验证，NeuSOGA能将不同传感模态和视角下的观测转化为紧凑符号表示，同时保留关键几何与拓扑结构。生成的表示可解释、可编辑且数学显式，优于神经潜在编码。

Conclusion: NeuSOGA提供了从观测到符号的可解释、可说明通路，为神经符号AI在几何智能领域建立了基础性的抽象机制。

Abstract: A fundamental challenge in artificial intelligence is the transformation of observations into explicit symbolic representations suitable for abstraction, interpretation, and reasoning. While modern AI systems achieve remarkable perceptual capabilities through large-scale statistical learning, the resulting knowledge is typically encoded within latent parameters that are difficult to inspect or manipulate analytically. Inspired by Neuro-Symbolic AI and theories of human abstraction, this paper investigates the formation of symbolic mathematical representations from geometric observations.
  We propose NeuSOGA (Neuro-Symbolic Geometric Abstraction), a framework that progressively transforms observations into topological abstractions, geometric abstractions, and ultimately symbolic mathematical representations. The architecture combines topology-guided structural discovery using Euclidean Distance Transforms, foundation-model perception using Segment Anything, adaptive multi-scale geometric abstraction, and symbolic synthesis through Implicit Area Splines.
  The resulting representation is an analytical implicit model supporting arbitrary-order smoothness, additive composition, and closed-form evaluation. Unlike neural latent encodings, the generated representation remains interpretable, editable, and mathematically explicit. Experiments on ModelNet40 point clouds, arbitrary-view projections, and segmented optical observations demonstrate that NeuSOGA transforms diverse observations into compact symbolic representations while preserving essential geometric and topological structure across sensing modalities and viewing directions.
  NeuSOGA provides an interpretable and explainable pathway from observation to symbol and establishes

</details>


### [362] [EdiTikZ: Scientific Figure Editing from Revision Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01409)
*Christian Greisinger,Zhixue Zhao,Steffen Eger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DaEdiTikZ——首个基于自然修订轨迹的大规模科学图表编辑数据集（39.1万个TikZ编辑对和78.1万条编辑指令），并训练出紧凑的Qwen3.5-based EdiTikZ模型（4B/9B），通过联合学习和强化学习在科学图表编辑任务上超越所有基线，人类评估优于GPT-5.6-Sol并与Gemini-3.1-Pro持平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学图表编辑是重要但尚未充分探索的任务。现有方法依赖昂贵的专有智能体系统、主要关注评估或使用合成编辑构造训练数据。作者指出自然发生的科研修订与发展轨迹可作为可扩展的监督信号，从而避免上述局限。

Method: 1）从arXiv、GitHub和TeX SE中挖掘391K个合理的TikZ编辑对，构建DaEdiTikZ数据集；2）用VLM基于渲染图像和TikZ代码推断781K条有向编辑指令；3）构建含790个实例的人工精炼基准DaEdiTikZ-Bench；4）训练两个Qwen3.5-based压缩模型（4B和9B），联合学习重建和编辑任务，再通过强化学习用互补奖励（渲染保真度和编辑应用）优化。

Result: 自动评估中9B模型优于所有测试基线；人类评估（9名标注者、4,320个评分）显示其优于GPT-5.6-Sol并与Gemini-3.1-Pro持平；在严重的分布外偏移下，在2K训练序列长度区间内仍与GPT-5.6-Sol保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 利用自然发生的科研修订轨迹作为大规模监督来源是构建科学图表编辑模型的有效且可扩展途径。紧凑的开源模型可以达到甚至超越专有大型模型的编辑质量，验证了数据驱动+强化学习范式的潜力。模型和数据集将开源发布。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have shown strong performance in generating scientific figures from text or images. However, producing publication-ready figures requires iterative refinement, making scientific figure editing an important yet largely unexplored task. Existing approaches rely on costly proprietary agentic systems, focus primarily on evaluation, or construct training supervision from synthetically generated edits. Instead, we leverage naturally occurring scientific revision and development trajectories as a scalable source of supervision. To this end, we introduce DaEdiTikZ, the first large-scale dataset of revision-derived scientific figure edits, constructed by mining 391K plausible TikZ edit pairs from arXiv, GitHub, and TeX SE and inferring 781K directed edit instructions with a VLM conditioned on rendered figures and TikZ code. We further introduce DaEdiTikZ-Bench, a human-refined benchmark with 790 instances, and train two compact Qwen3.5-based EdiTikZ models (4B and 9B) by jointly learning reconstruction and editing, followed by reinforcement learning (RL) with complementary rewards for rendered fidelity and edit application. Automatic evaluation places our 9B model above all tested baselines, while human evaluation with 9 annotators and 4,320 ratings places it above GPT-5.6-Sol and on par with Gemini-3.1-Pro. Under severe out-of-distribution shifts, it remains competitive with GPT-5.6-Sol near its 2K training sequence-length regime. Models and datasets will be released.

</details>


### [363] [Parsing the Stream: A Live Trace Model for Long-Horizon Agents and Their Observers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01466)
*Egor Pakhomov,Erik Nijkamp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"实时轨迹模型"(live trace model)：将追加式事件账本增量折叠为类型化运行状态，再编译成面向不同消费者的视图，以解决长程智能体轨迹超出人类监控者与智能体自身上下文的问题。针对观察者侧，用LLM作为读者代理验证，编译视图相比读取原始轨迹可减少约14-15倍输入token、5-7倍成本，同时准确率更高(0.85-0.87 vs 0.48)。针对智能体侧，在120链路顺序依赖任务中，维护运行统计量的折叠机制在完整上下文提示失败的情况下成功(30/30 vs 8/30)。论文从失败中提炼出11条轨迹折叠的候选需求，并用一个顺序敏感任务族界定折叠失效的边界。代码、基准、可再生的合成语料库与工作台轨迹全部开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程智能体的运行轨迹会同时超出两类消费者的能力边界：一是人工监控者（无法在大规模原始日志中定位关键信息），二是智能体自身（有界上下文限制，轨迹必须被重新折叠回其中）。现有方案缺乏一种统一、可审计且高效的方式同时服务于这两类消费者。

Method: 提出实时轨迹模型(live trace model)：1) 一个只追加的事件账本(event ledger)；2) 增量折叠为类型化运行状态(typed run state)；3) 编译为按消费者定制的视图(per-consumer views)。对于观察者侧使用LLM reader作为代理进行评测；对于智能体侧则定义了120链路顺序依赖任务，并对比完整上下文提示、提示级草稿本(prompt-level scratchpad)与折叠机制，且通过两臂分解(two-arm decomposition)将折叠的准确率归因于确定性聚合、成本优势归因于紧凑性。还设计了顺序敏感任务族以界定折叠的局限边界。

Result: 观察者侧：编译视图相比预算封顶的单次原始轨迹读取，输入token减少约14x和15x，成本降低5-7倍，准确率由0.48提升至0.85-0.87。智能体侧：在30次独立试验(清洁协议)下，折叠机制达到30/30而完整上下文提示仅8/30；提示级草稿本以更低成本匹配折叠的准确率。分解结果显示：折叠的准确率来自确定性聚合，成本优势来自紧凑性；其剩余独特价值为确定性可审计性以及从同一状态服务于观察者。此外从失败案例中提炼出11条轨迹折叠候选需求，并以顺序敏感任务族展示折叠在此类任务上失效。

Conclusion: 折叠式轨迹(live trace model)能同时显著改善两个消费者的体验——观察者获得结构化、高准确率且低成本的视图，智能体获得超越全上下文提示的长链依赖维持能力。尽管在成本与准确率上部分可被更便宜的替代方案（如scratchpad）追平，折叠的独特价值在于确定性可审计性与单一状态源同时服务于两类消费者。但该方法存在明确边界：顺序敏感类任务中折叠不再带来帮助。由此归纳的11条候选需求为未来轨迹折叠系统的设计提供了指导。

Abstract: A long-horizon agent's trace outgrows both of its consumers: the human observer monitoring the run, and the agent itself, whose bounded context the trace must be folded back into. We present a live trace model, an append-only event ledger folded incrementally into typed run state and compiled into per-consumer views, and evaluate it for both consumers against deterministic ground truth. For the observer side, evaluated with an LLM reader as proxy, the compiled view answers monitoring questions using approximately 14x and 15x fewer input tokens (by reader) and at 5-7x lower cost than a budget-capped single-call reading of the raw trace, with higher accuracy (0.85-0.87 versus 0.48). Because the questions were co-designed with the view schema, we treat the token and cost reduction, conditional on schema coverage, as the transferable result. For the agent, on 120-link sequential-dependency tasks, mechanisms that maintain the task's running statistic in per-step state succeed where full-context prompting fails (30/30 versus 8/30 under a clean protocol, n=30, labeled descriptive owing to benchmark-system co-development); a prompt-level scratchpad matches the fold's accuracy at lower cost, and a two-arm decomposition attributes the fold's accuracy to its deterministic aggregate and its cost advantage to its compactness. The fold's remaining value over cheaper alternatives is deterministic auditability and serving the observer from the same state. We derive eleven candidate requirements for trace folding from observed failures and delimit them with an order-sensitive task family on which the fold ceases to help. Code, benchmarks, a regenerable synthetic corpus, and all workbench traces are released.

</details>


### [364] [Harness-of-Harness: Multi-Day Autonomous Software Development with Continual Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01481)
*Haoyang Yan,Min-le Su,Hangfan Zhang,Zhanhao Li,Chen Zhang,Shao Zhang,Yang Chen,Lei Bai,Shuyue Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Harness-of-Harness (HoH)框架，使基于LLM的编码智能体在自主软件开发过程中持续改进。HoH在现有编码智能体装备上运行，将执行组织为迭代的规划-编码-测试循环，通过平衡修复与能力增长、划分可验证的小规模开发步骤、分离实现期测试与独立评估、约束可验证输出而非规定工作流等方式，持续提升交付质量。在GameCraft-Bench、FrontierSWE和ProgramBench三个基准上，对三种装备-模型组合（Codex+GPT-5.5、OpenCode+DeepSeek-V4-Pro、Pi+MiniMax-M3），HoH较独立装备平均相对提升52.25%，最大提升82.86%。在70余次迭代的多日部署中，HoH自主开发出具有连贯剧情、完整核心机制、可玩体验、精美画面和集成音频的第一人称射击游戏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM编码智能体已被用于自主软件开发，但现有方法在长时间自主开发过程中难以持续改进软件质量。需要一种框架使编码智能体能够在不依赖人类干预的情况下，在迭代过程中不断优化和完善所开发的软件系统。

Method: HoH作为元层面的框架，作用于现有编码智能体装备之上，组织其执行形成"规划-编码-测试"的迭代循环。核心策略包括：平衡修复与能力增长、将开发划分成小而可验证的增量、分离实现期测试与独立评估、只约束可验证输出而非规定智能体工作流；同时渐进式地暴露交付物、角色特定工具和技能，鼓励复用而非从零重建，并维护版本化项目历史。

Result: 在三个基准（GameCraft-Bench、FrontierSWE、ProgramBench）上，对三组装备-模型组合进行测试，HoH均优于对应独立装备，三次迭代后平均相对增益52.25%，最大增益82.86%。在多日超过70次迭代的实际部署中，HoH成功自主开发出一个包含连贯剧情、完整核心机制、可玩体验、精美视觉与集成音频的第一人称射击游戏。

Conclusion: HoH证明了在不规定具体智能体行为的前提下，通过对现有编码装备进行系统性组织与约束，即可显著提升LLM编码智能体的自主软件开发能力，实现接近全自主的完整软件系统交付。

Abstract: This paper studies autonomous software development, in which LLM-based coding agents transform high-level requirements into complete, functional, and usable software systems without human intervention. We introduce Harness-of-Harness (HoH), a framework that enables coding agents to continually improve software during autonomous development. HoH operates on existing coding-agent harnesses, and organizes their executions into iterative planning-coding-testing loops. To sustain improvement across loops, HoH balances repair with capability growth, scopes development into small and verifiable increments, separates implementation-time testing from independent evaluation, and constrains verifiable outputs rather than prescribing agent workflows. It progressively exposes deliverables, role-specific tools, and skills, encourages reuse rather than recreation, and maintains versioned project histories. On GameCraft-Bench, FrontierSWE, and ProgramBench, three harness-model pairs (Codex with GPT-5.5, OpenCode with DeepSeek-V4-Pro, and Pi with MiniMax-M3), HoH consistently outperforms the corresponding standalone harnesses, achieving an average relative gain of 52.25 percent and a maximum gain of 82.86 percent after three iterations. In a multi-day deployment with more than 70 iterations, HoH autonomously develops a first-person-shooter game, featuring a coherent storyline, fully implemented core mechanics, human-playable experience, polished visuals and integrated audio. Github: https://github.com/Flesymeb/HarnessOfHarness Project Page: https://flesymeb.github.io/HarnessOfHarness/

</details>


### [365] [When Guardrails Look Effective: Construct Validity Failures in LLM Agent Commerce Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01519)
*Peiying Zhu,Sidi Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对基于语言模型代理的多轮买卖交互仿真提出了构建效度审计框架。在一个可配置的酒店交易testbed中，最初报告市场护栏带来+87.4、+35.0、+28.8的福利增益，但控制协议结构（schema与buyer chooser）后，对比变为+7.2、-13.9、+23.8。进一步审计显示生成噪声占变异的49.9%、卖家激励检查呈非单调性，且利润最大化卖家本已实现first-best福利。作者提出包含激励效度、协议隔离、随机稳定性、福利核算四要素的构建效度契约，判定原始估计在协议隔离下为INVALID，受控研究在激励效度与随机稳定性下仍为INCONCLUSIVE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式仿真日益用语言模型代理来评估市场政策，但其输出（价格、利润、消费者剩余、福利）看似经济化，可能并未体现声明所声称的行为。作者旨在审计这种潜在的风险——即仿真的表面经济数字与真实机制行为之间可能存在鸿沟，为政策结论的可信度建立系统验证标准。

Method: 采用多轮买卖testbed（可配置的酒店交易）。实验设计包括：（1）对比受护栏与不受护栏代理在固定schema和buyer chooser前后的一对一福利差异；（2）跨Qwen2.5 1.5B-14B模型尺度梯度评估；（3）多生成代次以测量随机稳定性，用bootstrap置信区间与残差方差分解评估生成噪声；（4）卖家激励检查（非单调利润压力测试）；（5）脚本化正向对照组（profit-maximizing与强制低效捆绑的卖家）；最终提出一个四要素构建效度契约（激励效度、协议隔离、随机稳定性、福利核算），并给出INVALID/INCONCLUSIVE判定。

Result: 固定schema和buyer chooser后，配对对比从+87.4/+35.0/+28.8变为+7.2/-13.9/+23.8。最大14B单代效应平均为+229，但三代平均降至+37.6（95% bootstrap区间[-34.2, 109.3]），代际残差占该事后探针49.9%的变异。激励检查非单调：加大利润压力反而产生低于默认提示词的利润。利润最大化卖家已实现first-best福利，护栏主要起再分配与降低福利的作用；仅当卖家被显式编程强制低效捆绑时护栏才创造福利。依据契约，原始估计在协议隔离上判为INVALID，受控研究在激励效度与随机稳定性上仍为INCONCLUSIVE。
